    22→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    23→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    24→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    25→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))

    29→def build_finding_items(
    30→    state: dict,

    38→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    39→    out: list[dict] = []

    43→            continue

    45→            continue

    52→        item["id"] = finding_id
    53→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    54→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    55→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    56→        item["review_weight"] = (

    60→        if item["is_subjective"]:

    81→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    82→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    83→
    84→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
    86→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
    87→
    88→    # Synthesis stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked

    90→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
    91→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
    92→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
    93→
    94→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
    95→    if kind == "workflow_action":

    97→

   100→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(

   106→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   107→            item.get("id", ""),
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   111→        return (
   112→            1,
   113→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
   114→            subjective_score_value(item),
   115→            item.get("id", ""),
   116→        )

   197→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   198→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   199→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   200→    for item in items:
   201→        if group == "file":
   202→            key = item.get("file", "")
   203→        elif group == "detector":
   204→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   205→        elif group == "cluster":
   206→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   207→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   208→        else:
   209→            key = "items"
   210→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   211→    return grouped
   212→
   213→

   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from collections import deque
     7→from typing import TypedDict
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    10→    ALL_STATUSES,
    11→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    12→    build_create_plan_item,
    13→    build_subjective_items,
    14→    build_synthesis_stage_items,
    15→    scope_matches,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    18→    build_finding_items,
    19→    group_queue_items,
    20→    item_explain,
    21→    item_sort_key,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    24→    apply_plan_order as _apply_plan_order,
    25→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    26→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    29→
    30→
    31→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    32→class QueueBuildOptions:
    33→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    34→
    35→    count: int | None = 1
    36→    scan_path: str | None = None
    37→    scope: str | None = None
    38→    status: str = "open"
    39→    include_subjective: bool = True
    40→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    41→    chronic: bool = False
    42→    explain: bool = False
    43→    plan: dict | None = None
    44→    include_skipped: bool = False
    45→    cluster: str | None = None
    46→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    47→
    48→
    49→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    50→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    51→
    52→    items: list[dict]
    53→    total: int
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→    suppressed_count: int
    56→
    57→
    58→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    60→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    61→
    62→
    63→_WORKFLOW_KINDS = frozenset({"workflow_stage", "workflow_action"})
    64→
    65→
    66→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    67→    items: list[dict],
    68→    *,
    69→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    70→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    71→) -> list[dict]:
    72→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.
    73→
    74→    Workflow items (synthesis stages, create-plan) are excluded from the
    75→    interleave and prepended — they maintain their natural high-priority sort.
    76→    """
    77→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    78→        return items
    79→
    80→    workflow_items: list[dict] = []
    81→    subjective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    82→    objective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        if item.get("kind") in _WORKFLOW_KINDS:
    85→            workflow_items.append(item)
    86→        elif _is_subjective_queue_item(item):
    87→            subjective_items.append(item)
    88→        else:
    89→            objective_items.append(item)
    90→
    91→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    92→        return items
    93→
    94→    interleaved: list[dict] = list(workflow_items)
    95→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    96→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    97→            if not objective_items:
    98→                break
    99→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
   100→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
   101→            if not subjective_items:
   102→                break
   103→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
   104→
   105→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
   106→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
   107→    return interleaved
   108→
   109→
   110→def build_work_queue(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
   113→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   114→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   115→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   116→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   117→
   118→    status = resolved_options.status
   119→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   120→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   121→    try:
   122→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   123→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   124→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   125→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   126→
   127→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   128→        state,
   129→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   130→        status_filter=status,
   131→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   132→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   133→    )
   134→
   135→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   136→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   137→    suppressed_count = 0
   138→    if (
   139→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   140→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   141→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   142→    ):
   143→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   144→            state,
   145→            state.get("findings", {}),
   146→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   147→        )
   148→        for item in subjective_items:
   149→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   150→                continue
   151→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   152→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   153→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   154→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   155→                suppressed_count += 1
   156→                continue
   157→            all_items.append(item)
   158→
   159→    # Inject synthesis stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   160→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   161→        synth_items = build_synthesis_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   162→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   163→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   164→        if plan_item is not None:
   165→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   166→
   167→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   168→
   169→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   170→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   171→    if resolved_options.plan:
   172→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   173→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   174→            all_items,
   175→            resolved_options.plan,
   176→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   177→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   178→            new_ids=new_ids,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   182→    should_collapse = (
   183→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   184→        and resolved_options.plan
   185→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   186→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   187→    )
   188→    if should_collapse:
   189→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   190→
   191→    should_interleave = (
   192→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   193→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   194→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   195→        and not resolved_options.scope
   196→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   197→    )
   198→    if should_interleave:
   199→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   200→
   201→    total = len(all_items)
   202→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   203→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   204→
   205→    if resolved_options.explain:
   206→        for item in all_items:
   207→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   208→
   209→    return {
   210→        "items": all_items,
   211→        "total": total,
   212→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   213→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   214→        "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
   215→    }
   216→
   217→
   218→__all__ = [
   219→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   220→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   221→    "WorkQueueResult",
   222→    "build_work_queue",
   223→    "group_queue_items",
   224→]
   225→

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    21→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    22→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    23→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    51→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    52→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    53→    weight_by_confidence = {
    54→        "high": 1.0,
    55→        "medium": 0.7,
    56→        "low": 0.3,
    57→    }
    58→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    59→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    60→        weight *= 10.0
    61→    return float(weight)
    62→
    63→
    64→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    65→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    66→    if not scope:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    70→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    71→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    72→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    73→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    74→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    75→
    76→    if "*" in scope:
    77→        return any(
    78→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    79→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    80→        )
    81→
    82→    if "::" in scope:
    83→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    84→
    85→    lowered = scope.lower()
    86→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        return (
    88→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    89→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    90→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    91→        )
    92→
    93→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    94→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    95→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    96→
    97→    return (
    98→        detector == scope
    99→        or filepath == scope
   100→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   105→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   106→
   107→
   108→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   109→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   110→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   111→    target = cleaned.lower()
   112→
   113→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   114→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   115→            return str(dim_key)
   116→    return slugify(cleaned)
   117→
   118→
   119→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   120→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   121→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   122→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   123→    return {
   124→        cleaned.lower(),
   125→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   126→        slugify(cleaned),
   127→        canonical.lower(),
   128→        slugify(canonical),
   129→    }
   130→
   131→
   132→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   133→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   134→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   135→    if not lang:
   136→        return None
   137→
   138→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   139→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   140→        return None
   141→
   142→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   143→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   144→        return None
   145→
   146→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   147→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   148→        return None
   149→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   150→
   151→
   152→def primary_command_for_finding(
   153→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   154→) -> str:
   155→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   156→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   157→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   158→        available_fixers = [
   159→            fixer
   160→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   161→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   162→        ]
   163→        if available_fixers:
   164→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   165→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   166→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   167→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   168→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   169→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   170→
   171→
   172→def build_synthesis_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``synthesis::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   174→
   175→    Returns an empty list when no synthesis stages are pending.
   176→    """
   177→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis_playbook import (
   178→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   179→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS,
   180→    )
   181→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   182→        SYNTHESIS_IDS,
   183→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS,
   184→    )
   185→
   186→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   187→    order_set = set(order)
   188→    present = order_set & SYNTHESIS_IDS
   189→    if not present:
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   193→    confirmed = set(meta.get("synthesis_stages", {}).keys())
   194→
   195→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   196→    open_review_count = sum(
   197→        1 for f in findings.values()
   198→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   199→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    label_map = dict(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS)
   203→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   204→
   205→    items: list[dict] = []
   206→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   207→        if sid not in present:
   208→            continue
   209→        if name in confirmed:
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   213→        deps = SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   214→        blocked_by = sorted(
   215→            f"synthesis::{dep}" for dep in deps
   216→            if f"synthesis::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   217→        )
   218→
   219→        cmd = f"desloppify plan synthesize --stage {name}"
   220→        if name == "commit":
   221→            cmd = 'desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."'
   222→
   223→        items.append({
   224→            "id": sid,
   225→            "tier": 1,
   226→            "confidence": "high",
   227→            "detector": "synthesis",
   228→            "file": ".",
   229→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   230→            "stage_name": name,
   231→            "stage_index": idx,
   232→            "summary": f"Planning: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   233→            "detail": {
   234→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   235→                "stage": name,
   236→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   237→            },
   238→            "primary_command": cmd,
   239→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   240→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   241→        })
   242→    return items
   243→
   244→
   245→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> dict | None:
   246→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if it's in the queue.
   247→
   248→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   249→    """
   250→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID
   251→
   252→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   253→        return None
   254→
   255→    return {
   256→        "id": WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   257→        "tier": 1,
   258→        "confidence": "high",
   259→        "detector": "workflow",
   260→        "file": ".",
   261→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   262→        "summary": "Create prioritized plan from review results",
   263→        "detail": {},
   264→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan",
   265→        "blocked_by": [],
   266→        "is_blocked": False,
   267→    }
   268→
   269→
   270→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   271→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   272→    if not dim_scores:
   273→        return {}
   274→
   275→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   276→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   277→    for entry in entries:
   278→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   279→        if not name:
   280→            continue
   281→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   282→        [REDACTED](name)
   283→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   284→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   285→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   286→            if not key:
   287→                continue
   288→            aliases.add(key)
   289→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   290→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   291→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   292→        for alias in aliases:
   293→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   294→    return scores
   295→
   296→
   297→def _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   298→    """Build scorecard-like entries for unassessed placeholder dimensions.
   299→
   300→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` goes through the scorecard pipeline which
   301→    intentionally hides placeholders from display.  This function reads
   302→    ``dimension_scores`` directly so initial-review items are never lost.
   303→    """
   304→    entries: list[dict] = []
   305→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   306→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   307→            continue
   308→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   309→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   310→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   311→            continue
   312→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   313→            continue
   314→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   315→        entries.append(
   316→            {
   317→                "name": name,
   318→                "score": float(data.get("score", 0.0)),
   319→                "strict": float(data.get("strict", 0.0)),
   320→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   321→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   322→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   323→                "placeholder": True,
   324→                "stale": False,
   325→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   326→                "cli_keys": [dim_key] if dim_key else [],
   327→            }
   328→        )
   329→    return entries
   330→
   331→
   332→def build_subjective_items(
   333→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   334→) -> list[dict]:
   335→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   336→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   337→    if not dim_scores:
   338→        return []
   339→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   340→
   341→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   342→
   343→    # The scorecard pipeline hides unassessed placeholders from display.
   344→    # Merge them in so initial-review items always appear in the queue.
   345→    seen_dims: set[str] = {
   346→        str(e.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   347→        for e in subjective_entries
   348→        if e.get("dimension_key")
   349→    }
   350→    for entry in _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores):
   351→        dk = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   352→        if dk and dk not in seen_dims:
   353→            subjective_entries.append(entry)
   354→            seen_dims.add(dk)
   355→
   356→    if not subjective_entries:
   357→        return []
   358→    unassessed_dims = {
   359→        str(name).strip()
   360→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   361→            dim_scores
   362→        )
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   366→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   367→    for finding in findings.values():
   368→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   369→            continue
   370→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   371→        if not dim_key:
   372→            continue
   373→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   374→
   375→    items: list[dict] = []
   376→    def _prepare_command(
   377→        cli_keys: list[str],
   378→        *,
   379→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   380→    ) -> str:
   381→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   382→        if cli_keys:
   383→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   384→        if force_review_rerun:
   385→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   386→        return command
   387→
   388→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   389→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   390→        if not name:
   391→            continue
   392→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   394→            continue
   395→
   396→        [REDACTED](name)
   397→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   398→        cli_keys = [
   399→            str(key).strip().lower()
   400→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   401→            if str(key).strip()
   402→        ]
   403→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   404→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   405→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   406→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   407→            name in unassessed_dims
   408→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   409→        )
   410→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   411→        # Items whose only action is --force-review-rerun are gated by the
   412→        # review preflight while objective backlog exists.  Flag them so the
   413→        # queue builder can suppress them until the backlog drains.
   414→        is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   415→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   416→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   417→        if open_review > 0:
   418→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   419→        elif is_unassessed:
   420→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   421→        elif is_stale:
   422→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   423→                cli_keys,
   424→                force_review_rerun=True,
   425→            )
   426→        elif cli_keys:
   427→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   428→                cli_keys,
   429→                force_review_rerun=True,
   430→            )
   431→        else:
   432→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   433→                cli_keys,
   434→                force_review_rerun=True,
   435→            )
   436→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   437→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   438→        items.append(
   439→            {
   440→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   441→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   442→                "file": ".",
   443→                "confidence": "medium",
   444→                "summary": summary,
   445→                "detail": {
   446→                    "dimension_name": name,
   447→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   448→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   449→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   450→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   451→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   452→                },
   453→                "status": "open",
   454→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   455→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   456→                "backlog_gated": is_backlog_gated,
   457→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   458→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   459→            }
   460→        )
   461→    return items
   462→
   463→
   464→__all__ = [
   465→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   466→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   467→    "build_create_plan_item",
   468→    "build_subjective_items",
   469→    "build_synthesis_stage_items",
   470→    "detail_dict",
   471→    "is_review_finding",
   472→    "is_subjective_finding",

     9→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    10→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    11→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    12→    if not scan_history:
    13→        return set()
    14→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    15→    if not threshold:
    16→        return set()
    17→    return {
    18→        finding_id
    19→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    20→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    21→    }

    24→def apply_plan_order(
    25→    items: list[dict],
    26→    plan: dict,
    27→    *,
    28→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    29→    cluster: str | None = None,
    30→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    31→) -> list[dict]:
    32→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    33→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    34→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    35→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    36→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    37→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    38→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    39→
    40→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    41→    for item in items:
    42→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    43→
    44→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    45→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    46→        if override.get("description"):
    47→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    48→        if override.get("note"):
    49→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    50→        if override.get("cluster"):
    51→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    52→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    53→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    54→                "name": cluster_name,
    55→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    56→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    57→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    58→            }
    59→
    60→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    61→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    62→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    63→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    64→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    65→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    66→
    67→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    68→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    69→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    70→    _new = new_ids or set()
    71→    for item in items:
    72→        item_id = item["id"]
    73→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    74→            continue
    75→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    76→            skipped_items.append(item)
    77→        elif item_id in _new:
    78→            remaining_new.append(item)
    79→        else:
    80→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    81→
    82→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    83→    if include_skipped:
    84→        result = result + skipped_items
    85→
    86→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
    87→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
    88→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    89→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
    90→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
    91→            if skip_entry:
    92→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
    93→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
    94→                if skip_reason:
    95→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
    96→
    97→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
    98→    if effective_cluster:
    99→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   100→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   101→        if cluster_member_ids:
   102→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   103→    return result

   106→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   107→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   108→    try:
   109→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   110→
   111→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   112→        if meta:
   113→            return meta.action_type
   114→    except ImportError as exc:
   115→        _ = exc
   116→    return "manual_fix"

   119→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   120→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   121→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   122→    if not clusters:
   123→        return items
   124→
   125→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   126→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   127→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   128→            continue
   129→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   130→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   131→
   132→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   133→        return items
   134→

   136→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   137→    for item in items:

   139→        if cluster_name:

   141→        else:
   142→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   143→

   145→    for cluster_name, members in cluster_members.items():

   150→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   151→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   152→        if "desloppify fix" in action:

   154→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   155→            action_type = "reorganize"
   156→        else:

   158→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:

   184→            "detector": detector,
   185→            "file": "",
   186→        }
   187→        result.append(meta_item)
   188→
   189→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   190→    return result
   191→
   192→
   193→__all__ = ["apply_plan_order", "collapse_clusters", "new_item_ids"]
   194→
   195→

1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     9→    QueueBuildOptions,
    10→    WorkQueueResult,
    11→    build_work_queue,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _subjective_threshold(state: dict[str, Any], *, default: float = 95.0) -> float:
    16→    config = state.get("config", {})
    17→    raw_target = default
    18→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    19→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    20→    try:
    21→        value = float(raw_target)
    22→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    23→        value = default
    24→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    28→class OpenPlanQueuePolicy:
    29→    count: int | None = None
    30→    scan_path: str | None = None
    31→    include_subjective: bool = True
    32→
    33→
    34→def build_open_plan_queue(
    35→    state: dict[str, Any],
    36→    policy: OpenPlanQueuePolicy | None = None,
    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    38→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    39→    policy = policy or OpenPlanQueuePolicy()
    40→    resolved_scan_path: str | None
    41→    if isinstance(policy.scan_path, str):
    42→        resolved_scan_path = policy.scan_path
    43→    else:
    44→        raw_state_path = state.get("scan_path")
    45→        resolved_scan_path = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, str) else None
    46→    return build_work_queue(
    47→        state,
    48→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    49→            count=policy.count,
    50→            scan_path=resolved_scan_path,
    51→            status="open",
    52→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
    53→            subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state),
    54→        ),
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→__all__ = ["OpenPlanQueuePolicy", "build_open_plan_queue"]
    59→

1→"""Finding prioritization/selection helpers for next items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanItem, PlanState
     6→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     7→    QueueBuildOptions,
     8→    build_work_queue,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→def get_next_items(
    13→    state: PlanState,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int = 1,
    16→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    17→) -> list[PlanItem]:
    18→    """Get the N highest-priority open findings.
    19→
    20→    Legacy plan API intentionally returns only finding items (not synthetic
    21→    subjective queue items) so existing planner consumers stay stable.
    22→    The *tier* parameter is accepted for backward compatibility but ignored.
    23→    """
    24→    result = build_work_queue(
    25→        state,
    26→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    27→            count=count,
    28→            scan_path=scan_path,
    29→            status="open",
    30→            include_subjective=False,
    31→        ),
    32→    )
    33→    return [item for item in result["items"] if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    34→
    35→
    36→def get_next_item(
    37→    state: PlanState,
    38→    tier: int | None = None,
    39→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    40→) -> PlanItem | None:
    41→    """Get the highest-priority open finding."""
    42→    items = get_next_items(state, tier=tier, count=1, scan_path=scan_path)
    43→    return items[0] if items else None
    44→

1→"""Shared subjective follow-up rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Protocol
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→class SubjectiveFollowup(Protocol):
    11→    """Contract for objects passed to print_subjective_followup."""
    12→
    13→    low_assessed: object  # falsy when no dimensions are below threshold
    14→    threshold_label: str
    15→    rendered: str
    16→    command: str
    17→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    18→
    19→
    20→def print_subjective_followup(
    21→    followup: SubjectiveFollowup,
    22→    *,
    23→    leading_newline: bool = False,
    24→    objective_backlog: int = 0,
    25→) -> bool:
    26→    """Render common subjective quality/integrity guidance lines.
    27→
    28→    When *objective_backlog* > 0, quality nudges are suppressed entirely — the
    29→    queue already surfaces subjective work when it becomes actionable.
    30→    Integrity warnings are always shown (anti-gaming safeguard).
    31→
    32→    Returns True when any line is rendered.
    33→    """
    34→    printed = False
    35→    prefix = "\n" if leading_newline else ""
    36→    if followup.low_assessed and objective_backlog <= 0:
    37→        print(
    38→            colorize(
    39→                f"{prefix}  Subjective quality (<{followup.threshold_label}%): "
    40→                f"{followup.rendered}",
    41→                "cyan",
    42→            )
    43→        )
    44→        print(
    45→            colorize(
    46→                f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
    47→                "dim",
    48→            )
    49→        )
    50→        printed = True
    51→
    52→    if followup.integrity_lines:
    53→        if printed:
    54→            print()
    55→        for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
    56→            print(colorize(f"  {message}", style))
    57→        printed = True
    58→    return printed
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["print_subjective_followup"]
    62→

1→"""Dimension and overall scoring aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import detector_stats_by_mode
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     9→    DIMENSIONS,
    10→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    11→    SCORING_MODES,
    12→    ScoreMode,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import (
    15→    compute_health_breakdown,
    16→    compute_health_score,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import (
    19→    compute_score_impact,
    20→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import (
    23→    append_subjective_dimensions,
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class ScoreBundle:
    29→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    30→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    overall_score: float
    33→    objective_score: float
    34→    strict_score: float
    35→    verified_strict_score: float
    36→
    37→
    38→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    39→    findings: dict,
    40→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    41→    *,
    42→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    43→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    44→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    45→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    46→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    47→
    48→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    49→        totals = {
    50→            mode: {
    51→                "checks": 0,
    52→                "issues": 0,
    53→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    54→                "detectors": {},
    55→            }
    56→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    57→        }
    58→
    59→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    60→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    61→            if potential <= 0:
    62→                continue
    63→
    64→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    65→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    66→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    67→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    68→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    69→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    70→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    71→                    "potential": potential,
    72→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    73→                    "issues": issues,
    74→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    75→                }
    76→
    77→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    78→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    79→            if total_checks <= 0:
    80→                continue
    81→            dim_score = (
    82→                max(
    83→                    0.0,
    84→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    85→                )
    86→                * 100
    87→            )
    88→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    89→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    90→                "tier": dim.tier,
    91→                "checks": total_checks,
    92→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    93→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    94→            }
    95→
    96→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    97→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    98→            results[mode],
    99→            findings,
   100→            subjective_assessments,
   101→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   102→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   103→        )
   104→    return results
   105→
   106→
   107→def compute_dimension_scores(
   108→    findings: dict,
   109→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   110→    *,
   111→    strict: bool = False,
   112→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   113→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   114→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   115→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   116→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   117→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   118→        findings,
   119→        potentials,
   120→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   121→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   122→    )[mode]
   123→
   124→
   125→def compute_score_bundle(
   126→    findings: dict,
   127→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   128→    *,
   129→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   130→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   131→) -> ScoreBundle:
   132→    """Compute all score channels from one scoring engine pass."""
   133→    by_mode = compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   134→        findings,
   135→        potentials,
   136→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   137→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    lenient_scores = by_mode["lenient"]
   141→    strict_scores = by_mode["strict"]
   142→    verified_strict_scores = by_mode["verified_strict"]
   143→
   144→    mechanical_lenient_scores = {
   145→        name: data
   146→        for name, data in lenient_scores.items()
   147→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   148→    }
   149→
   150→    return ScoreBundle(
   151→        dimension_scores=lenient_scores,
   152→        strict_dimension_scores=strict_scores,
   153→        verified_strict_dimension_scores=verified_strict_scores,
   154→        overall_score=compute_health_score(lenient_scores),
   155→        objective_score=compute_health_score(mechanical_lenient_scores),
   156→        strict_score=compute_health_score(strict_scores),
   157→        verified_strict_score=compute_health_score(verified_strict_scores),
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→__all__ = [
   162→    "ScoreBundle",
   163→    "compute_dimension_scores_by_mode",
   164→    "compute_dimension_scores",
   165→    "compute_health_breakdown",
   166→    "compute_health_score",
   167→    "compute_score_bundle",
   168→    "compute_score_impact",
   169→    "get_dimension_for_detector",
   170→]
   171→

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     8→    PlanModel,
     9→    SkipEntry,
    10→    empty_plan,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    14→
    15→
    16→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    17→
    18→
    19→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    20→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    21→    try:
    22→        from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    23→
    24→        config = load_config()
    25→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    26→        return max(0, int(value))
    27→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    29→
    30→
    31→def append_log_entry(
    32→    plan: PlanModel,
    33→    action: str,
    34→    *,
    35→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    36→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    37→    actor: str = "user",
    38→    note: str | None = None,
    39→    detail: dict | None = None,
    40→) -> None:
    41→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    42→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    43→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    44→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    45→        "action": action,
    46→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    47→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    48→        "actor": actor,
    49→        "note": note,
    50→        "detail": detail or {},
    51→    }
    52→    log.append(entry)
    53→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    54→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    55→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    56→
    57→
    58→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59→# Helpers
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→
    62→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    63→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    64→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    65→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    66→    if finding_id in order:
    67→        order.remove(finding_id)
    68→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    69→
    70→
    71→def _resolve_position(
    72→    order: list[str],
    73→    position: str,
    74→    target: str | None = None,
    75→    offset: int | None = None,
    76→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    77→) -> int:
    78→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    79→
    80→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    81→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    82→    """
    83→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    84→
    85→    if position == "top":
    86→        return 0
    87→    if position == "bottom":
    88→        return len(order)
    89→
    90→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    91→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    92→
    93→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    94→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    95→            order,
    96→            moving,
    97→            position=position,
    98→            offset=offset,
    99→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
   100→        )
   101→
   102→    return len(order)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _resolve_relative_position(
   106→    order: list[str],
   107→    moving: set[str],
   108→    *,
   109→    position: str,
   110→    target: str,
   111→) -> int:
   112→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
   113→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
   114→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
   115→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
   119→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
   120→        if item_id == target:
   121→            return i
   122→    return None
   123→
   124→
   125→def _resolve_offset_position(
   126→    order: list[str],
   127→    moving: set[str],
   128→    *,
   129→    position: str,
   130→    offset: int,
   131→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
   132→) -> int:
   133→    if not finding_ids:
   134→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
   135→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
   136→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
   137→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
   138→    if current_idx is None:
   139→        if position == "up":
   140→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
   141→        return len(clean_order)
   142→    if position == "up":
   143→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   144→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   145→
   146→
   147→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   148→# Move items
   149→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→
   151→def move_items(
   152→    plan: PlanModel,
   153→    finding_ids: list[str],
   154→    position: str,
   155→    target: str | None = None,
   156→    offset: int | None = None,
   157→) -> int:
   158→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   159→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   160→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   161→
   162→    # Remove from skipped if present
   163→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   164→    for fid in finding_ids:
   165→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   166→
   167→    # Remove from current position in order
   168→    for fid in finding_ids:
   169→        if fid in order:
   170→            order.remove(fid)
   171→
   172→    # Resolve insertion point
   173→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   174→
   175→    # Insert in original order
   176→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   177→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   178→
   179→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   180→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   181→    if position == "top":
   182→        promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   183→        existing_promoted = set(promoted)
   184→        for fid in finding_ids:
   185→            if fid not in existing_promoted:
   186→                promoted.append(fid)
   187→                existing_promoted.add(fid)
   188→        plan["promoted_ids"] = promoted
   189→
   190→    return len(finding_ids)
   191→
   192→
   193→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   194→# Skip / unskip
   195→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   196→
   197→def skip_items(
   198→    plan: PlanModel,
   199→    finding_ids: list[str],
   200→    *,
   201→    kind: str = "temporary",
   202→    reason: str | None = None,
   203→    note: str | None = None,
   204→    attestation: str | None = None,
   205→    review_after: int | None = None,
   206→    scan_count: int = 0,
   207→) -> int:
   208→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   209→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   210→    now = utc_now()
   211→    count = 0
   212→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   213→    skip_set = set(finding_ids)
   214→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   215→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]
   216→    for fid in finding_ids:
   217→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   218→        skipped[fid] = {
   219→            "finding_id": fid,
   220→            "kind": kind,
   221→            "reason": reason,
   222→            "note": note,
   223→            "attestation": attestation,
   224→            "created_at": now,
   225→            "review_after": review_after,
   226→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   227→        }
   228→        count += 1
   229→    return count
   230→
   231→
   232→def unskip_items(
   233→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   234→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   235→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   236→
   237→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   238→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   239→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   240→    """
   241→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   242→    count = 0
   243→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   244→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   245→    for fid in finding_ids:
   246→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   247→        if entry is not None:
   248→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   249→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   250→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   251→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   252→            count += 1
   253→    return count, need_reopen
   254→
   255→
   256→def resurface_stale_skips(
   257→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   258→) -> list[str]:
   259→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   260→
   261→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   262→    """
   263→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   264→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   265→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   266→    for fid in list(skipped):
   267→        entry = skipped[fid]
   268→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   269→            continue
   270→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   271→        if review_after is None:
   272→            continue
   273→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   274→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   275→            skipped.pop(fid)
   276→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   277→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   278→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   279→    return resurfaced
   280→
   281→
   282→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   283→# Describe / annotate
   284→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   285→
   286→def describe_finding(
   287→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   288→) -> None:
   289→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   290→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   291→    now = utc_now()
   292→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   293→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   294→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   295→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   296→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   297→
   298→
   299→def annotate_finding(
   300→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   301→) -> None:
   302→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   303→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   304→    now = utc_now()
   305→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   306→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   307→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   308→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   309→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   310→
   311→
   312→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   313→# Clusters
   314→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   315→
   316→def create_cluster(
   317→    plan: PlanModel,
   318→    name: str,
   319→    description: str | None = None,
   320→    action: str | None = None,
   321→) -> Cluster:
   322→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   323→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   324→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   325→        raise ValueError(
   326→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   327→        )
   328→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   329→        raise ValueError(
   330→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for synthesis epics: {name!r}"
   331→        )
   332→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   333→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   334→    now = utc_now()
   335→    cluster: Cluster = {
   336→        "name": name,
   337→        "description": description,
   338→        "finding_ids": [],
   339→        "created_at": now,
   340→        "updated_at": now,
   341→        "auto": False,
   342→        "cluster_key": "",
   343→        "action": action,
   344→        "user_modified": False,
   345→    }
   346→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   347→    return cluster
   348→
   349→
   350→def add_to_cluster(
   351→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   352→) -> int:
   353→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   354→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   355→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   356→    if cluster is None:
   357→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   358→
   359→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   360→    count = 0
   361→    now = utc_now()
   362→    for fid in finding_ids:
   363→        if fid not in member_ids:
   364→            member_ids.append(fid)
   365→            count += 1
   366→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   367→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   368→        if fid not in overrides:
   369→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   370→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   371→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   372→
   373→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   374→    return count
   375→
   376→
   377→def remove_from_cluster(
   378→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   379→) -> int:
   380→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   381→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   382→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   383→    if cluster is None:
   384→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   385→
   386→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   387→    now = utc_now()
   388→    count = 0
   389→    for fid in finding_ids:
   390→        if fid in member_ids:
   391→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   392→            count += 1
   393→        # Clear cluster from override
   394→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   395→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   396→            override["cluster"] = None
   397→            override["updated_at"] = now
   398→
   399→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   400→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   401→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   402→
   403→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   404→    return count
   405→
   406→
   407→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   408→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   409→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   410→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   411→    if cluster is None:
   412→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   413→
   414→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   415→    now = utc_now()
   416→    for fid in orphaned:
   417→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   418→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   419→            override["cluster"] = None
   420→            override["updated_at"] = now
   421→
   422→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   423→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   424→
   425→    return orphaned
   426→
   427→
   428→def merge_clusters(
   429→    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str
   430→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   431→    """Move all source findings to target, copy missing metadata, delete source.
   432→
   433→    Returns ``(added_count, source_finding_ids)``.
   434→    """
   435→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   436→    if source_name == target_name:
   437→        raise ValueError("Cannot merge a cluster into itself")
   438→    source = plan["clusters"].get(source_name)
   439→    if source is None:
   440→        raise ValueError(f"Source cluster {source_name!r} does not exist")
   441→    target = plan["clusters"].get(target_name)
   442→    if target is None:
   443→        raise ValueError(f"Target cluster {target_name!r} does not exist")
   444→
   445→    source_ids = list(source.get("finding_ids", []))
   446→    target_ids: list[str] = target["finding_ids"]
   447→    now = utc_now()
   448→
   449→    # Add source findings to target (deduplicate)
   450→    existing = set(target_ids)
   451→    added = 0
   452→    for fid in source_ids:
   453→        if fid not in existing:
   454→            target_ids.append(fid)
   455→            existing.add(fid)
   456→            added += 1
   457→        # Update override to point to target cluster
   458→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   459→        if fid not in overrides:
   460→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   461→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = target_name
   462→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   463→
   464→    # Copy metadata from source if target is missing them
   465→    if not target.get("description") and source.get("description"):
   466→        target["description"] = source["description"]
   467→    if not target.get("action_steps") and source.get("action_steps"):
   468→        target["action_steps"] = list(source["action_steps"])
   469→    if not target.get("action") and source.get("action"):
   470→        target["action"] = source["action"]
   471→
   472→    target["updated_at"] = now
   473→
   474→    # Delete source cluster
   475→    plan["clusters"].pop(source_name, None)
   476→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == source_name:
   477→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   478→
   479→    return added, source_ids
   480→
   481→
   482→def move_cluster(
   483→    plan: PlanModel,
   484→    cluster_name: str,
   485→    position: str,
   486→    target: str | None = None,
   487→    offset: int | None = None,
   488→) -> int:
   489→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   490→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   491→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   492→    if cluster is None:
   493→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   494→
   495→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   496→    if not member_ids:
   497→        return 0
   498→
   499→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   500→
   501→
   502→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   503→# Focus
   504→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   505→
   506→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   507→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   508→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   509→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   510→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   511→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   512→
   513→
   514→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   515→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   516→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   517→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   518→
   519→
   520→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   521→# Reset
   522→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   523→
   524→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   525→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   526→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   527→    plan.clear()
   528→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   529→        plan[k] = v
   530→    plan["created"] = created
   531→
   532→
   533→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   534→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   535→
   536→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   537→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   538→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   539→    """
   540→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   541→    found = 0
   542→
   543→    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
   544→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   545→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]
   546→
   547→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   548→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   549→    for fid in finding_ids:
   550→        was_present = False
   551→        if fid in order:
   552→            order.remove(fid)
   553→            was_present = True
   554→        if fid in skipped:
   555→            skipped.pop(fid)
   556→            was_present = True
   557→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   558→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   559→            if fid in ids:
   560→                ids.remove(fid)
   561→                was_present = True
   562→        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
   563→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   564→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   565→            override["cluster"] = None
   566→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   567→        if was_present:
   568→            found += 1
   569→
   570→    return found
   571→
   572→
   573→__all__ = [
   574→    "add_to_cluster",
   575→    "annotate_finding",
   576→    "append_log_entry",
   577→    "clear_focus",
   578→    "create_cluster",
   579→    "delete_cluster",
   580→    "describe_finding",
   581→    "merge_clusters",
   582→    "move_cluster",
   583→    "move_items",
   584→    "purge_ids",
   585→    "remove_from_cluster",
   586→    "reset_plan",
   587→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   588→    "set_focus",
   589→    "skip_items",
   590→    "unskip_items",
   591→]
   592→

1→"""Shared plan constants and helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→CONFIDENCE_ORDER = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
     6→
     7→
     8→def is_subjective_phase(phase) -> bool:
     9→    """Return True when a phase is subjective/review oriented."""
    10→    label = (phase.label or "").lower()
    11→    run_name = getattr(phase.run, "__name__", "").lower()
    12→    return (
    13→        "subjective" in label
    14→        or "review" in label
    15→        or run_name == "phase_subjective_review"
    16→    )
    17→
