    88→    # Review findings are scored via subjective dimensions, not mechanical dimensions.
    89→    "review": DetectorScoringPolicy("review", None, None, file_based=True),
    90→}
    91→_BASE_DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES: dict[str, DetectorScoringPolicy] = dict(
    92→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES
    93→)
    94→
    95→# Detectors where potential = file count but findings are per-(file, sub-type).
    96→# Per-file weighted failures are capped at 1.0 to match the file-based denominator.
    97→FILE_BASED_DETECTORS = {
    98→    detector
    99→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items()
   100→    if policy.file_based
   101→}
   102→
   103→
   104→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:
   105→    """Derive dimensions from DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.
   106→
   107→    Each unique (dimension, tier) pair becomes a Dimension, with its detectors
   108→    collected automatically. Order follows first-seen in the policies dict.
   109→    """
   110→    # Collect (dimension_name -> tier) preserving first-seen order,
   111→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   112→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}
   113→    grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   114→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():
   115→        if policy.dimension is None or policy.tier is None:
   116→            continue
   117→        if policy.dimension not in dim_tiers:
   118→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   119→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   120→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   121→    return [
   122→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   123→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   124→    ]
   125→
   126→
   127→DIMENSIONS = _build_dimensions()
   128→DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME = {d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS}
   129→
   130→TIER_WEIGHTS = {
   131→    Tier.AUTO_FIX: 1,
   132→    Tier.QUICK_FIX: 2,
   133→    Tier.JUDGMENT: 3,
   134→    Tier.MAJOR_REFACTOR: 4,
   135→}
   136→CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {Confidence.HIGH: 1.0, Confidence.MEDIUM: 0.7, Confidence.LOW: 0.3}
   137→
   138→# Minimum checks for full dimension weight — below this, weight is dampened
   139→# proportionally. Prevents small-sample dimensions from swinging the overall score.
   140→MIN_SAMPLE = 200
   141→
   142→# Holistic review weight: findings with file="." and detail.holistic=True
   143→# get a 10x weight multiplier for display/priority purposes (issues list,
   144→# remediation engine).  NOT used in score computation — review findings are
   145→# excluded from the detection scoring pipeline (scored via assessments only).
   146→HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER = 10.0
   147→HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL = 10
   148→
   149→# Budget: subjective dimensions get this fraction of the overall score.
   150→# Mechanical dimensions get the remainder.
   151→SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60
   152→MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 1.0 - SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   153→
   154→# Per-dimension weighting within the mechanical pool.
   155→# Keep this balanced: no special boost for security/test in the pool itself.
   156→MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   157→    "file health": 2.0,
   158→    "code quality": 1.0,
   159→    "duplication": 1.0,
   160→    "test health": 1.0,
   161→    "security": 1.0,
   162→}
   163→
   164→# Per-dimension weighting within the subjective pool.
   165→# Emphasize elegance and contract/type coherence for a stronger architecture north star.
   166→SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   167→    "high elegance": 22.0,
   168→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
   169→    "low elegance": 12.0,
   170→    "contracts": 12.0,
   171→    "type safety": 12.0,
   172→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
   173→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
   174→    # Low-but-meaningful structural signal (about half of the subjective
   175→    # average weight) so it matters without dominating craftsmanship axes.
   176→    "structure nav": 5.0,
   177→    "error consistency": 3.0,
   178→    "naming quality": 2.0,
   179→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
   180→    "design coherence": 10.0,
   181→}
   182→
   183→# Synthetic check count for subjective dimensions in dimension_scores.
   184→SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS = 10
   185→
   186→FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
   187→    "lenient": frozenset({"open"}),
   188→    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix"}),
   189→    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive"}),
   190→}
   191→
   192→# Tolerance for treating a subjective score as "on target" in integrity checks.
   193→# Scores within this band of the target are flagged as potential gaming.
   194→SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE = 0.05
   195→
   196→
   197→def matches_target_score(
   198→    score: object,
   199→    target: object,
   200→    *,
   201→    tolerance: float = SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
   202→) -> bool:
   203→    """Return True when score is within tolerance of target."""
   204→    try:
   205→        score_value = float(score)
   206→        target_value = float(target)
   207→        tolerance_value = max(0.0, float(tolerance))
   208→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   209→        return False
   210→    return abs(score_value - target_value) <= tolerance_value
   211→
   212→
   213→def register_scoring_policy(policy: DetectorScoringPolicy) -> None:
   214→    """Register a scoring policy at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   215→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES[policy.detector] = policy
   216→    _rebuild_derived()
   217→
   218→
   219→def reset_registered_scoring_policies() -> None:
   220→    """Reset runtime-added scoring policies to built-in defaults."""
   221→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.clear()
   222→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.update(_BASE_DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES)
   223→    _rebuild_derived()
   224→
   225→
   226→def _rebuild_derived() -> None:
   227→    """Rebuild DIMENSIONS, DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME, FILE_BASED_DETECTORS from current state.
   228→
   229→    Mutates existing objects in-place so that all references (including imports
   230→    that bound the original objects) see the updates.
   231→    """
   232→    new_dims = _build_dimensions()
   233→    DIMENSIONS.clear()
   234→    DIMENSIONS.extend(new_dims)
   235→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.clear()
   236→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.update({d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS})
   237→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.clear()
   238→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.update(
   239→        det for det, pol in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items() if pol.file_based
   240→    )
   241→
   242→
   243→def detector_policy(detector: str) -> DetectorScoringPolicy:
   244→    """Get scoring policy for a detector, with a safe default fallback."""
   245→    return DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(
   246→        detector,
   247→        DetectorScoringPolicy(detector=detector, dimension=None, tier=None),
   248→    )
   249→
   250→
   251→__all__ = [
   252→    "CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS",
   253→    "DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES",
   254→    "DIMENSIONS",
   255→    "DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME",
   256→    "FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE",
   257→    "FILE_BASED_DETECTORS",
   258→    "HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER",
   259→    "HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL",
   260→    "MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS",
   261→    "MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION",
   262→    "MIN_SAMPLE",
   263→    "SCORING_MODES",
   264→    "SECURITY_EXCLUDED_ZONES",
   265→    "SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS",
   266→    "SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS",
   267→    "SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE",
   268→    "SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION",
   269→    "TIER_WEIGHTS",
   270→    "DetectorScoringPolicy",
   271→    "Dimension",
   272→    "ScoreMode",
   273→    "detector_policy",
   274→    "matches_target_score",
   275→    "register_scoring_policy",
   276→    "reset_registered_scoring_policies",
   277→]
   278→

1→"""Health score aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     6→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     7→    MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
     8→    MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
     9→    MIN_SAMPLE,
    10→    SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
    16→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
    17→
    18→
    19→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
    20→    return float(
    21→        MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
    22→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
    23→            1.0,
    24→        )
    25→    )
    26→
    27→
    28→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
    29→    subjective_meta = (
    30→        data.get("detectors", {}).get("subjective_assessment", {})
    31→        if isinstance(data, dict)
    32→        else {}
    33→    )
    34→    configured = (
    35→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")
    36→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)
    37→        else None
    38→    )
    39→    if is_numeric(configured):
    40→        return max(0.0, float(configured))
    41→
    42→    return float(
    43→        SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
    44→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
    45→            1.0,
    46→        )
    47→    )
    48→
    49→
    50→def compute_health_breakdown(
    51→    dimension_scores: dict,
    52→    *,
    53→    score_key: str = "score",
    54→) -> dict[str, object]:
    55→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
    56→    if not dimension_scores:
    57→        return {
    58→            "overall_score": 100.0,
    59→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
    60→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
    61→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
    62→            "subjective_avg": None,
    63→            "entries": [],
    64→        }
    65→
    66→    mech_sum = 0.0
    67→    mech_weight = 0.0
    68→    subj_sum = 0.0
    69→    subj_weight = 0.0
    70→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    71→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    72→
    73→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
    74→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
    75→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
    76→        if is_subjective:
    77→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
    78→            effective = configured
    79→            subj_sum += score * effective
    80→            subj_weight += effective
    81→            subjective_rows.append(
    82→                {
    83→                    "name": str(name),
    84→                    "score": score,
    85→                    "configured_weight": configured,
    86→                    "effective_weight": effective,
    87→                }
    88→            )
    89→            continue
    90→
    91→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
    92→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
    93→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
    94→        effective = configured * sample_factor
    95→        mech_sum += score * effective
    96→        mech_weight += effective
    97→        mechanical_rows.append(
    98→            {
    99→                "name": str(name),
   100→                "score": score,
   101→                "checks": checks,
   102→                "sample_factor": sample_factor,
   103→                "configured_weight": configured,
   104→                "effective_weight": effective,
   105→            }
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   109→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   110→
   111→    if subj_avg is None:
   112→        mechanical_fraction = 1.0
   113→        subjective_fraction = 0.0
   114→        overall_score = round(mech_avg, 1)
   115→    elif mech_weight == 0:
   116→        mechanical_fraction = 0.0
   117→        subjective_fraction = 1.0
   118→        overall_score = round(subj_avg, 1)
   119→    else:
   120→        mechanical_fraction = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   121→        subjective_fraction = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   122→        overall_score = round(
   123→            mech_avg * mechanical_fraction + subj_avg * subjective_fraction,
   124→            1,
   125→        )
   126→
   127→    entries: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   128→    for row in mechanical_rows:
   129→        pool_share = (
   130→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / mech_weight if mech_weight > 0 else 0.0
   131→        )
   132→        per_point = mechanical_fraction * pool_share
   133→        score = float(row["score"])
   134→        entries.append(
   135→            {
   136→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   137→                "pool": "mechanical",
   138→                "score": score,
   139→                "checks": float(row["checks"]),
   140→                "sample_factor": float(row["sample_factor"]),
   141→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   142→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   143→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   144→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   145→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   146→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   147→            }
   148→        )
   149→
   150→    for row in subjective_rows:
   151→        pool_share = (
   152→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / subj_weight if subj_weight > 0 else 0.0
   153→        )
   154→        per_point = subjective_fraction * pool_share
   155→        score = float(row["score"])
   156→        entries.append(
   157→            {
   158→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   159→                "pool": "subjective",
   160→                "score": score,
   161→                "checks": 0.0,
   162→                "sample_factor": 1.0,
   163→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   164→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   165→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   166→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   167→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   168→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   169→            }
   170→        )
   171→
   172→    return {
   173→        "overall_score": overall_score,
   174→        "mechanical_fraction": mechanical_fraction,
   175→        "subjective_fraction": subjective_fraction,
   176→        "mechanical_avg": mech_avg,
   177→        "subjective_avg": subj_avg,
   178→        "entries": entries,
   179→    }
   180→
   181→
   182→def compute_health_score(
   183→    dimension_scores: dict,
   184→    *,
   185→    score_key: str = "score",
   186→) -> float:
   187→    """Budget-weighted blend of mechanical and subjective dimension scores."""
   188→    return float(
   189→        compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key=score_key)[
   190→            "overall_score"
   191→        ]
   192→    )
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = ["compute_health_breakdown", "compute_health_score"]
   196→
   197→

1→"""Detector-to-score impact helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     6→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES,
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
     9→    Dimension,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_score
    12→
    13→
    14→def compute_score_impact(
    15→    dimension_scores: dict,
    16→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    17→    detector: str,
    18→    issues_to_fix: int,
    19→) -> float:
    20→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
    21→    target_dim = None
    22→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    23→        if detector in dim.detectors:
    24→            target_dim = dim
    25→            break
    26→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
    27→        return 0.0
    28→
    29→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    30→    if potential <= 0:
    31→        return 0.0
    32→
    33→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
    34→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
    35→
    36→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
    37→    if not det_data:
    38→        return 0.0
    39→
    40→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
    41→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
    42→
    43→    total_potential = 0
    44→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
    45→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
    46→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
    47→        if not det_values:
    48→            continue
    49→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
    50→        if det == detector:
    51→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
    52→        else:
    53→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
    54→    if total_potential <= 0:
    55→        return 0.0
    56→
    57→    new_dim_score = (
    58→        max(
    59→            0.0,
    60→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
    61→        )
    62→        * 100
    63→    )
    64→
    65→    simulated = {name: dict(data) for name, data in dimension_scores.items()}
    66→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
    67→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
    68→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
    69→
    70→
    71→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
    72→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
    73→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
    74→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
    75→        return None
    76→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
    77→
    78→
    79→__all__ = ["compute_score_impact", "get_dimension_for_detector"]
    80→
    81→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass
    16→class ReconcileResult:
    17→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    18→
    19→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    20→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    changes: int = 0
    23→
    24→
    25→def _find_candidates(
    26→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    27→) -> list[str]:
    28→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    29→    candidates: list[str] = []
    30→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    31→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    32→            continue
    33→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    34→            candidates.append(fid)
    35→    return candidates
    36→
    37→
    38→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    39→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    40→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    41→    if finding is None:
    42→        return False
    43→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _supersede_id(
    47→    plan: PlanModel,
    48→    state: StateModel,
    49→    finding_id: str,
    50→    now: str,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    53→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    54→    detector = ""
    55→    file = ""
    56→    summary = ""
    57→    if finding:
    58→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    60→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    61→
    62→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    63→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    64→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    65→
    66→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    67→        "original_id": finding_id,
    68→        "original_detector": detector,
    69→        "original_file": file,
    70→        "original_summary": summary,
    71→        "status": "superseded",
    72→        "superseded_at": now,
    73→        "remapped_to": None,
    74→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    # Preserve any existing override note
    78→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    79→    if override and override.get("note"):
    80→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    81→
    82→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    83→
    84→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster finding_ids
    85→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    86→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    87→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    if finding_id in promoted:
    92→        promoted.remove(finding_id)
    93→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    94→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    95→        if finding_id in ids:
    96→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    97→
    98→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
    99→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
   100→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   101→        override["cluster"] = None
   102→        override["updated_at"] = now
   103→
   104→    return True
   105→
   106→
   107→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   108→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   109→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   110→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   111→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   112→
   113→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   114→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   115→        try:
   116→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   117→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   118→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   119→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   120→                to_prune.append(fid)
   121→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   122→            to_prune.append(fid)
   123→
   124→    for fid in to_prune:
   125→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   126→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   127→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   128→
   129→    return to_prune
   130→
   131→
   132→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   133→    plan: PlanModel,
   134→    state: StateModel,
   135→) -> ReconcileResult:
   136→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   137→
   138→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   139→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   140→    """
   141→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   142→    result = ReconcileResult()
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   145→
   146→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   147→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   148→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   149→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   150→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   151→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   152→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   153→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   154→
   155→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   156→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   157→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   158→    referenced_ids = {
   159→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   160→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   161→    }
   162→
   163→    # Check each referenced ID
   164→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   165→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   166→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   167→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   168→                result.changes += 1
   169→
   170→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead findings, delete empty epics
   171→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   172→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   173→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   174→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   175→            continue
   176→        finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   177→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if _is_finding_alive(state, fid)]
   178→        if alive_ids != finding_ids:
   179→            cluster["finding_ids"] = alive_ids
   180→            result.changes += 1
   181→        if not alive_ids:
   182→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   183→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   184→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   185→        result.changes += 1
   186→
   187→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   188→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   189→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   190→
   191→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   192→    if resurfaced:
   193→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   194→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   195→
   196→    # Prune old superseded entries
   197→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   198→    result.pruned = pruned
   199→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   200→
   201→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   202→    if result.changes > 0:
   203→        from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
   204→
   205→        append_log_entry(
   206→            plan,
   207→            "reconcile",
   208→            finding_ids=result.superseded,
   209→            actor="system",
   210→            detail={
   211→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   212→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   213→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   214→            },
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    return result
   218→
   219→
   220→@dataclass
   221→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   222→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   223→
   224→    new_ids: set[str]
   225→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   226→    synthesis_injected: bool
   227→
   228→
   229→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   230→    plan: PlanModel,
   231→    state: StateModel,
   232→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   233→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   234→
   235→    Appends new finding IDs to queue_order and injects synthesis::pending
   236→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new findings to sync.
   237→    """
   238→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   239→        compute_new_finding_ids,
   240→        sync_synthesis_needed,
   241→    )
   242→
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   245→    if not new_ids:
   246→        return None
   247→
   248→    # Add new finding IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   249→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   250→    existing = set(order)
   251→    added: list[str] = []
   252→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   253→        if fid not in existing:
   254→            order.append(fid)
   255→            added.append(fid)
   256→
   257→    # Inject synthesis::pending if needed
   258→    synthesis_result = sync_synthesis_needed(plan, state)
   259→    synthesis_injected = bool(
   260→        synthesis_result and getattr(synthesis_result, "injected", False)
   261→    )
   262→
   263→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   264→        new_ids=new_ids,
   265→        added_to_queue=added,
   266→        synthesis_injected=synthesis_injected,
   267→    )
   268→
   269→
   270→__all__ = [
   271→    "ReconcileResult",
   272→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   273→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   274→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   275→]
   276→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    21→
    22→SYNTHESIS_PREFIX = "synthesis::"
    23→SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS = (
    24→    "synthesis::observe",
    25→    "synthesis::reflect",
    26→    "synthesis::organize",
    27→    "synthesis::commit",
    28→)
    29→SYNTHESIS_IDS = set(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS)
    30→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    31→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    32→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("synthesis::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    33→
    34→
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→# Result dataclasses
    37→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→@dataclass
    52→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    53→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    54→
    55→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    56→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    57→
    58→    @property
    59→    def changes(self) -> int:
    60→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    61→
    62→
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→# ID helpers
    65→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66→
    67→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    68→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    69→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    70→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    71→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    72→    )
    73→
    74→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    75→    if not dim_scores:
    76→        return set()
    77→
    78→    stale: set[str] = set()
    79→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    80→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    81→            continue
    82→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    83→        if dim_key:
    84→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    85→    return stale
    86→
    87→
    88→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    89→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    90→
    91→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    92→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    93→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    94→    """
    95→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    96→
    97→    # Primary source: subjective_assessments with placeholder=True
    98→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    99→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
   100→        unscored: set[str] = set()
   101→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
   102→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   103→                continue
   104→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
   105→                continue
   106→            if dim_key:
   107→                unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
   108→        return unscored
   109→
   110→    # Fallback: check dimension_scores directly for placeholder subjective
   111→    # dimensions.  This handles the common case where subjective_assessments
   112→    # hasn't been populated yet but dimension_scores already has placeholder
   113→    # entries from scan.  We can't use scorecard_subjective_entries() here
   114→    # because the scorecard pipeline intentionally hides placeholders.
   115→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   116→    if not dim_scores:
   117→        return set()
   118→
   119→    unscored = set()
   120→    for _name, data in dim_scores.items():
   121→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   122→            continue
   123→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   124→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   125→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   126→            continue
   127→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   128→            continue
   129→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   130→        if dim_key:
   131→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
   132→    return unscored
   133→
   134→
   135→def _current_under_target_ids(
   136→    state: StateModel,
   137→    *,
   138→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
   139→) -> set[str]:
   140→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   141→
   142→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   143→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   144→    """
   145→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
   146→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
   147→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
   148→    )
   149→
   150→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   151→    if not dim_scores:
   152→        return set()
   153→
   154→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   155→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   156→
   157→    under_target: set[str] = set()
   158→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   159→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
   160→            continue
   161→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   162→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   163→            continue
   164→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   165→        if not dim_key:
   166→            continue
   167→        fid = f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   168→        if fid not in stale_ids and fid not in unscored_ids:
   169→            under_target.add(fid)
   170→    return under_target
   171→
   172→
   173→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   174→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   175→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   176→
   177→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   178→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   179→
   180→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   181→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   182→    """
   183→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   184→    if not promoted:
   185→        return 0
   186→    last_idx = -1
   187→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   188→        if fid in promoted:
   189→            last_idx = i
   190→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   191→
   192→
   193→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   194→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   195→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   196→
   197→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   198→    plan: PlanModel,
   199→    state: StateModel,
   200→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   201→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   202→
   203→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   204→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   205→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   206→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   207→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   208→    """
   209→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   210→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   211→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   212→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   213→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   214→
   215→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   216→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   217→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   218→        fid for fid in order
   219→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   220→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   221→        and fid not in stale_ids
   222→    ]
   223→    for fid in to_remove:
   224→        order.remove(fid)
   225→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   226→
   227→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   228→    existing = set(order)
   229→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   230→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   231→        if uid not in existing:
   232→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   233→            result.injected.append(uid)
   234→
   235→    return result
   236→
   237→
   238→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   239→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   240→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   241→
   242→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   243→    plan: PlanModel,
   244→    state: StateModel,
   245→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   246→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   247→
   248→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   249→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   250→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   251→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   252→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   253→    """
   254→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   255→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   256→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   257→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   258→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   259→
   260→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   261→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   262→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   263→        fid for fid in order
   264→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   265→        and fid not in stale_ids
   266→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   267→    ]
   268→    for fid in to_remove:
   269→        order.remove(fid)
   270→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   271→
   272→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   273→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   274→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   275→        existing = set(order)
   276→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   277→            if sid not in existing:
   278→                order.append(sid)
   279→                result.injected.append(sid)
   280→
   281→    return result
   282→
   283→
   284→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   285→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   286→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   287→
   288→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   289→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   290→
   291→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   292→    """
   293→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   294→    review_ids = sorted(
   295→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   296→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   297→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   298→    )
   299→    if not review_ids:
   300→        return ""
   301→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   302→
   303→
   304→@dataclass
   305→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   306→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   307→
   308→    injected: bool = False
   309→    pruned: bool = False
   310→
   311→    @property
   312→    def changes(self) -> int:
   313→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   314→
   315→
   316→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   317→    plan: PlanModel,
   318→    state: StateModel,
   319→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   320→    """Inject 4 synthesis stage IDs at front of queue when review findings change.
   321→
   322→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_synthesis_meta``.
   323→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion removes them.
   324→    """
   325→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   326→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   327→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   328→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   329→    confirmed = set(meta.get("synthesis_stages", {}).keys())
   330→
   331→    # Check if any synthesis stage is already in queue
   332→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in SYNTHESIS_IDS)
   333→
   334→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   335→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   336→
   337→    # If hash changed and no stages present, inject unconfirmed stages
   338→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   339→        insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   340→        stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   341→        existing = set(order)
   342→        injected_count = 0
   343→        for sid, name in zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names):
   344→            if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   345→                order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   346→                injected_count += 1
   347→        if injected_count:
   348→            result.injected = True
   349→
   350→    return result
   351→
   352→
   353→@dataclass
   354→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   355→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   356→
   357→    injected: bool = False
   358→
   359→    @property
   360→    def changes(self) -> int:
   361→        return int(self.injected)
   362→
   363→
   364→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   365→    plan: PlanModel,
   366→    state: StateModel,
   367→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   368→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   369→
   370→    Only injects when:
   371→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   372→    - At least one objective finding exists
   373→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   374→    - No synthesis stages are pending
   375→    """
   376→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   377→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   378→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   379→
   380→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   381→        return result
   382→
   383→    # Don't inject if synthesis stages are pending
   384→    if any(sid in order for sid in SYNTHESIS_IDS):
   385→        return result
   386→
   387→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   388→    unscored = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   389→    if unscored:
   390→        return result
   391→
   392→    # Check that objective findings exist
   393→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   394→    has_objective = any(
   395→        f.get("status") == "open"
   396→        and f.get("detector") not in ("review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment")
   397→        for f in findings.values()
   398→    )
   399→    if not has_objective:
   400→        return result
   401→
   402→    # Insert after any subjective items, before findings
   403→    insert_at = 0
   404→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   405→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(SYNTHESIS_PREFIX):
   406→            insert_at = i + 1
   407→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   408→    result.injected = True
   409→    return result
   410→
   411→
   412→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   413→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last synthesis.
   414→
   415→    Returns an empty set when no prior synthesis has recorded ``synthesized_ids``.
   416→    """
   417→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   418→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   419→    current = {
   420→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   421→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   422→    }
   423→    return current - synthesized if synthesized else set()
   424→
   425→
   426→def is_synthesis_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   427→    """Side-effect-free check: is synthesis needed?
   428→
   429→    Returns True when any ``synthesis::*`` stage ID is in the queue OR the
   430→    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed synthesis.
   431→    """
   432→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   433→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   434→    if order & SYNTHESIS_IDS:
   435→        return True
   436→    # Legacy check
   437→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   438→        return True
   439→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   440→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   441→    if not last_hash:
   442→        return False
   443→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   444→    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)
   445→
   446→
   447→__all__ = [
   448→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   449→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   450→    "SYNTHESIS_IDS",
   451→    "SYNTHESIS_PREFIX",
   452→    "SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS",
   453→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   454→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   455→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   456→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   457→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   458→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   459→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   460→    "_current_under_target_ids",
   461→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   462→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   463→    "is_synthesis_stale",
   464→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   465→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   466→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   467→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   468→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   469→]
   470→

1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_stale_ids,
    13→    _current_under_target_ids,
    14→    _current_unscored_ids,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    17→
    18→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    19→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    20→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    21→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    22→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    23→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    24→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    25→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    26→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    27→
    28→
    29→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→# Grouping key computation
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    34→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    35→
    36→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    37→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    38→    """
    39→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    40→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    41→    if kind:
    42→        return kind
    43→
    44→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    45→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    46→    parts = fid.split("::")
    47→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    48→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    49→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    50→        candidate = parts[-1]
    51→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    52→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    53→            return candidate
    54→    return None
    55→
    56→
    57→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    58→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    59→
    60→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    61→    """
    62→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    63→
    64→    if meta is None:
    65→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    66→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    67→
    68→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    69→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    70→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    71→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    72→        if dimension:
    73→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    74→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    75→
    76→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    77→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    78→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    79→    ):
    80→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    81→        if file:
    82→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    83→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    84→
    85→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    86→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    87→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    88→        if subtype:
    89→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    90→
    91→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    92→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    93→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    94→
    95→    # Everything else → by detector
    96→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    97→
    98→
    99→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
   100→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
   101→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
   102→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
   103→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   104→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   105→    # detector::security → auto/security
   106→    parts = key.split("::")
   107→    if len(parts) == 2:
   108→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   109→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   110→        if prefix_type == "review":
   111→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   112→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   113→    if len(parts) == 3:
   114→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   115→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   116→
   117→
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→# Description and action generation
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→def _generate_description(
   123→    cluster_name: str,
   124→    members: list[dict],
   125→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   126→    subtype: str | None,
   127→) -> str:
   128→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   129→    count = len(members)
   130→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   131→
   132→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   133→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   134→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   135→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   136→
   137→    if detector == "structural":
   138→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   139→        if len(files) == 1:
   140→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   141→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   142→
   143→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   144→    if subtype:
   145→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   146→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   147→
   148→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   149→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   150→
   151→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   152→
   153→
   154→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   155→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   156→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   157→        return None
   158→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   159→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   160→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   161→        return fixer_name
   162→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   163→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   164→        if subtype in fixer:
   165→            return fixer
   166→    return None
   167→
   168→
   169→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   170→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   171→
   172→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   173→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   174→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   175→    """
   176→    if " — " in guidance:
   177→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   178→    return guidance
   179→
   180→
   181→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   182→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   183→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   184→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   185→}
   186→
   187→
   188→def _generate_action(
   189→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   190→    subtype: str | None,
   191→) -> str:
   192→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   193→
   194→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   195→    """
   196→    if meta is None:
   197→        return "review and fix each finding"
   198→
   199→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   200→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   201→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   202→        if matched_fixer:
   203→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   204→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   205→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   206→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   207→
   208→    if meta.tool == "move":
   209→        return "desloppify move"
   210→
   211→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   212→    if meta.guidance:
   213→        if subtype:
   214→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   215→        return meta.guidance
   216→
   217→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   218→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   219→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   220→    )
   221→
   222→
   223→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   224→# Repair
   225→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   226→
   227→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
   228→    """Clear override cluster refs that point to non-existent clusters."""
   229→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   230→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   231→    repaired = 0
   232→    for override in overrides.values():
   233→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
   234→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
   235→            override["cluster"] = None
   236→            override["updated_at"] = now
   237→            repaired += 1
   238→    return repaired
   239→
   240→
   241→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   242→# Main algorithm
   243→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   244→
   245→def auto_cluster_findings(
   246→    plan: PlanModel,
   247→    state: StateModel,
   248→    *,
   249→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
   250→) -> int:
   251→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   252→
   253→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   254→    """
   255→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   256→    changes = 0
   257→
   258→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   259→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   260→
   261→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   262→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   263→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   264→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   265→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   266→
   267→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   268→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   269→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   270→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   271→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   272→            continue
   273→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   274→            continue
   275→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   276→            continue
   277→
   278→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   279→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   280→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   281→        if key is None:
   282→            continue
   283→
   284→        groups[key].append(fid)
   285→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   286→
   287→    # Drop singleton groups
   288→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   289→
   290→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   291→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   292→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   293→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   294→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   295→            if ck:
   296→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   297→
   298→    now = utc_now()
   299→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   300→
   301→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   302→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   303→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   304→
   305→        # Representative finding for metadata
   306→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   307→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   308→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   309→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   310→
   311→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   312→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   313→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   314→
   315→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   316→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   317→
   318→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   319→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   320→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   321→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   322→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   323→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   324→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   325→                if new_ids:
   326→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   327→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   328→                    changes += 1
   329→            else:
   330→                # Replace membership wholesale
   331→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   332→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   333→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   334→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   335→                    cluster["description"] = description
   336→                    cluster["action"] = action
   337→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   338→                    changes += 1
   339→        else:
   340→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   341→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   342→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   343→                # Append a disambiguator
   344→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   345→
   346→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   347→                "name": cluster_name,
   348→                "description": description,
   349→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   350→                "created_at": now,
   351→                "updated_at": now,
   352→                "auto": True,
   353→                "cluster_key": key,
   354→                "action": action,
   355→                "user_modified": False,
   356→            }
   357→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   358→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   359→            changes += 1
   360→
   361→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   362→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   363→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   364→        for fid in member_ids:
   365→            if fid not in overrides:
   366→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   367→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   368→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   369→
   370→    # --- Subjective dimension clusters (unscored + stale) -------------------
   371→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   372→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   373→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   374→    )
   375→    unscored_state_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   376→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   377→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   378→    )
   379→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   380→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids
   381→    )
   382→
   383→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   384→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   385→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   386→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   387→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   388→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   389→
   390→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   391→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   392→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   393→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   394→            new_ids = set(unscored_queue_ids)
   395→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   396→                cluster["finding_ids"] = unscored_queue_ids
   397→                cluster["description"] = description
   398→                cluster["action"] = action
   399→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   400→                changes += 1
   401→        else:
   402→            clusters[_UNSCORED_NAME] = {
   403→                "name": _UNSCORED_NAME,
   404→                "description": description,
   405→                "finding_ids": unscored_queue_ids,
   406→                "created_at": now,
   407→                "updated_at": now,
   408→                "auto": True,
   409→                "cluster_key": _UNSCORED_KEY,
   410→                "action": action,
   411→                "user_modified": False,
   412→            }
   413→            existing_by_key[_UNSCORED_KEY] = _UNSCORED_NAME
   414→            changes += 1
   415→
   416→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   417→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY, _UNSCORED_NAME)
   418→        for fid in unscored_queue_ids:
   419→            if fid not in overrides:
   420→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   421→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   422→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   423→
   424→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   425→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   426→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   427→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   428→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   429→        action = (
   430→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   431→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   432→            + " --force-review-rerun"
   433→        )
   434→
   435→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   436→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   437→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   438→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   439→            new_ids = set(stale_queue_ids)
   440→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   441→                cluster["finding_ids"] = stale_queue_ids
   442→                cluster["description"] = description
   443→                cluster["action"] = action
   444→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   445→                changes += 1
   446→        else:
   447→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   448→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   449→                "description": description,
   450→                "finding_ids": stale_queue_ids,
   451→                "created_at": now,
   452→                "updated_at": now,
   453→                "auto": True,
   454→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   455→                "action": action,
   456→                "user_modified": False,
   457→            }
   458→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   459→            changes += 1
   460→
   461→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   462→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   463→        for fid in stale_queue_ids:
   464→            if fid not in overrides:
   465→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   466→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   467→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   468→
   469→    # -- Under-target review cluster (optional, current but below target) ----
   470→    # _current_under_target_ids already excludes stale and unscored dims.
   471→    # Don't filter through all_subjective_ids — these IDs may not be in
   472→    # queue_order yet (no sync function adds them).  We inject them into
   473→    # queue_order below so they get proper positions.
   474→    under_target_ids = _current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   475→    stale_state_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   476→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   477→
   478→    # Prune: remove IDs that were previously in the under-target cluster
   479→    # but are no longer under target (they've improved above threshold).
   480→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   481→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   482→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   483→    _ut_prune = [
   484→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   485→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   486→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   487→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   488→        and fid in order
   489→    ]
   490→    for fid in _ut_prune:
   491→        order.remove(fid)
   492→        changes += 1
   493→    if len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   494→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   495→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   496→        description = (
   497→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   498→            f"dimensions under target score"
   499→        )
   500→        action = (
   501→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   502→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   503→            + " --force-review-rerun"
   504→        )
   505→
   506→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   507→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   508→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   509→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   510→            new_ids = set(under_target_queue_ids)
   511→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   512→                cluster["finding_ids"] = under_target_queue_ids
   513→                cluster["description"] = description
   514→                cluster["action"] = action
   515→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   516→                changes += 1
   517→        else:
   518→            under_target_cluster = {
   519→                "name": _UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   520→                "description": description,
   521→                "finding_ids": under_target_queue_ids,
   522→                "created_at": now,
   523→                "updated_at": now,
   524→                "auto": True,
   525→                "[REDACTED],
   526→                "action": action,
   527→                "user_modified": False,
   528→            }
   529→            under_target_cluster["optional"] = True
   530→            clusters[_UNDER_TARGET_NAME] = under_target_cluster
   531→            existing_by_key[_UNDER_TARGET_KEY] = _UNDER_TARGET_NAME
   532→            changes += 1
   533→
   534→        # Ensure under-target IDs are in queue_order (at the back)
   535→        order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   536→        existing_order = set(order)
   537→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   538→            if fid not in existing_order:
   539→                order.append(fid)
   540→
   541→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   542→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY, _UNDER_TARGET_NAME)
   543→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   544→            if fid not in overrides:
   545→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   546→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   547→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   548→
   549→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   550→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   551→        cluster = clusters[name]
   552→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   553→            continue
   554→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   555→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   556→            continue
   557→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   558→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   559→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   560→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   561→            if alive:
   562→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   563→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   564→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   565→                    changes += 1
   566→                continue
   567→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   568→        # Delete stale cluster
   569→        del clusters[name]
   570→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   571→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   572→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   573→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   574→                override["cluster"] = None
   575→                override["updated_at"] = now
   576→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   577→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   578→        changes += 1
   579→
   580→    # Repair any orphaned override cluster references from prior bugs
   581→    repaired = _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, now)
   582→    changes += repaired
   583→
   584→    plan["updated"] = now
   585→    return changes
   586→
   587→
   588→__all__ = [
   589→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   590→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   591→]
   592→
