```
1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema_migrations import (
     8→    ensure_container_types as _ensure_container_types,
     9→    migrate_deferred_to_skipped as _migrate_deferred_to_skipped,
    10→    migrate_epics_to_clusters as _migrate_epics_to_clusters,
    11→    migrate_v5_to_v6 as _migrate_v5_to_v6,
    12→    normalize_cluster_defaults as _normalize_cluster_defaults,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    15→
    16→PLAN_VERSION = 6
    17→
    18→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}
    19→
    20→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    21→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = {
    22→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    23→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    24→}
    25→
    26→
    27→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    28→    finding_id: Required[str]
    29→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    30→    reason: str | None
    31→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    32→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    33→    created_at: str
    34→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    35→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    36→
    37→
    38→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    39→    finding_id: Required[str]
    40→    description: str | None
    41→    note: str | None
    42→    cluster: str | None
    43→    created_at: str
    44→    updated_at: str
    45→
    46→
    47→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    48→    name: Required[str]
    49→    description: str | None
    50→    finding_ids: list[str]
    51→    created_at: str
    52→    updated_at: str
    53→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    54→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    55→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    56→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    57→
    58→
    59→class ExecutionLogEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    60→    timestamp: Required[str]
    61→    action: Required[str]  # "done", "skip", "unskip", "resolve", "reconcile", "cluster_done", "focus", "reset"
    62→    finding_ids: list[str]
    63→    cluster_name: str | None
    64→    actor: str  # "user" | "system" | "agent"
    65→    note: str | None
    66→    detail: dict  # action-specific extra data
    67→
    68→
    69→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    70→    original_id: Required[str]
    71→    original_detector: str
    72→    original_file: str
    73→    original_summary: str
    74→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    75→    superseded_at: str
    76→    remapped_to: str | None
    77→    candidates: list[str]
    78→    note: str | None
    79→
    80→
    81→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    82→    version: Required[int]
    83→    created: Required[str]
    84→    updated: Required[str]
    85→    queue_order: list[str]
    86→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    87→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    88→    active_cluster: str | None
    89→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    90→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    91→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    92→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
    93→    plan_start_scores: dict  # frozen score snapshot from plan creation cycle
    94→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
    95→    epic_synthesis_meta: dict  # synthesis engine metadata
    96→
    97→
    98→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    99→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   100→    now = utc_now()
   101→    return {
   102→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   103→        "created": now,
   104→        "updated": now,
   105→        "queue_order": [],
   106→        "deferred": [],
   107→        "skipped": {},
   108→        "active_cluster": None,
   109→        "overrides": {},
   110→        "clusters": {},
   111→        "superseded": {},
   112→        "promoted_ids": [],
   113→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   114→        "execution_log": [],
   115→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   116→    }
   117→
   118→
   119→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   120→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   121→
   122→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   123→    v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters),
   124→    and v5 (gates) to v6 (unified queue).
   125→    """
   126→    defaults = empty_plan()
   127→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   128→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   129→
   130→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   131→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   132→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   133→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   134→    _migrate_v5_to_v6(plan)
   135→
   136→
   137→def synthesis_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   138→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   139→    return {
   140→        name: cluster
   141→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   142→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   143→    }
   144→
   145→
   146→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   147→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   148→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   149→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   150→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   151→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   152→
   153→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   154→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   155→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   156→    if overlap:
   157→        raise ValueError(
   158→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   159→        )
   160→
   161→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   162→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   163→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   164→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   165→            raise ValueError(
   166→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   167→            )
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   172→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   173→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   174→    "Cluster",
   175→    "ItemOverride",
   176→    "PlanModel",

   181→    "empty_plan",
   182→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   183→    "synthesis_clusters",
   184→    "validate_plan",
   185→]
```

```
    90→def _compose_scorecard_dimensions(subjective_order: tuple[str, ...]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    91→    merged: list[str] = []
```

```
     2→

    23→
    24→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    25→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    26→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    27→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    28→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    29→
    30→    if (
    31→        overall_score is not None
    32→        and objective_score is not None
    33→        and strict_score is not None
    34→    ):
    35→        header_score = (
    36→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    37→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    38→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    39→        )
    40→    elif overall_score is not None:
    41→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    42→    else:
    43→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    44→
    45→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    46→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    47→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    48→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    49→
    50→    lines = [
    51→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    52→        "",
    53→        f"{header_score} | "
    54→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    55→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    56→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    57→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    58→        "",
    59→    ]
    60→
    61→    if total_files:
    62→        loc_str = (
    63→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    64→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    65→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    66→        )
    67→        lines.append(
    68→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    69→        )
    70→
    71→    return lines
    72→
    73→
    74→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    75→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    76→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    77→    if not dim_scores:
    78→        return []
    79→
    80→    lines = [
    81→        "## Health by Dimension",
    82→        "",
    83→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    84→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    85→    ]
    86→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    87→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    88→    subjective_display_names = {
    89→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    90→    }
    91→
    92→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    93→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    94→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    95→            return True
    96→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    97→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    98→
    99→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   100→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   101→        if not ds:
   102→            continue
   103→        static_names.add(dim.name)
   104→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
   105→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   106→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   107→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   108→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   109→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   110→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   111→        lines.append(
   112→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   113→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   114→        )
   115→
   116→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   117→
   118→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   119→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   120→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   121→    ]
   122→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   123→
   124→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   125→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   126→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   127→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   128→            continue
   129→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   130→            continue
   131→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   132→        rendered_names.add(name)
   133→
   134→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   135→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   136→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   137→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   138→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   139→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   140→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   141→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   142→        lines.append(
   143→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   144→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   145→        )
   146→
   147→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   148→        (name, ds)
   149→        for name, ds in sorted(
   150→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   151→        )
   152→        if (
   153→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   154→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   155→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   156→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   157→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   158→        )
   159→    ]
   160→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   161→
   162→    if subjective_rows:
   163→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   164→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   165→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   166→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   167→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   168→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   169→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   170→            lines.append(
   171→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   172→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   173→            )
   174→
   175→    lines.append("")
   176→    return lines
   177→
   178→
   179→def _plan_item_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   180→    """Build per-file sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   181→
   182→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   183→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   184→    raw_target = (
   185→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   186→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   187→        else 95
   188→    )
   189→    try:
   190→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   191→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   192→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   193→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   194→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   195→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   196→
   197→    queue = build_work_queue(
   198→        queue_state,
   199→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   200→            count=None,
   201→            scan_path=scan_path,
   202→            status="open",
   203→            include_subjective=True,
   204→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   205→        ),
   206→    )
   207→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   208→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   209→    for item in open_items:
   210→        by_file[item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   211→
   212→    lines: list[str] = []
   213→    total_count = len(open_items)
   214→    if not open_items:
   215→        return lines
   216→
   217→    lines.extend([
   218→        "---",
   219→        f"## Open Items ({total_count})",
   220→        "",
   221→    ])
   222→
   223→    sorted_files = sorted(
   224→        by_file.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→    )
   226→    for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→        lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→        lines.append("")
   230→        for item in file_items:
   231→            if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                    lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                continue
   237→
   238→            conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→            lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→            lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→        lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   247→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   248→
   249→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   250→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   251→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   252→    """
   253→    findings = state["findings"]
   254→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   255→
   256→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   257→    if plan is None:
   258→        try:
   259→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   260→            plan = load_plan()
   261→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   262→            plan = {}
   263→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   264→        plan = {}
   265→    plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   266→    plan.setdefault("skipped", {})
   267→    plan.setdefault("clusters", {})
   268→
   269→    has_plan = bool(
   270→        plan
   271→        and (
   272→            plan.get("queue_order")
   273→            or plan.get("skipped")
   274→            or plan.get("clusters")
   275→        )
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   279→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   280→    lines.extend(_summary_lines(stats))
   281→
   282→    if has_plan:
   283→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   284→        queue = build_work_queue(
   285→            state,
   286→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   287→                count=None,
   288→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   289→                status="open",
   290→                include_subjective=True,
   291→            ),
   292→        )
   293→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   294→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   295→
   296→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   297→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   298→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   299→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   300→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   301→        if remaining:
   302→            lines.append("---")
   303→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   304→            lines.append("")
   305→
   306→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(findings, state=state))
   307→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   308→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   309→    else:
   310→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(findings, state=state))
   311→
   312→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   313→
   314→    return "\n".join(lines)
   315→
```

```
    38→def _scorecard_subjective(
    39→    state: dict,
    40→    dim_scores: dict,
    41→) -> list[dict]:
    42→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    43→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    dim_scores: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    52→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    53→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    56→            continue
    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    58→        if strict_val < threshold:
    59→            low.append(
    60→                (
    61→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low
    68→
    69→
    70→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    72→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    73→    state = runtime.state
    74→    config = runtime.config
    75→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    79→    if skill_warning:
    80→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    81→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    82→    if config_warning:
    83→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    84→
    85→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(state=state)
    86→    _get_items(args, state, config)

    88→
    89→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    90→    plan_data: dict | None,
    91→    *,
    92→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    93→    scope: str | None,
    94→) -> str | None:
    95→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    96→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    97→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    98→        if active_cluster:
    99→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   100→    return effective_cluster

   106→    items: list[dict],
   107→    state: dict,

   109→    plan_data: dict | None,
   110→) -> dict:
   111→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   112→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   113→    )
   114→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   115→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   116→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   117→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   118→        state,
   119→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   120→    )
   121→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   122→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   123→    ]
   124→    return payload

   127→def _emit_requested_output(

   133→    if output_file:

   135→            output_file,
   136→            payload,

   139→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   140→        ):

   142→        raise SystemExit(1)
   143→
   144→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   145→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   146→        return True
```

---

## Complete Analysis: Work Queue Ranking and Ordering Systems

### Overview of the Pipeline

The work queue is built in `build_work_queue()` at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` (lines 110-215). The ordering pipeline has **five sequential stages**, each of which can reorder items:

1. **`item_sort_key` sort** (ranking.py) -- initial algorithmic sort
2. **`apply_plan_order`** (plan_order.py) -- living plan reorder 
3. **`collapse_clusters`** (plan_order.py) -- cluster collapse + re-sort
4. **`_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail`** (core.py) -- interleave subjective/objective
5. **Truncation** -- `items[:count]`

---

### Stage 1: `item_sort_key` -- The Base Ranking Algorithm

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`, lines 81-127

This is the foundational sort. It produces a tuple with the first element being a "tier" that establishes priority bands:

```python
def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")

    # Tier -3: Initial-review subjective items (highest priority)
    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))

    # Tier -2: Synthesis stages (observe/reflect/organize/commit)
    if kind == "workflow_stage":
        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))

    # Tier -1: Workflow actions (e.g., create-plan)
    if kind == "workflow_action":
        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))

    # Tier 0: Clusters (collapsed meta-items)
    if kind == "cluster":
        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3)
        return (0, action_pri, -int(item.get("member_count", 0)), item.get("id", ""))

    # Tier 1, sub-tier 1: Subjective findings (sort AFTER mechanical)
    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
        return (1, 1, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))

    # Tier 1, sub-tier 0: Mechanical findings (sort BEFORE subjective)
    detail = detail_dict(item)
    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    return (
        1,
        0,                                                          # mechanical before subjective
        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),     # high=0, medium=1, low=2
        -review_weight,                                             # higher weight first
        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),                         # higher count first
        item.get("id", ""),                                         # stable tiebreak
    )
```

**Priority bands from highest to lowest:**

| Tier | Kind | Sort key elements |
|------|------|-------------------|
| -3 | Initial-review subjective (unassessed dimensions) | score ascending, id |
| -2 | Synthesis workflow stages | blocked_penalty asc, stage_index asc, id |
| -1 | Workflow actions (create-plan) | id |
| 0 | Collapsed clusters | action_type_priority asc, member_count desc, id |
| 1,0 | Mechanical findings | confidence_rank asc, review_weight desc, count desc, id |
| 1,1 | Subjective findings/dimensions | subjective_score asc, id |

**`ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY`** from helpers.py line 20:
```python
ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
```

**`CONFIDENCE_ORDER`** from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/common.py`:
```python
CONFIDENCE_ORDER = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
```

**`subjective_score_value()`** (ranking.py lines 22-26): Returns the strict score for a subjective dimension, with lower scores sorting first (worst dimensions get priority).

---

### Stage 2: `apply_plan_order` -- Living Plan Reorder

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`, lines 24-103

This is where the plan's explicit `queue_order` list overrides the algorithmic sort. The logic:

```python
def apply_plan_order(items, plan, *, include_skipped=False, cluster=None, new_ids=None):
    queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    skipped_map = plan.get("skipped", {})
    skipped_ids = set(skipped_map.keys())
    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")

    # Build ID lookup
    by_id = {item["id"]: item for item in items}

    # Attach plan metadata (description, note, cluster info) to items
    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
        if override.get("description"):
            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
        # ... cluster attachment ...

    # PHASE 1: Items in queue_order (excluding skipped) -- in plan order
    ordered = []
    for finding_id in queue_order:
        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])

    # PHASE 2: Remaining items NOT in queue_order, split into existing vs new
    remaining_existing = []
    remaining_new = []
    for item in items:
        if item_id in skipped_ids:
            skipped_items.append(item)
        elif item_id in _new:
            remaining_new.append(item)
        else:
            remaining_existing.append(item)

    # FINAL ORDER: plan-ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new [+ skipped]
    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    if include_skipped:
        result = result + skipped_items
```

**Key observations:**

1. Items in `queue_order` maintain their explicit position -- this COMPLETELY overrides `item_sort_key` for those items.
2. Items NOT in `queue_order` appear after plan-ordered items, in their original `item_sort_key` order.
3. **New items** (from most recent scan) sort AFTER existing unordered items. This is determined by `new_item_ids()` which checks `scan_history[-1].timestamp`.
4. Skipped items are excluded unless `include_skipped=True`.
5. Cluster filtering: if `active_cluster` or `cluster` arg is set, result is filtered to only cluster member IDs (line 98-102).

**`promoted_ids` interaction:** The plan tracks IDs the user explicitly moved to the top. These are used by the stale_dimensions sync (line 177-190 of stale_dimensions.py) to insert new unscored subjective dimensions AFTER promoted items rather than at position 0. This prevents auto-injected items from leapfrogging user-promoted work.

---

### Stage 3: `collapse_clusters` -- Cluster Collapse + Re-sort

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`, lines 119-191

Only runs when:
- `collapse_clusters` option is True (default)
- Plan exists
- No cluster filter is active
- No `active_cluster` is set

```python
def collapse_clusters(items, plan):
    # Only collapse AUTO clusters (cluster.auto == True)
    fid_to_cluster = {fid: name for name, cluster in clusters.items() 
                      if cluster.get("auto") for fid in cluster.finding_ids}
    
    # Separate cluster members from non-cluster items
    # Replace 2+ member clusters with a single meta-item (kind="cluster")
    # Non-cluster items are kept as-is
    
    result = non_cluster_items + [meta_items...]
    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)  # RE-SORT everything!
```

**CRITICAL: This re-sorts the entire list using `item_sort_key`!** After `apply_plan_order` carefully placed items in plan order, `collapse_clusters` calls `result.sort(key=item_sort_key)` (line 189). This means:

- **Cluster meta-items** get sorted by tier 0 (between workflow items and findings).
- **Non-cluster items** that were in plan-order lose their plan-based ordering and revert to algorithmic sort.

**This is a potential conflict.** The plan order is partially lost when cluster collapse happens. However, the conditions for collapse are fairly narrow (auto-clusters only, no active focus, no cluster filter).

---

### Stage 4: `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail` -- Interleave

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`, lines 66-107

Only runs when:
- `include_subjective` is True
- Status is "open" or "all"
- Not chronic mode
- No scope filter
- No cluster filter

```python
def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(items, *, objective_burst=3, subjective_burst=1):
    # Separate into: workflow_items, subjective_items, objective_items
    # If either subjective or objective pool is empty, return as-is
    
    # Interleave: 3 objective, 1 subjective, 3 objective, 1 subjective, ...
    interleaved = workflow_items + [interleaved mix] + [remainder]
```

**Effect:** After all ordering is done, this takes the sorted list and interleaves subjective items at a 3:1 ratio with objective items. Workflow items (synthesis stages, create-plan) are prepended at the front untouched.

**This further disrupts plan ordering** for subjective items that were explicitly positioned. A subjective item at position 2 in the plan could end up at position 4+ after interleaving.

---

### The Scoring System (Separate from Queue Ordering)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py`

The scoring weight fractions are:

```python
SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60   # 60% of overall score
MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.40   # 40% of overall score
```

**Mechanical dimension weights** (within the 40% pool):
```python
MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS = {
    "file health": 2.0,
    "code quality": 1.0,
    "duplication": 1.0,
    "test health": 1.0,
    "security": 1.0,
}
```

**Subjective dimension weights** (within the 60% pool):
```python
SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS = {
    "high elegance": 22.0,
    "mid elegance": 22.0,
    "low elegance": 12.0,
    "contracts": 12.0,
    "type safety": 12.0,
    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
    "logic clarity": 6.0,
    "structure nav": 5.0,
    "error consistency": 3.0,
    "naming quality": 2.0,
    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
    "design coherence": 10.0,
}
```

**Health score computation** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/health.py`):
- Each mechanical dimension gets weight = `configured_weight * min(1.0, checks/MIN_SAMPLE)` where `MIN_SAMPLE=200`.
- Each subjective dimension gets weight = configured_weight (no sample dampening).
- If both pools exist: `overall = mech_avg * 0.40 + subj_avg * 0.60`
- If only mechanical: `overall = mech_avg` (fraction = 1.0)
- If only subjective: `overall = subj_avg` (fraction = 1.0)

**Important:** These scoring weights are for the OVERALL HEALTH SCORE computation, NOT for queue ordering. The queue ordering uses a completely different system (`item_sort_key`). There is a conceptual mismatch: subjective dimensions are 60% of the health score but are deprioritized in the queue (tier 1, sub-tier 1 -- after mechanical findings).

---

### Plan Queue Population (How items get into queue_order)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`

The plan's `queue_order` is populated through several mechanisms:

1. **Unscored dimensions** (`sync_unscored_dimensions`): Prepended to the FRONT of `queue_order`, after any `promoted_ids`. These correspond to the tier -3 initial-review items.

2. **Stale dimensions** (`sync_stale_dimensions`): Appended to the BACK of `queue_order`, but only when no objective items remain in the queue.

3. **Under-target dimensions** (`_current_under_target_ids`, used in `auto_cluster`): Appended to the back of `queue_order` if not already present.

4. **Synthesis stages** (`sync_synthesis_needed`): Inserted at front (after promoted_ids) when review finding hash changes. These are `synthesis::observe`, `synthesis::reflect`, `synthesis::organize`, `synthesis::commit`.

5. **Create-plan workflow** (`sync_create_plan_needed`): Inserted after subjective/synthesis items when conditions are met.

6. **Manual moves** (`move_items` in operations.py): User can explicitly position items via `plan move`.

7. **Review import sync** (`sync_plan_after_review_import` in reconcile.py): New review finding IDs appended to end of queue_order.

8. **Reconciliation** (`reconcile_plan_after_scan`): Dead findings are moved to `superseded` and removed from queue_order.

---

### Queue Policy Entry Points

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/queue_policy.py`

The `build_open_plan_queue()` function provides a simplified entry point for the plan command. It uses `_subjective_threshold()` which reads from `config.target_strict_score` (default 95.0).

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/select.py`

The `get_next_items()` function is the legacy plan API. It explicitly sets `include_subjective=False` and only returns items with `kind == "finding"`. This means the legacy API completely ignores subjective items, synthesis stages, and workflow actions.

---

### Auto-Clustering

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`

Auto-clustering groups findings by:
- `auto::detector` for pure auto-fix detectors
- `typed::detector::subtype` for judgment detectors with subtypes  
- `file::detector::basename` for structural/responsibility_cohesion
- `review::dimension` for review findings
- `detector::detector` as fallback

Minimum cluster size is 2 (except unscored dimensions which have minimum 1). Clusters get `action_type` from registry metadata, which feeds into `ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY` for cluster sorting.

---

### Identified Conflicts and Tensions

**1. collapse_clusters re-sorts and destroys plan order**

In `plan_order.py` line 189, `collapse_clusters` calls `result.sort(key=item_sort_key)` after items have been carefully placed in plan order. This means:
- Non-cluster items lose their plan-based position
- The re-sort only matters when auto-clusters are present and no cluster focus is set

**2. Subjective interleaving disrupts plan order**

The 3:1 interleave guardrail runs AFTER plan ordering. A subjective item that the user moved to position 1 via `plan move` could be pushed back by the interleave. However, this only applies if `initial_review` subjective items aren't in tier -3 (which would put them in workflow_items, exempted from interleaving). Non-initial subjective items at tier 1 sub-tier 1 would be affected.

**3. Scoring weights vs. queue priority mismatch**

Subjective dimensions are 60% of the health score but sort AFTER mechanical items in the queue (tier 1, sub-tier 1 vs tier 1, sub-tier 0). The interleave guardrail (3 objective : 1 subjective) partially compensates but still weights mechanical items 3x in the queue.

**4. `new_item_ids` can push high-priority findings to the back**

In `apply_plan_order`, items from the most recent scan that aren't in `queue_order` are sorted after existing items, regardless of their `item_sort_key` ranking. A high-confidence new finding would appear after low-confidence existing findings.

**5. Legacy API ignores all non-finding items**

`get_next_items()` in select.py filters to `kind == "finding"` only, completely ignoring synthesis stages, workflow actions, subjective dimensions, and clusters. Any consumer using this API gets a fundamentally different ordering than `build_work_queue()`.

**6. Synthesis stage dependency ordering is fragile**

Synthesis stages sort by `stage_index` (0-3 for observe/reflect/organize/commit) with a `blocked_penalty`. But the blocked_by computation (helpers.py lines 212-217) only checks whether dependency stages are "present and not confirmed," not whether they've been properly completed with valid output.

**7. Backlog-gated subjective items can silently disappear**

In core.py lines 152-156, subjective items marked `backlog_gated` are silently dropped when `has_objective_backlog` is True. The `suppressed_count` is tracked but these items vanish from the queue without explanation to the user.

**8. `review_finding_weight` 10x holistic multiplier**

In helpers.py lines 50-61, holistic review findings get a 10x weight multiplier (`weight *= 10.0`). This is the same `HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER = 10.0` from scoring policy. In the sort key, this puts holistic reviews far ahead of non-holistic ones via `-review_weight`.

---

### Complete Data Flow Diagram

```
build_work_queue(state, options)
  |
  +--> build_finding_items(state)     # mechanical + subjective findings
  |      Filter by: status, scope, chronic, suppressed
  |      Enrich with: is_review, is_subjective, review_weight, subjective_score
  |
  +--> build_subjective_items(state)  # synthetic subjective dimension items
  |      Filter by: threshold, scope, backlog_gated
  |      Source: scorecard_subjective_entries + unassessed placeholders
  |
  +--> build_synthesis_stage_items()  # synthesis::observe/reflect/organize/commit
  +--> build_create_plan_item()       # workflow::create-plan
  |
  +--> all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)    # STAGE 1: algorithmic sort
  |
  +--> _apply_plan_order(all_items, plan)   # STAGE 2: plan reorder
  |      Explicit queue_order overrides sort
  |      Skipped items excluded
  |      New items sorted last
  |
  +--> _collapse_clusters(items, plan)      # STAGE 3: collapse + RE-SORT
  |      Auto-clusters become meta-items
  |      result.sort(key=item_sort_key)  <-- DESTROYS plan order
  |
  +--> _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(items)  # STAGE 4: 3:1 interleave
  |      Workflow items prepended
  |      3 objective, 1 subjective pattern
  |
  +--> items[:count]                        # STAGE 5: truncation
```

```
1→"""Health score aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     6→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     7→    MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
     8→    MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
     9→    MIN_SAMPLE,
    10→    SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
    16→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
    17→
    18→
    19→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
    20→    return float(
    21→        MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
    22→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
    23→            1.0,
    24→        )
    25→    )
    26→
    27→
    28→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
    29→    subjective_meta = (
    30→        data.get("detectors", {}).get("subjective_assessment", {})
    31→        if isinstance(data, dict)
    32→        else {}
    33→    )
    34→    configured = (
    35→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")
    36→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)
    37→        else None
    38→    )
    39→    if is_numeric(configured):
    40→        return max(0.0, float(configured))
    41→
    42→    return float(
    43→        SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
    44→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
    45→            1.0,
    46→        )
    47→    )
    48→
    49→
    50→def compute_health_breakdown(
    51→    dimension_scores: dict,
    52→    *,
    53→    score_key: str = "score",
    54→) -> dict[str, object]:
    55→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
    56→    if not dimension_scores:
    57→        return {
    58→            "overall_score": 100.0,
    59→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
    60→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
    61→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
    62→            "subjective_avg": None,
    63→            "entries": [],
    64→        }
    65→
    66→    mech_sum = 0.0
    67→    mech_weight = 0.0
    68→    subj_sum = 0.0
    69→    subj_weight = 0.0
    70→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    71→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    72→
    73→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
    74→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
    75→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
    76→        if is_subjective:
    77→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
    78→            effective = configured
    79→            subj_sum += score * effective
    80→            subj_weight += effective
    81→            subjective_rows.append(
    82→                {
    83→                    "name": str(name),
    84→                    "score": score,
    85→                    "configured_weight": configured,
    86→                    "effective_weight": effective,
    87→                }
    88→            )
    89→            continue
    90→
    91→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
    92→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
    93→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
    94→        effective = configured * sample_factor
    95→        mech_sum += score * effective
    96→        mech_weight += effective
    97→        mechanical_rows.append(
    98→            {
    99→                "name": str(name),
   100→                "score": score,
   101→                "checks": checks,
   102→                "sample_factor": sample_factor,
   103→                "configured_weight": configured,
   104→                "effective_weight": effective,
   105→            }
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   109→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   110→
   111→    if subj_avg is None:
   112→        mechanical_fraction = 1.0
   113→        subjective_fraction = 0.0
   114→        overall_score = round(mech_avg, 1)
   115→    elif mech_weight == 0:
   116→        mechanical_fraction = 0.0
   117→        subjective_fraction = 1.0
   118→        overall_score = round(subj_avg, 1)
   119→    else:
   120→        mechanical_fraction = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   121→        subjective_fraction = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   122→        overall_score = round(
   123→            mech_avg * mechanical_fraction + subj_avg * subjective_fraction,
   124→            1,
   125→        )
   126→
   127→    entries: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   128→    for row in mechanical_rows:
   129→        pool_share = (
   130→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / mech_weight if mech_weight > 0 else 0.0
   131→        )
   132→        per_point = mechanical_fraction * pool_share
   133→        score = float(row["score"])
   134→        entries.append(
   135→            {
   136→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   137→                "pool": "mechanical",
   138→                "score": score,
   139→                "checks": float(row["checks"]),
   140→                "sample_factor": float(row["sample_factor"]),
   141→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   142→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   143→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   144→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   145→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   146→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   147→            }
   148→        )
   149→
   150→    for row in subjective_rows:
   151→        pool_share = (
   152→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / subj_weight if subj_weight > 0 else 0.0
   153→        )
   154→        per_point = subjective_fraction * pool_share
   155→        score = float(row["score"])
   156→        entries.append(
   157→            {
   158→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   159→                "pool": "subjective",
   160→                "score": score,
   161→                "checks": 0.0,
   162→                "sample_factor": 1.0,
   163→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   164→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   165→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   166→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   167→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   168→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   169→            }
   170→        )
   171→
   172→    return {
   173→        "overall_score": overall_score,
   174→        "mechanical_fraction": mechanical_fraction,
   175→        "subjective_fraction": subjective_fraction,
   176→        "mechanical_avg": mech_avg,
   177→        "subjective_avg": subj_avg,
   178→        "entries": entries,
   179→    }
   180→
   181→
   182→def compute_health_score(
   183→    dimension_scores: dict,
   184→    *,
   185→    score_key: str = "score",
   186→) -> float:
   187→    """Budget-weighted blend of mechanical and subjective dimension scores."""
   188→    return float(
   189→        compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key=score_key)[
   190→            "overall_score"
   191→        ]
   192→    )
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = ["compute_health_breakdown", "compute_health_score"]
   196→
   197→
```

```
1→"""Dimension and overall scoring aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import detector_stats_by_mode
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     9→    DIMENSIONS,
    10→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    11→    SCORING_MODES,
    12→    ScoreMode,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import (
    15→    compute_health_breakdown,
    16→    compute_health_score,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import (
    19→    compute_score_impact,
    20→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import (
    23→    append_subjective_dimensions,
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class ScoreBundle:
    29→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    30→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    overall_score: float
    33→    objective_score: float
    34→    strict_score: float
    35→    verified_strict_score: float
    36→
    37→
    38→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    39→    findings: dict,
    40→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    41→    *,
    42→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    43→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    44→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    45→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    46→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    47→
    48→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    49→        totals = {
    50→            mode: {
    51→                "checks": 0,
    52→                "issues": 0,
    53→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    54→                "detectors": {},
    55→            }
    56→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    57→        }
    58→
    59→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    60→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    61→            if potential <= 0:
    62→                continue
    63→
    64→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    65→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    66→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    67→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    68→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    69→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    70→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    71→                    "potential": potential,
    72→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    73→                    "issues": issues,
    74→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    75→                }
    76→
    77→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    78→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    79→            if total_checks <= 0:
    80→                continue
    81→            dim_score = (
    82→                max(
    83→                    0.0,
    84→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    85→                )
    86→                * 100
    87→            )
    88→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    89→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    90→                "tier": dim.tier,
    91→                "checks": total_checks,
    92→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    93→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    94→            }
    95→
    96→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    97→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    98→            results[mode],
    99→            findings,
   100→            subjective_assessments,
   101→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   102→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   103→        )
   104→    return results
   105→
   106→
   107→def compute_dimension_scores(
   108→    findings: dict,
   109→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   110→    *,
   111→    strict: bool = False,
   112→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   113→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   114→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   115→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   116→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   117→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   118→        findings,
   119→        potentials,
   120→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   121→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   122→    )[mode]
   123→
   124→
   125→def compute_score_bundle(
   126→    findings: dict,
   127→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   128→    *,
   129→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   130→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   131→) -> ScoreBundle:
   132→    """Compute all score channels from one scoring engine pass."""
   133→    by_mode = compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   134→        findings,
   135→        potentials,
   136→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   137→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    lenient_scores = by_mode["lenient"]
   141→    strict_scores = by_mode["strict"]
   142→    verified_strict_scores = by_mode["verified_strict"]
   143→
   144→    mechanical_lenient_scores = {
   145→        name: data
   146→        for name, data in lenient_scores.items()
   147→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   148→    }
   149→
   150→    return ScoreBundle(
   151→        dimension_scores=lenient_scores,
   152→        strict_dimension_scores=strict_scores,
   153→        verified_strict_dimension_scores=verified_strict_scores,
   154→        overall_score=compute_health_score(lenient_scores),
   155→        objective_score=compute_health_score(mechanical_lenient_scores),
   156→        strict_score=compute_health_score(strict_scores),
   157→        verified_strict_score=compute_health_score(verified_strict_scores),
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→__all__ = [
   162→    "ScoreBundle",
   163→    "compute_dimension_scores_by_mode",
   164→    "compute_dimension_scores",
   165→    "compute_health_breakdown",
   166→    "compute_health_score",
   167→    "compute_score_bundle",
   168→    "compute_score_impact",
   169→    "get_dimension_for_detector",
   170→]
   171→
```

```
1→"""Detector-to-score impact helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     6→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES,
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
     9→    Dimension,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_score
    12→
    13→
    14→def compute_score_impact(
    15→    dimension_scores: dict,
    16→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    17→    detector: str,
    18→    issues_to_fix: int,
    19→) -> float:
    20→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
    21→    target_dim = None
    22→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    23→        if detector in dim.detectors:
    24→            target_dim = dim
    25→            break
    26→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
    27→        return 0.0
    28→
    29→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    30→    if potential <= 0:
    31→        return 0.0
    32→
    33→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
    34→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
    35→
    36→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
    37→    if not det_data:
    38→        return 0.0
    39→
    40→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
    41→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
    42→
    43→    total_potential = 0
    44→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
    45→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
    46→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
    47→        if not det_values:
    48→            continue
    49→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
    50→        if det == detector:
    51→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
    52→        else:
    53→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
    54→    if total_potential <= 0:
    55→        return 0.0
    56→
    57→    new_dim_score = (
    58→        max(
    59→            0.0,
    60→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
    61→        )
    62→        * 100
    63→    )
    64→
    65→    simulated = {name: dict(data) for name, data in dimension_scores.items()}
    66→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
    67→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
    68→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
    69→
    70→
    71→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
    72→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
    73→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
    74→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
    75→        return None
    76→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
    77→
    78→
    79→__all__ = ["compute_score_impact", "get_dimension_for_detector"]
    80→
    81→
```

```
1→"""State scoring, statistics, and suppression accounting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from copy import deepcopy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "suppression_metrics",
     9→]
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
    12→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
    13→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import matches_target_score
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_findings
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    16→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import ScanCoverageRecord
    17→
    18→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = tuple(sorted(finding_status_tokens()))
    19→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2
    20→
    21→
    22→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    23→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    24→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    25→    if isinstance(history, list):
    26→        for entry in reversed(history):
    27→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    28→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    29→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    30→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    31→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    32→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    33→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    34→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    35→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    36→    return None
    37→
    38→
    39→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    40→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    41→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    42→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    43→
    44→    for finding in findings.values():
    45→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    46→            continue
    47→        status = finding["status"]
    48→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    49→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    50→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    51→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    52→
    53→    return counters, tier_stats
    54→
    55→
    56→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    57→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    58→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    59→    try:
    60→        score = float(raw)
    61→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    62→        score = 0.0
    63→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    64→
    65→
    66→def _subjective_target_matches(
    67→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    68→) -> list[str]:
    69→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    70→    matches = [
    71→        dimension
    72→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    73→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    74→    ]
    75→    return sorted(matches)
    76→
    77→
    78→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    79→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    80→    return {
    81→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    82→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    83→        "matched_count": 0,
    84→        "matched_dimensions": [],
    85→        "reset_dimensions": [],
    86→    }
    87→
    88→
    89→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    90→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    91→    *,
    92→    target: float,
    93→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    94→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""
    95→    normalized_target = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(target)))
    96→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
    97→        subjective_assessments,
    98→        target=normalized_target,
    99→    )
   100→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
   101→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
   102→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   103→
   104→    if len(matched_dimensions) < _SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD:
   105→        meta["status"] = "warn" if matched_dimensions else "pass"
   106→        return subjective_assessments, meta
   107→
   108→    adjusted = deepcopy(subjective_assessments)
   109→    for dimension in matched_dimensions:
   110→        payload = adjusted.get(dimension)
   111→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   112→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   113→            payload["integrity_penalty"] = "target_match_reset"
   114→        else:
   115→            adjusted[dimension] = {
   116→                "score": 0.0,
   117→                "integrity_penalty": "target_match_reset",
   118→            }
   119→
   120→    meta["status"] = "penalized"
   121→    meta["reset_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   122→    return adjusted, meta
   123→
   124→
   125→def _aggregate_scores(
   126→    dim_scores: dict, compute_health_score_fn
   127→) -> dict[str, float]:
   128→    """Derive the 4 aggregate scores from dimension-level data."""
   129→    mechanical = {
   130→        n: d
   131→        for n, d in dim_scores.items()
   132→        if "subjective_assessment" not in d.get("detectors", {})
   133→    }
   134→    return {
   135→        "overall_score": compute_health_score_fn(dim_scores),
   136→        "strict_score": compute_health_score_fn(
   137→            dim_scores, score_key="strict_score"
   138→        ),
   139→        "objective_score": compute_health_score_fn(mechanical),
   140→        "verified_strict_score": compute_health_score_fn(
   141→            mechanical, [REDACTED]
   142→        ),
   143→    }
   144→
   145→
   146→def _active_scan_coverage(state: StateModel) -> ScanCoverageRecord:
   147→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
   148→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict) or not scan_coverage:
   149→        return {}
   150→
   151→    lang_name = state.get("lang")
   152→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
   153→        payload = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
   154→        return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
   155→
   156→    if len(scan_coverage) == 1:
   157→        only = next(iter(scan_coverage.values()))
   158→        return only if isinstance(only, dict) else {}
   159→    return {}
   160→
   161→
   162→def _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   163→    state: StateModel,
   164→    *,
   165→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict],
   166→) -> None:
   167→    coverage_payload = _active_scan_coverage(state)
   168→    detectors_payload = coverage_payload.get("detectors", {})
   169→    if not isinstance(detectors_payload, dict):
   170→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   171→            "status": "full",
   172→            "confidence": 1.0,
   173→            "detectors": [],
   174→            "dimensions": [],
   175→        }
   176→        return
   177→
   178→    reduced_detectors: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   179→    for detector, raw in detectors_payload.items():
   180→        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
   181→            continue
   182→        status = str(raw.get("status", "full")).lower()
   183→        confidence = coerce_confidence(raw.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   184→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
   185→            continue
   186→        reduced_detectors[str(detector)] = dict(raw)
   187→
   188→    reduced_dimensions: list[str] = []
   189→    score_confidence_detectors: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   190→    for detector, payload in reduced_detectors.items():
   191→        score_confidence_detectors.append(
   192→            {
   193→                "detector": detector,
   194→                "status": str(payload.get("status", "reduced")),
   195→                "confidence": round(
   196→                    coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0), 2
   197→                ),
   198→                "summary": str(payload.get("summary", "") or ""),
   199→                "impact": str(payload.get("impact", "") or ""),
   200→                "remediation": str(payload.get("remediation", "") or ""),
   201→                "tool": str(payload.get("tool", "") or ""),
   202→                "reason": str(payload.get("reason", "") or ""),
   203→            }
   204→        )
   205→
   206→    for dim_name, dim_data in dimension_scores.items():
   207→        if not isinstance(dim_data, dict):
   208→            continue
   209→        detectors = dim_data.get("detectors", {})
   210→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   211→            continue
   212→
   213→        impacts: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   214→        for detector_name, detector_meta in detectors.items():
   215→            reduced = reduced_detectors.get(str(detector_name))
   216→            if not isinstance(detector_meta, dict):
   217→                continue
   218→            if reduced is None:
   219→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_status", None)
   220→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   221→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_summary", None)
   222→                continue
   223→            confidence = coerce_confidence(reduced.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   224→            status = str(reduced.get("status", "reduced"))
   225→            summary = str(reduced.get("summary", "") or "")
   226→            detector_meta["coverage_status"] = status
   227→            detector_meta["coverage_confidence"] = round(confidence, 2)
   228→            detector_meta["coverage_summary"] = summary
   229→            impacts.append(
   230→                {
   231→                    "detector": str(detector_name),
   232→                    "status": status,
   233→                    "confidence": round(confidence, 2),
   234→                    "summary": summary,
   235→                }
   236→            )
   237→
   238→        if not impacts:
   239→            dim_data.pop("coverage_status", None)
   240→            dim_data.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   241→            dim_data.pop("coverage_impacts", None)
   242→            continue
   243→
   244→        reduced_dimensions.append(str(dim_name))
   245→        dim_data["coverage_status"] = "reduced"
   246→        dim_data["coverage_confidence"] = round(
   247→            min(coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0) for item in impacts),
   248→            2,
   249→        )
   250→        dim_data["coverage_impacts"] = impacts
   251→
   252→    if not score_confidence_detectors:
   253→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   254→            "status": "full",
   255→            "confidence": 1.0,
   256→            "detectors": [],
   257→            "dimensions": [],
   258→        }
   259→        return
   260→
   261→    state["score_confidence"] = {
   262→        "status": "reduced",
   263→        "confidence": round(
   264→            min(
   265→                coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   266→                for item in score_confidence_detectors
   267→            ),
   268→            2,
   269→        ),
   270→        "detectors": score_confidence_detectors,
   271→        "dimensions": sorted(set(reduced_dimensions)),
   272→    }
   273→
   274→
   275→def _update_objective_health(
   276→    state: StateModel,
   277→    findings: dict,
   278→    *,
   279→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   280→) -> None:
   281→    """Compute canonical score trio from dimension scoring."""
   282→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   283→    if not pots:
   284→        return
   285→
   286→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with desloppify.scoring
   287→    # (scoring -> _scoring/results/core -> _scoring/subjective/core -> review -> state -> _state/merge -> _state/scoring)
   288→    from desloppify.scoring import (
   289→        compute_health_score,
   290→        compute_score_bundle,
   291→        merge_potentials,
   292→    )
   293→
   294→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   295→    if not merged:
   296→        return
   297→
   298→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   299→    integrity_target = (
   300→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   301→        if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float)
   302→        else None
   303→    )
   304→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   305→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   306→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   307→            subjective_assessments,
   308→            target=integrity_target,
   309→        )
   310→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   311→
   312→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   313→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   314→        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   315→        state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   316→        state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   317→        state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   318→        state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   319→        return
   320→
   321→    # Use the full scorecard dimensions for 0-score placeholders so every
   322→    # dimension the review workflow covers gets a placeholder when unreviewed.
   323→    # Explicit assessments outside this set still count
   324→    # (append_subjective_dimensions handles that).
   325→    allowed_subjective: set[str] | None = None
   326→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   327→    if lang_name:
   328→        try:
   329→            from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import (
   330→                load_dimensions_for_lang,
   331→            )
   332→
   333→            dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   334→            if dims:
   335→                allowed_subjective = set(dims)
   336→        except (ImportError, AttributeError) as exc:
   337→            _ = exc
   338→
   339→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   340→        findings,
   341→        merged,
   342→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   343→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   344→    )
   345→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   346→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   347→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   348→
   349→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   350→
   351→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   352→        name: dict(
   353→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   354→            strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   355→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   356→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   357→            issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
   358→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   359→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   360→        )
   361→        for name in lenient_scores
   362→    }
   363→    for data in state["dimension_scores"].values():
   364→        data["strict"] = data["strict_score"]
   365→
   366→    # Carry forward mechanical dimensions from a prior scan that are absent
   367→    # now (e.g. duplication when --skip-slow is used).
   368→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   369→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   370→            continue
   371→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   372→            continue
   373→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   374→            continue
   375→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   376→        carried.setdefault("score", 0.0)
   377→        carried.setdefault("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))
   378→        carried.setdefault("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0)))
   379→        # Backfill for state files written before verified_strict_score existed.
   380→        carried.setdefault(
   381→            "verified_strict_score",
   382→            carried.get("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))),
   383→        )
   384→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   385→
   386→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   387→        state,
   388→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   389→    )
   390→
   391→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
   392→
   393→
   394→def _recompute_stats(
   395→    state: StateModel,
   396→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   397→    *,
   398→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   399→) -> None:
   400→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from findings."""
   401→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   402→    findings = path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], scan_path)
   403→    counters, tier_stats = _count_findings(findings)
   404→    state["stats"] = {
   405→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   406→        **counters,
   407→        "by_tier": {
   408→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   409→        },
   410→    }
   411→    _update_objective_health(
   412→        state,
   413→        findings,
   414→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   415→    )
   416→
   417→
   418→def _empty_suppression_metrics() -> dict[str, int | float]:
   419→    return {
   420→        "last_ignored": 0,
   421→        "last_raw_findings": 0,
   422→        "last_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   423→        "last_ignore_patterns": 0,
   424→        "recent_scans": 0,
   425→        "recent_ignored": 0,
   426→        "recent_raw_findings": 0,
   427→        "recent_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   428→    }
   429→
   430→
   431→def suppression_metrics(state: StateModel, *, window: int = 5) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   432→    """Summarize ignore suppression from recent scan history."""
   433→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   434→    if not history:
   435→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   436→
   437→    scans_with_suppression = [
   438→        entry
   439→        for entry in history
   440→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
   441→        and (
   442→            "ignored" in entry
   443→            or "raw_findings" in entry
   444→            or "suppressed_pct" in entry
   445→            or "ignore_patterns" in entry
   446→        )
   447→    ]
   448→    if not scans_with_suppression:
   449→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   450→
   451→    recent = scans_with_suppression[-max(1, window) :]
   452→    last = recent[-1]
   453→
   454→    recent_ignored = sum(int(entry.get("ignored", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   455→    recent_raw = sum(int(entry.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   456→    recent_pct = round(recent_ignored / recent_raw * 100, 1) if recent_raw else 0.0
   457→
   458→    last_ignored = int(last.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   459→    last_raw = int(last.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   460→    if "suppressed_pct" in last:
   461→        last_pct = round(float(last.get("suppressed_pct") or 0.0), 1)
   462→    else:
   463→        last_pct = round(last_ignored / last_raw * 100, 1) if last_raw else 0.0
   464→
   465→    return {
   466→        "last_ignored": last_ignored,
   467→        "last_raw_findings": last_raw,
   468→        "last_suppressed_pct": last_pct,
   469→        "last_ignore_patterns": int(last.get("ignore_patterns", 0) or 0),
   470→        "recent_scans": len(recent),
   471→        "recent_ignored": recent_ignored,
   472→        "recent_raw_findings": recent_raw,
   473→        "recent_suppressed_pct": recent_pct,
   474→    }
   475→
```
