   104→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:
   105→    """Derive dimensions from DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.
   106→
   107→    Each unique (dimension, tier) pair becomes a Dimension, with its detectors
   108→    collected automatically. Order follows first-seen in the policies dict.
   109→    """
   110→    # Collect (dimension_name -> tier) preserving first-seen order,
   111→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   112→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}
   113→    grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   114→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():
   115→        if policy.dimension is None or policy.tier is None:
   116→            continue
   117→        if policy.dimension not in dim_tiers:
   118→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   119→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   120→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   121→    return [
   122→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   123→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   124→    ]
   125→
   126→
   127→DIMENSIONS = _build_dimensions()
   128→DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME = {d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS}
   129→
   130→TIER_WEIGHTS = {
   131→    Tier.AUTO_FIX: 1,
   132→    Tier.QUICK_FIX: 2,
   133→    Tier.JUDGMENT: 3,
   134→    Tier.MAJOR_REFACTOR: 4,
   135→}
   136→CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {Confidence.HIGH: 1.0, Confidence.MEDIUM: 0.7, Confidence.LOW: 0.3}
   137→
   138→# Minimum checks for full dimension weight — below this, weight is dampened
   139→# proportionally. Prevents small-sample dimensions from swinging the overall score.
   140→MIN_SAMPLE = 200
   141→
   142→# Holistic review weight: findings with file="." and detail.holistic=True
   143→# get a 10x weight multiplier for display/priority purposes (issues list,
   144→# remediation engine).  NOT used in score computation — review findings are
   145→# excluded from the detection scoring pipeline (scored via assessments only).
   146→HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER = 10.0
   147→HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL = 10
   148→
   149→# Budget: subjective dimensions get this fraction of the overall score.
   150→# Mechanical dimensions get the remainder.
   151→SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60
   152→MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 1.0 - SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   153→
   154→# Per-dimension weighting within the mechanical pool.
   155→# Keep this balanced: no special boost for security/test in the pool itself.
   156→MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   157→    "file health": 2.0,
   158→    "code quality": 1.0,
   159→    "duplication": 1.0,
   160→    "test health": 1.0,
   161→    "security": 1.0,
   162→}
   163→
   164→# Per-dimension weighting within the subjective pool.
   165→# Emphasize elegance and contract/type coherence for a stronger architecture north star.
   166→SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   167→    "high elegance": 22.0,
   168→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
   169→    "low elegance": 12.0,
   170→    "contracts": 12.0,
   171→    "type safety": 12.0,
   172→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
   173→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
   174→    # Low-but-meaningful structural signal (about half of the subjective
   175→    # average weight) so it matters without dominating craftsmanship axes.
   176→    "structure nav": 5.0,
   177→    "error consistency": 3.0,
   178→    "naming quality": 2.0,
   179→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
   180→    "design coherence": 10.0,
   181→}
   182→
   183→# Synthetic check count for subjective dimensions in dimension_scores.
   184→SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS = 10
   185→
   186→FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
   187→    "lenient": frozenset({"open"}),
   188→    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix"}),
   189→    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive"}),
   190→}
   191→
   192→# Tolerance for treating a subjective score as "on target" in integrity checks.
   193→# Scores within this band of the target are flagged as potential gaming.
   194→SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE = 0.05
   195→
   196→
   197→def matches_target_score(
   198→    score: object,
   199→    target: object,
   200→    *,
   201→    tolerance: float = SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
   202→) -> bool:
   203→    """Return True when score is within tolerance of target."""
   204→    try:
   205→        score_value = float(score)
   206→        target_value = float(target)
   207→        tolerance_value = max(0.0, float(tolerance))
   208→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   209→        return False
   210→    return abs(score_value - target_value) <= tolerance_value
   211→
   212→
   213→def register_scoring_policy(policy: DetectorScoringPolicy) -> None:
   214→    """Register a scoring policy at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   215→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES[policy.detector] = policy
   216→    _rebuild_derived()
   217→
   218→
   219→def reset_registered_scoring_policies() -> None:
   220→    """Reset runtime-added scoring policies to built-in defaults."""
   221→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.clear()
   222→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.update(_BASE_DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES)
   223→    _rebuild_derived()
   224→
   225→
   226→def _rebuild_derived() -> None:
   227→    """Rebuild DIMENSIONS, DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME, FILE_BASED_DETECTORS from current state.
   228→
   229→    Mutates existing objects in-place so that all references (including imports
   230→    that bound the original objects) see the updates.
   231→    """
   232→    new_dims = _build_dimensions()
   233→    DIMENSIONS.clear()
   234→    DIMENSIONS.extend(new_dims)
   235→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.clear()
   236→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.update({d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS})
   237→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.clear()
   238→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.update(
   239→        det for det, pol in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items() if pol.file_based
   240→    )
   241→
   242→
   243→def detector_policy(detector: str) -> DetectorScoringPolicy:
   244→    """Get scoring policy for a detector, with a safe default fallback."""
   245→    return DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(
   246→        detector,
   247→        DetectorScoringPolicy(detector=detector, dimension=None, tier=None),
   248→    )
   249→
   250→
   251→__all__ = [
   252→    "CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS",
   253→    "DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES",
   254→    "DIMENSIONS",
   255→    "DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME",
   256→    "FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE",
   257→    "FILE_BASED_DETECTORS",
   258→    "HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER",
   259→    "HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL",

   261→    "MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION",

1→"""Per-detector scoring calculations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     8→    CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS,
     9→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    10→    HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER,
    11→    SCORING_MODES,
    12→    ScoreMode,
    13→    detector_policy,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import Finding
    16→
    17→# Tiered file-count cap thresholds for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    18→# Controls how many findings per file contribute to the weighted failure sum.
    19→_FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD = 6     # findings in file for high cap
    20→_FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD = 3      # findings in file for mid cap
    21→_FILE_CAP_HIGH = 2.0             # cap value at high concentration
    22→_FILE_CAP_MID = 1.5              # cap value at mid concentration
    23→_FILE_CAP_LOW = 1.0              # cap value at low concentration (1-2 findings)
    24→
    25→
    26→def merge_potentials(potentials_by_lang: dict[str, dict[str, int]]) -> dict[str, int]:
    27→    """Sum potentials across languages per detector."""
    28→    merged: dict[str, int] = {}
    29→    for lang_potentials in potentials_by_lang.values():
    30→        for detector, count in lang_potentials.items():
    31→            merged[detector] = merged.get(detector, 0) + count
    32→    return merged
    33→
    34→
    35→def _iter_scoring_candidates(
    36→    detector: str,
    37→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    38→    excluded_zones: frozenset[str],
    39→):
    40→    """Yield in-scope findings for a detector (zone-filtered)."""
    41→    for finding in findings.values():
    42→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    43→            continue
    44→        if finding.get("detector") != detector:
    45→            continue
    46→        if finding.get("zone", "production") in excluded_zones:
    47→            continue
    48→        yield finding
    49→
    50→
    51→def _finding_weight(finding: Finding, *, use_loc_weight: bool) -> float:
    52→    """Compute the scoring weight for a single finding."""
    53→    if use_loc_weight:
    54→        return finding.get("detail", {}).get("loc_weight", 1.0)
    55→    return CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS.get(finding.get("confidence", "medium"), 0.7)
    56→
    57→
    58→def _file_count_cap(findings_in_file: int) -> float:
    59→    """Tiered cap for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    60→
    61→    Keeps file-count denominator semantics while preserving concentration signal:
    62→    1-2 findings => _FILE_CAP_LOW, 3-5 => _FILE_CAP_MID, 6+ => _FILE_CAP_HIGH.
    63→    """
    64→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD:
    65→        return _FILE_CAP_HIGH
    66→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD:
    67→        return _FILE_CAP_MID
    68→    return _FILE_CAP_LOW
    69→
    70→
    71→@dataclass
    72→class _ModeAccum:
    73→    """Per-mode accumulator for file-based detector scoring."""
    74→
    75→    by_file: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    76→    by_file_count: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    77→    file_cap: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    78→    holistic_sum: float = 0.0
    79→    issue_count: int = 0
    80→
    81→
    82→def _file_based_failures_by_mode(
    83→    detector: str,
    84→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    85→    policy,
    86→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]]:
    87→    """Accumulate weighted failures by score mode for file-based detectors."""
    88→    accum: dict[ScoreMode, _ModeAccum] = {mode: _ModeAccum() for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    89→
    90→    for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones):
    91→        status = finding.get("status", "open")
    92→        holistic = finding.get("file") == "." and finding.get("detail", {}).get(
    93→            "holistic"
    94→        )
    95→
    96→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    97→            if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
    98→                continue
    99→
   100→            if holistic:
   101→                accum[mode].holistic_sum += (
   102→                    _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False) * HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
   103→                )
   104→                accum[mode].issue_count += 1
   105→                continue
   106→
   107→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=policy.use_loc_weight)
   108→            file_key = finding.get("file", "")
   109→            a = accum[mode]
   110→            a.by_file[file_key] = a.by_file.get(file_key, 0.0) + weight
   111→            a.by_file_count[file_key] = a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0) + 1
   112→            if policy.use_loc_weight and file_key not in a.file_cap:
   113→                a.file_cap[file_key] = weight
   114→            a.issue_count += 1
   115→
   116→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]] = {}
   117→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   118→        a = accum[mode]
   119→        if policy.use_loc_weight:
   120→            weighted = sum(
   121→                min(weighted_sum, a.file_cap.get(file_key, weighted_sum))
   122→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   123→            )
   124→        else:
   125→            weighted = sum(
   126→                min(weighted_sum, _file_count_cap(a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0)))
   127→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   128→            )
   129→        out[mode] = (a.issue_count, weighted + a.holistic_sum)
   130→    return out
   131→
   132→
   133→def detector_stats_by_mode(
   134→    detector: str,
   135→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   136→    potential: int,
   137→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]]:
   138→    """Compute (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures) for each score mode."""
   139→    if potential <= 0:
   140→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   141→
   142→    # Review and concern findings are scored via subjective assessments only —
   143→    # exclude them from the detection-side scoring pipeline so resolving these
   144→    # findings never changes the score directly.
   145→    if detector in ("review", "concerns"):
   146→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   147→
   148→    policy = detector_policy(detector)
   149→
   150→    if policy.file_based:
   151→        mode_failures = _file_based_failures_by_mode(detector, findings, policy)
   152→    else:
   153→        issue_count: dict[ScoreMode, int] = {mode: 0 for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   154→        weighted_failures: dict[ScoreMode, float] = {
   155→            mode: 0.0 for mode in SCORING_MODES
   156→        }
   157→
   158→        for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(
   159→            detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones
   160→        ):
   161→            status = finding.get("status", "open")
   162→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False)
   163→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   164→                if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
   165→                    continue
   166→                issue_count[mode] += 1
   167→                weighted_failures[mode] += weight
   168→
   169→        mode_failures = {
   170→            mode: (issue_count[mode], weighted_failures[mode]) for mode in SCORING_MODES
   171→        }
   172→
   173→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]] = {}
   174→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   175→        issues, weighted = mode_failures[mode]
   176→        pass_rate = max(0.0, (potential - weighted) / potential)
   177→        out[mode] = (pass_rate, issues, weighted)
   178→    return out
   179→
   180→
   181→def detector_pass_rate(
   182→    detector: str,
   183→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   184→    potential: int,
   185→    *,
   186→    strict: bool = False,
   187→) -> tuple[float, int, float]:
   188→    """Pass rate for one detector.
   189→
   190→    Returns (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures).
   191→    Zero potential -> (1.0, 0, 0.0).
   192→    """
   193→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   194→    return detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)[mode]
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = [
   198→    "detector_pass_rate",
   199→    "detector_stats_by_mode",
   200→    "merge_potentials",
   201→]
   202→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    subjective_strict_scores,
    16→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    19→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    20→
    21→
    22→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    23→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    24→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    25→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    26→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    27→
    28→
    29→def build_finding_items(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    *,
    32→    scan_path: str | None,
    33→    status_filter: str,
    34→    scope: str | None,
    35→    chronic: bool,
    36→) -> list[dict]:
    37→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    38→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    39→    out: list[dict] = []
    40→
    41→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    42→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    43→            continue
    44→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    45→            continue
    46→        if chronic and not (
    47→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    48→        ):
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        item = dict(finding)
    52→        item["id"] = finding_id
    53→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    54→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    55→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    56→        item["review_weight"] = (
    57→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    58→        )
    59→        subjective_score = None
    60→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    61→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    62→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    63→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    64→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    65→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    66→            )
    67→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    68→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    69→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    70→            item,
    71→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    72→        )
    73→
    74→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    75→            continue
    76→        out.append(item)
    77→
    78→    return out
    79→
    80→
    81→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    82→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    83→
    84→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
    86→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
    87→
    88→    # Synthesis stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
    89→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
    90→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
    91→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
    92→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
    93→
    94→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
    95→    if kind == "workflow_action":
    96→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    97→
    98→    if kind == "cluster":
    99→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   100→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   101→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   102→        )
   103→        return (
   104→            0,
   105→            action_pri,
   106→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   107→            item.get("id", ""),
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   111→        return (
   112→            1,
   113→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
   114→            subjective_score_value(item),
   115→            item.get("id", ""),
   116→        )
   117→
   118→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   119→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   120→    return (
   121→        1,
   122→        0,
   123→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   124→        -review_weight,
   125→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   126→        item.get("id", ""),
   127→    )
   128→
   129→
   130→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   131→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   132→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   133→        return {
   134→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   135→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   136→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   137→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   138→            "policy": "Synthesis stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   139→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   140→        }
   141→
   142→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   143→        return {
   144→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   145→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after synthesis stages, before findings.",
   146→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   147→        }
   148→
   149→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   150→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   151→        return {
   152→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   153→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   154→            "initial_review": initial,
   155→            "policy": (
   156→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   157→                if initial else
   158→                "Subjective dimensions sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   159→            ),
   160→            "ranking_factors": ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   161→        }
   162→
   163→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   164→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   165→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   166→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   167→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   168→    if is_subjective:
   169→        ranking_factors = ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   170→    elif is_review:
   171→        ranking_factors = [
   172→            "confidence asc",
   173→            "review_weight desc",
   174→            "count desc",
   175→            "id asc",
   176→        ]
   177→    else:
   178→        ranking_factors = ["confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   179→    explain = {
   180→        "kind": "finding",
   181→        "confidence": confidence,
   182→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   183→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   184→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   185→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   186→    }
   187→    if is_review:
   188→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   189→    if is_subjective:
   190→        explain["policy"] = (
   191→            "Subjective findings sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   192→        )
   193→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   194→    return explain
   195→
   196→
   197→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   198→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   199→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   200→    for item in items:
   201→        if group == "file":
   202→            key = item.get("file", "")
   203→        elif group == "detector":
   204→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   205→        elif group == "cluster":
   206→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   207→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   208→        else:
   209→            key = "items"
   210→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   211→    return grouped
   212→
   213→
   214→__all__ = [
   215→    "build_finding_items",
   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",
   218→    "subjective_score_value",
   219→    "group_queue_items",
   220→]
   221→

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    21→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    22→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    23→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    51→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    52→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    53→    weight_by_confidence = {
    54→        "high": 1.0,
    55→        "medium": 0.7,
    56→        "low": 0.3,
    57→    }
    58→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    59→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    60→        weight *= 10.0
    61→    return float(weight)
    62→
    63→
    64→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    65→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    66→    if not scope:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    70→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    71→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    72→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    73→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    74→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    75→
    76→    if "*" in scope:
    77→        return any(
    78→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    79→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    80→        )
    81→
    82→    if "::" in scope:
    83→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    84→
    85→    lowered = scope.lower()
    86→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        return (
    88→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    89→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    90→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    91→        )
    92→
    93→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    94→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    95→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    96→
    97→    return (
    98→        detector == scope
    99→        or filepath == scope
   100→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   105→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   106→
   107→
   108→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   109→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   110→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   111→    target = cleaned.lower()
   112→
   113→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   114→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   115→            return str(dim_key)
   116→    return slugify(cleaned)
   117→
   118→
   119→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   120→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   121→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   122→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   123→    return {
   124→        cleaned.lower(),
   125→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   126→        slugify(cleaned),
   127→        canonical.lower(),
   128→        slugify(canonical),
   129→    }
   130→
   131→
   132→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   133→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   134→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   135→    if not lang:
   136→        return None
   137→
   138→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   139→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   140→        return None
   141→
   142→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   143→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   144→        return None
   145→
   146→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   147→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   148→        return None
   149→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   150→
   151→
   152→def primary_command_for_finding(
   153→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   154→) -> str:
   155→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   156→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   157→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   158→        available_fixers = [
   159→            fixer
   160→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   161→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   162→        ]
   163→        if available_fixers:
   164→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   165→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   166→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   167→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   168→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   169→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   170→
   171→
   172→def build_synthesis_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``synthesis::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   174→
   175→    Returns an empty list when no synthesis stages are pending.
   176→    """
   177→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis_playbook import (
   178→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   179→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS,
   180→    )
   181→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   182→        SYNTHESIS_IDS,
   183→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS,
   184→    )
   185→
   186→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   187→    order_set = set(order)
   188→    present = order_set & SYNTHESIS_IDS
   189→    if not present:
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   193→    confirmed = set(meta.get("synthesis_stages", {}).keys())
   194→
   195→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   196→    open_review_count = sum(
   197→        1 for f in findings.values()
   198→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   199→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    label_map = dict(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS)
   203→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   204→
   205→    items: list[dict] = []
   206→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   207→        if sid not in present:
   208→            continue
   209→        if name in confirmed:
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   213→        deps = SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   214→        blocked_by = sorted(
   215→            f"synthesis::{dep}" for dep in deps
   216→            if f"synthesis::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   217→        )
   218→
   219→        cmd = f"desloppify plan synthesize --stage {name}"
   220→        if name == "commit":
   221→            cmd = 'desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."'
   222→
   223→        items.append({
   224→            "id": sid,
   225→            "tier": 1,
   226→            "confidence": "high",
   227→            "detector": "synthesis",
   228→            "file": ".",
   229→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   230→            "stage_name": name,
   231→            "stage_index": idx,
   232→            "summary": f"Planning: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   233→            "detail": {
   234→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   235→                "stage": name,
   236→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   237→            },
   238→            "primary_command": cmd,
   239→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   240→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   241→        })
   242→    return items
   243→
   244→
   245→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> dict | None:
   246→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if it's in the queue.
   247→
   248→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   249→    """
   250→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID
   251→
   252→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   253→        return None
   254→
   255→    return {
   256→        "id": WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   257→        "tier": 1,
   258→        "confidence": "high",
   259→        "detector": "workflow",
   260→        "file": ".",
   261→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   262→        "summary": "Create prioritized plan from review results",
   263→        "detail": {},
   264→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan",
   265→        "blocked_by": [],
   266→        "is_blocked": False,
   267→    }
   268→
   269→
   270→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   271→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   272→    if not dim_scores:
   273→        return {}
   274→
   275→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   276→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   277→    for entry in entries:
   278→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   279→        if not name:
   280→            continue
   281→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   282→        [REDACTED](name)
   283→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   284→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   285→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   286→            if not key:
   287→                continue
   288→            aliases.add(key)
   289→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   290→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   291→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   292→        for alias in aliases:
   293→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   294→    return scores
   295→
   296→
   297→def _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   298→    """Build scorecard-like entries for unassessed placeholder dimensions.
   299→
   300→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` goes through the scorecard pipeline which
   301→    intentionally hides placeholders from display.  This function reads
   302→    ``dimension_scores`` directly so initial-review items are never lost.
   303→    """
   304→    entries: list[dict] = []
   305→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   306→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   307→            continue
   308→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   309→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   310→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   311→            continue
   312→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   313→            continue
   314→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   315→        entries.append(
   316→            {
   317→                "name": name,
   318→                "score": float(data.get("score", 0.0)),
   319→                "strict": float(data.get("strict", 0.0)),
   320→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   321→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   322→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   323→                "placeholder": True,
   324→                "stale": False,
   325→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   326→                "cli_keys": [dim_key] if dim_key else [],
   327→            }
   328→        )
   329→    return entries
   330→
   331→
   332→def build_subjective_items(
   333→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   334→) -> list[dict]:
   335→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   336→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   337→    if not dim_scores:
   338→        return []
   339→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   340→
   341→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   342→
   343→    # The scorecard pipeline hides unassessed placeholders from display.
   344→    # Merge them in so initial-review items always appear in the queue.
   345→    seen_dims: set[str] = {
   346→        str(e.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   347→        for e in subjective_entries
   348→        if e.get("dimension_key")
   349→    }
   350→    for entry in _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores):
   351→        dk = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   352→        if dk and dk not in seen_dims:
   353→            subjective_entries.append(entry)
   354→            seen_dims.add(dk)
   355→
   356→    if not subjective_entries:
   357→        return []
   358→    unassessed_dims = {
   359→        str(name).strip()
   360→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   361→            dim_scores
   362→        )
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   366→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   367→    for finding in findings.values():
   368→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   369→            continue
   370→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   371→        if not dim_key:
   372→            continue
   373→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   374→
   375→    items: list[dict] = []
   376→    def _prepare_command(
   377→        cli_keys: list[str],
   378→        *,
   379→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   380→    ) -> str:
   381→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   382→        if cli_keys:
   383→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   384→        if force_review_rerun:
   385→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   386→        return command
   387→
   388→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   389→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   390→        if not name:
   391→            continue
   392→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   394→            continue
   395→
   396→        [REDACTED](name)
   397→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   398→        cli_keys = [
   399→            str(key).strip().lower()
   400→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   401→            if str(key).strip()
   402→        ]
   403→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   404→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   405→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   406→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   407→            name in unassessed_dims
   408→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   409→        )
   410→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   411→        # Items whose only action is --force-review-rerun are gated by the
   412→        # review preflight while objective backlog exists.  Flag them so the
   413→        # queue builder can suppress them until the backlog drains.
   414→        is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   415→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   416→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   417→        if open_review > 0:
   418→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   419→        elif is_unassessed:
   420→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   421→        elif is_stale:
   422→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   423→                cli_keys,
   424→                force_review_rerun=True,
   425→            )
   426→        elif cli_keys:
   427→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   428→                cli_keys,
   429→                force_review_rerun=True,
   430→            )
   431→        else:
   432→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   433→                cli_keys,
   434→                force_review_rerun=True,
   435→            )
   436→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   437→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   438→        items.append(
   439→            {
   440→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   441→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   442→                "file": ".",
   443→                "confidence": "medium",
   444→                "summary": summary,
   445→                "detail": {
   446→                    "dimension_name": name,
   447→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   448→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   449→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   450→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   451→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   452→                },
   453→                "status": "open",
   454→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   455→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   456→                "backlog_gated": is_backlog_gated,
   457→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   458→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   459→            }
   460→        )
   461→    return items
   462→
   463→
   464→__all__ = [
   465→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   466→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   467→    "build_create_plan_item",
   468→    "build_subjective_items",
   469→    "build_synthesis_stage_items",

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from collections import deque
     7→from typing import TypedDict
     8→
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    10→    ALL_STATUSES,
    11→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    12→    build_create_plan_item,
    13→    build_subjective_items,

    21→    item_sort_key,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    24→    apply_plan_order as _apply_plan_order,
    25→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    26→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    29→
    30→
    31→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    32→class QueueBuildOptions:
    33→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    34→
    35→    count: int | None = 1
    36→    scan_path: str | None = None
    37→    scope: str | None = None
    38→    status: str = "open"
    39→    include_subjective: bool = True
    40→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    41→    chronic: bool = False
    42→    explain: bool = False
    43→    plan: dict | None = None
    44→    include_skipped: bool = False
    45→    cluster: str | None = None
    46→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    47→
    48→
    49→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    50→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    51→
    52→    items: list[dict]
    53→    total: int
    54→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    55→    suppressed_count: int
    56→
    57→
    58→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    59→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    60→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    61→
    62→
    63→_WORKFLOW_KINDS = frozenset({"workflow_stage", "workflow_action"})
    64→
    65→
    66→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    67→    items: list[dict],
    68→    *,
    69→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    70→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    71→) -> list[dict]:
    72→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.
    73→
    74→    Workflow items (synthesis stages, create-plan) are excluded from the
    75→    interleave and prepended — they maintain their natural high-priority sort.
    76→    """
    77→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    78→        return items
    79→
    80→    workflow_items: list[dict] = []
    81→    subjective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    82→    objective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        if item.get("kind") in _WORKFLOW_KINDS:
    85→            workflow_items.append(item)
    86→        elif _is_subjective_queue_item(item):
    87→            subjective_items.append(item)
    88→        else:
    89→            objective_items.append(item)
    90→
    91→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    92→        return items
    93→
    94→    interleaved: list[dict] = list(workflow_items)
    95→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    96→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    97→            if not objective_items:
    98→                break
    99→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
   100→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
   101→            if not subjective_items:
   102→                break
   103→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
   104→
   105→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
   106→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
   107→    return interleaved
   108→
   109→
   110→def build_work_queue(
   111→    state: StateModel,
   112→    *,
   113→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   114→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   115→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   116→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   117→
   118→    status = resolved_options.status
   119→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   120→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   121→    try:
   122→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   123→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   124→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   125→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   126→
   127→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   128→        state,
   129→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   130→        status_filter=status,
   131→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   132→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   133→    )
   134→
   135→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   136→    has_objective_backlog = bool(finding_items)
   137→    suppressed_count = 0
   138→    if (
   139→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   140→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   141→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   142→    ):
   143→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   144→            state,
   145→            state.get("findings", {}),
   146→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   147→        )
   148→        for item in subjective_items:
   149→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   150→                continue
   151→            # Suppress subjective items whose only action is a review rerun
   152→            # (--force-review-rerun) while objective backlog exists — the
   153→            # review preflight blocks reruns until the backlog drains.
   154→            if has_objective_backlog and item.get("backlog_gated"):
   155→                suppressed_count += 1
   156→                continue
   157→            all_items.append(item)
   158→
   159→    # Inject synthesis stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   160→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   161→        synth_items = build_synthesis_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   162→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   163→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   164→        if plan_item is not None:
   165→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   166→
   167→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   168→
   169→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   170→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   171→    if resolved_options.plan:
   172→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   173→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   174→            all_items,
   175→            resolved_options.plan,
   176→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   177→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   178→            new_ids=new_ids,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   182→    should_collapse = (
   183→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   184→        and resolved_options.plan
   185→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   186→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   187→    )
   188→    if should_collapse:
   189→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   190→
   191→    should_interleave = (
   192→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   193→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   194→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   195→        and not resolved_options.scope
   196→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   197→    )
   198→    if should_interleave:
   199→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   200→
   201→    total = len(all_items)
   202→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   203→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   204→
   205→    if resolved_options.explain:
   206→        for item in all_items:
   207→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   208→
   209→    return {
   210→        "items": all_items,
   211→        "total": total,
   212→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   213→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   214→        "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
   215→    }
   216→
   217→
   218→__all__ = [
   219→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   220→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   221→    "WorkQueueResult",
   222→    "build_work_queue",
   223→    "group_queue_items",
   224→]

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    subjective_strict_scores,
    16→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    19→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    20→
    21→
    22→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    23→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    24→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    25→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    26→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    27→
    28→
    29→def build_finding_items(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    *,
    32→    scan_path: str | None,
    33→    status_filter: str,
    34→    scope: str | None,
    35→    chronic: bool,
    36→) -> list[dict]:
    37→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    38→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    39→    out: list[dict] = []
    40→
    41→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    42→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    43→            continue
    44→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    45→            continue
    46→        if chronic and not (
    47→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    48→        ):
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        item = dict(finding)
    52→        item["id"] = finding_id
    53→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    54→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    55→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    56→        item["review_weight"] = (
    57→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    58→        )
    59→        subjective_score = None
    60→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    61→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    62→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    63→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    64→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    65→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    66→            )
    67→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    68→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    69→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    70→            item,
    71→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    72→        )
    73→
    74→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    75→            continue
    76→        out.append(item)
    77→
    78→    return out
    79→
    80→
    81→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    82→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    83→
    84→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
    86→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
    87→
    88→    # Synthesis stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
    89→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
    90→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
    91→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
    92→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
    93→
    94→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
    95→    if kind == "workflow_action":
    96→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    97→
    98→    if kind == "cluster":
    99→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   100→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   101→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   102→        )
   103→        return (
   104→            0,
   105→            action_pri,
   106→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   107→            item.get("id", ""),
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   111→        return (
   112→            1,
   113→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
   114→            subjective_score_value(item),
   115→            item.get("id", ""),
   116→        )
   117→
   118→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   119→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   120→    return (
   121→        1,
   122→        0,
   123→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   124→        -review_weight,
   125→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   126→        item.get("id", ""),
   127→    )
   128→
   129→
   130→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   131→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   132→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   133→        return {
   134→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   135→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   136→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   137→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   138→            "policy": "Synthesis stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   139→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   140→        }
   141→
   142→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   143→        return {
   144→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   145→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after synthesis stages, before findings.",
   146→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   147→        }
   148→
   149→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   150→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   151→        return {
   152→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   153→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   154→            "initial_review": initial,
   155→            "policy": (
   156→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   157→                if initial else
   158→                "Subjective dimensions sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   159→            ),
   160→            "ranking_factors": ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   161→        }
   162→
   163→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   164→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   165→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   166→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   167→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   168→    if is_subjective:
   169→        ranking_factors = ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   170→    elif is_review:
   171→        ranking_factors = [
   172→            "confidence asc",
   173→            "review_weight desc",
   174→            "count desc",
   175→            "id asc",
   176→        ]
   177→    else:
   178→        ranking_factors = ["confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   179→    explain = {
   180→        "kind": "finding",
   181→        "confidence": confidence,
   182→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   183→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   184→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   185→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   186→    }
   187→    if is_review:
   188→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   189→    if is_subjective:
   190→        explain["policy"] = (
   191→            "Subjective findings sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   192→        )
   193→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   194→    return explain
   195→
   196→
   197→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   198→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   199→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   200→    for item in items:
   201→        if group == "file":
   202→            key = item.get("file", "")
   203→        elif group == "detector":
   204→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   205→        elif group == "cluster":
   206→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   207→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   208→        else:
   209→            key = "items"
   210→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   211→    return grouped
   212→
   213→
   214→__all__ = [
   215→    "build_finding_items",
   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",
   218→    "subjective_score_value",
   219→    "group_queue_items",
   220→]
   221→

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import item_sort_key
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     7→
     8→
     9→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    10→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    11→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    12→    if not scan_history:
    13→        return set()
    14→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    15→    if not threshold:
    16→        return set()
    17→    return {
    18→        finding_id
    19→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    20→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def apply_plan_order(
    25→    items: list[dict],
    26→    plan: dict,
    27→    *,
    28→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    29→    cluster: str | None = None,
    30→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    31→) -> list[dict]:
    32→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    33→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    34→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    35→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    36→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    37→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    38→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    39→
    40→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    41→    for item in items:
    42→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    43→
    44→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    45→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    46→        if override.get("description"):
    47→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    48→        if override.get("note"):
    49→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    50→        if override.get("cluster"):
    51→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    52→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    53→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    54→                "name": cluster_name,
    55→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    56→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    57→            }
    58→
    59→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    60→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    61→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    62→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    63→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    64→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    65→
    66→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    67→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    68→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    69→    _new = new_ids or set()
    70→    for item in items:
    71→        item_id = item["id"]
    72→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    73→            continue
    74→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    75→            skipped_items.append(item)
    76→        elif item_id in _new:
    77→            remaining_new.append(item)
    78→        else:
    79→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    80→
    81→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    82→    if include_skipped:
    83→        result = result + skipped_items
    84→
    85→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
    86→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
    87→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    88→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
    89→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
    90→            if skip_entry:
    91→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
    92→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
    93→                if skip_reason:
    94→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
    95→
    96→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
    97→    if effective_cluster:
    98→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
    99→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   100→        if cluster_member_ids:
   101→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   102→    return result
   103→
   104→
   105→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   106→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   107→    try:
   108→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   109→
   110→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   111→        if meta:
   112→            return meta.action_type
   113→    except ImportError as exc:
   114→        _ = exc
   115→    return "manual_fix"
   116→
   117→
   118→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   119→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   120→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   121→    if not clusters:
   122→        return items
   123→
   124→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   125→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   126→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   127→            continue
   128→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   129→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   130→
   131→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   132→        return items
   133→
   134→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   135→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   136→    for item in items:
   137→        cluster_name = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   138→        if cluster_name:
   139→            cluster_members.setdefault(cluster_name, []).append(item)
   140→        else:
   141→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   142→
   143→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   144→    for cluster_name, members in cluster_members.items():
   145→        if len(members) < 2:
   146→            result.extend(members)
   147→            continue
   148→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   149→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   150→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   151→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   152→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   153→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   154→            action_type = "reorganize"
   155→        else:
   156→            action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   157→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   158→                action_type = "refactor"
   159→
   160→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   161→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   162→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   163→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   164→        else:
   165→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   166→
   167→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   168→        if not primary_command:
   169→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   170→
   171→        meta_item: dict = {
   172→            "id": cluster_name,
   173→            "kind": "cluster",
   174→            "action_type": action_type,
   175→            "summary": summary,
   176→            "members": members,
   177→            "member_count": len(members),
   178→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   179→            "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   180→            "cluster_auto": True,
   181→            "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   182→            "confidence": "high",
   183→            "detector": detector,
   184→            "file": "",
   185→        }
   186→        result.append(meta_item)
   187→
   188→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   189→    return result
   190→
   191→
   192→__all__ = ["apply_plan_order", "collapse_clusters", "new_item_ids"]
   193→

1→"""Finding prioritization/selection helpers for next items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanItem, PlanState
     6→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     7→    QueueBuildOptions,
     8→    build_work_queue,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→def get_next_items(
    13→    state: PlanState,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int = 1,
    16→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    17→) -> list[PlanItem]:
    18→    """Get the N highest-priority open findings.
    19→
    20→    Legacy plan API intentionally returns only finding items (not synthetic
    21→    subjective queue items) so existing planner consumers stay stable.
    22→    The *tier* parameter is accepted for backward compatibility but ignored.
    23→    """
    24→    result = build_work_queue(
    25→        state,
    26→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    27→            count=count,
    28→            scan_path=scan_path,
    29→            status="open",
    30→            include_subjective=False,
    31→        ),
    32→    )
    33→    return [item for item in result["items"] if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
    34→
    35→
    36→def get_next_item(
    37→    state: PlanState,
    38→    tier: int | None = None,
    39→    scan_path: str | None = None,
    40→) -> PlanItem | None:
    41→    """Get the highest-priority open finding."""
    42→    items = get_next_items(state, tier=tier, count=1, scan_path=scan_path)
    43→    return items[0] if items else None
    44→

1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     9→    QueueBuildOptions,
    10→    WorkQueueResult,
    11→    build_work_queue,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _subjective_threshold(state: dict[str, Any], *, default: float = 95.0) -> float:
    16→    config = state.get("config", {})
    17→    raw_target = default
    18→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    19→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    20→    try:
    21→        value = float(raw_target)
    22→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    23→        value = default
    24→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    28→class OpenPlanQueuePolicy:
    29→    count: int | None = None
    30→    scan_path: str | None = None
    31→    include_subjective: bool = True
    32→
    33→
    34→def build_open_plan_queue(
    35→    state: dict[str, Any],
    36→    policy: OpenPlanQueuePolicy | None = None,
    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    38→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    39→    policy = policy or OpenPlanQueuePolicy()
    40→    resolved_scan_path: str | None
    41→    if isinstance(policy.scan_path, str):
    42→        resolved_scan_path = policy.scan_path
    43→    else:
    44→        raw_state_path = state.get("scan_path")
    45→        resolved_scan_path = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, str) else None
    46→    return build_work_queue(
    47→        state,
    48→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    49→            count=policy.count,
    50→            scan_path=resolved_scan_path,
    51→            status="open",
    52→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
    53→            subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state),
    54→        ),
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→__all__ = ["OpenPlanQueuePolicy", "build_open_plan_queue"]
    59→

1→"""Action computation engine used by narrative query generation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionContext, ActionItem
    10→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    compute_score_impact,
    13→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    14→    merge_potentials,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    17→
    18→
    19→def supported_fixers(state: StateModel, lang: str | None) -> set[str] | None:
    20→    """Return supported fixers for the active language, or None when unknown."""
    21→    if not lang:
    22→        return None
    23→
    24→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities", {}).get(lang, {})
    25→    fixers = capabilities.get("fixers")
    26→    if isinstance(fixers, list):
    27→        return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
    28→
    29→    try:
    30→        return set(get_lang(lang).fixers.keys())
    31→    except (ImportError, ValueError):
    32→        return None
    33→
    34→
    35→def _impact_calculator(
    36→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
    37→    state: StateModel,
    38→) -> Callable[[str, int], float]:
    39→    """Build an impact estimator closure keyed by detector and count."""
    40→    merged_potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    41→    if not merged_potentials or not dimension_scores:
    42→        return lambda _detector, _count: 0.0
    43→
    44→    scoring_view = {
    45→        name: {
    46→            "score": values["score"],
    47→            "tier": values.get("tier", 3),
    48→            "detectors": values.get("detectors", {}),
    49→        }
    50→        for name, values in dimension_scores.items()
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    def _impact(detector: str, count: int) -> float:
    54→        return compute_score_impact(
    55→            scoring_view, merged_potentials, detector, count
    56→        )
    57→
    58→    return _impact
    59→
    60→
    61→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    62→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    63→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    64→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    65→
    66→
    67→def _fixer_has_applicable_findings(
    68→    state: StateModel, detector: str, fixer_name: str
    69→) -> bool:
    70→    """For the smells detector, verify the fixer has matching open findings.
    71→
    72→    The smells detector aggregates many smell types but each fixer only handles
    73→    one sub-type (e.g. ``dead-useeffect`` only fixes ``dead_useeffect`` smells).
    74→    Without this check, a React-specific fixer can be suggested to projects that
    75→    have no React code at all, producing a confusing "Found 0 candidates" result.
    76→
    77→    For all other detectors the fixer is considered universally applicable.
    78→    """
    79→    if detector != "smells":
    80→        return True

     6→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS

     8→DISPLAY_NAMES: dict[str, str] = {

    11→    "initialization_coupling": "Init coupling",
