```
    33→
    34→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    35→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    36→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    37→
    38→
    39→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    40→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    41→        return ""
    42→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    43→
    44→
    45→def _primary_lang_from_findings(findings: dict) -> str | None:
    46→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    47→    for finding in findings.values():
    48→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    49→            continue
    50→        raw_lang = finding.get("lang")
    51→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    52→            continue
    53→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    54→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    55→    if not counts:
    56→        return None
    57→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _dimension_display_name(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> str:
    61→    try:
    62→        # Deferred import to avoid circular: scoring -> _scoring/results/core
    63→        # -> _scoring/subjective/core -> intelligence.review -> state -> scoring
    64→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    65→            dimension_display_name,
    66→        )
    67→
    68→        return str(dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    69→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    70→        return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim_name, _display_fallback(dim_name))
    71→
    72→
    73→def _dimension_weight(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> float:
    74→    try:
    75→        # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    76→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    77→            dimension_weight,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→        return float(dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    81→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    82→        return 1.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def append_subjective_dimensions(
    86→    results: dict,
    87→    findings: dict,
    88→    assessments: dict | None,
    89→    failure_set: frozenset[str],
    90→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    91→) -> None:
    92→    """Append subjective review dimensions to results dict (mutates results).
    93→
    94→    Subjective scoring is evidence-first: open review findings for a dimension
    95→    determine pass-rate, while imported assessment scores are retained as
    96→    metadata for transparency.
    97→    """
    98→    # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    99→    from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.holistic import DIMENSIONS
   100→
   101→    raw_defaults = DIMENSIONS
   102→    allowed = (
   103→        {_normalize_dimension_key(name) for name in allowed_dimensions}
   104→        if allowed_dimensions is not None
   105→        else None
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    default_dimensions = []
   109→    for raw_dim in raw_defaults:
   110→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   111→        if not dim:
   112→            continue
   113→        if allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   114→            continue
   115→        default_dimensions.append(dim)
   116→
   117→    assessed: dict[str, dict] = {}
   118→    for raw_dim, payload in (assessments or {}).items():
   119→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   120→        if not dim:
   121→            continue
   122→        # Real assessments (from review imports) always count regardless of
   123→        # allowed_dimensions — they were deliberately imported and should not
   124→        # be silently discarded.  However, system-generated placeholders
   125→        # (scan_reset_subjective) are not real assessments and should respect
   126→        # the allowed_dimensions scope like defaults.
   127→        is_placeholder = isinstance(payload, dict) and (
   128→            payload.get("placeholder") is True
   129→            or payload.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   130→            or payload.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   131→        )
   132→        if is_placeholder and allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   133→            continue
   134→        assessed[dim] = payload
   135→    existing_lower = {k.lower() for k in results}
   136→    lang_name = _primary_lang_from_findings(findings)
   137→
   138→    all_dims = list(default_dimensions)
   139→    for dim_name in assessed:
   140→        if dim_name not in default_dimensions:
   141→            all_dims.append(dim_name)
   142→
   143→    for dim_name in all_dims:
   144→        is_default = dim_name in default_dimensions
   145→        assessment = assessed.get(dim_name)
   146→        has_assessment = isinstance(assessment, dict)
   147→        if not is_default and not assessment:
   148→            continue
   149→
   150→        display = _dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   151→        if display.lower() in existing_lower:
   152→            display = f"{display} (subjective)"
   153→
   154→        # Count open review/concern findings for display (work queue), but
   155→        # these do NOT drive the dimension score — only assessment scores do.
   156→        issue_count = sum(
   157→            1
   158→            for finding in findings.values()
   159→            if finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   160→            and finding.get("status") in failure_set
   161→            and _normalize_dimension_key(
   162→                finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension")
   163→            )
   164→            == dim_name
   165→        )
   166→
   167→        assessment_score = (
   168→            max(0.0, min(100.0, float(assessment.get("score", 0))))
   169→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   170→            else 0.0
   171→        )
   172→        integrity_penalty = (
   173→            assessment.get("integrity_penalty")
   174→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   175→            else None
   176→        )
   177→        reset_pending = bool(
   178→            isinstance(assessment, dict)
   179→            and (
   180→                assessment.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   181→                or assessment.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   182→                or assessment.get("placeholder") is True
   183→            )
   184→        )
   185→        if reset_pending:
   186→            score = 0.0
   187→            pass_rate = 0.0
   188→        elif integrity_penalty == "target_match_reset":
   189→            score = 0.0
   190→            pass_rate = 0.0
   191→        elif has_assessment:
   192→            # Assessment score drives the dimension score directly.
   193→            # Resolving review findings does NOT change this score —
   194→            # only a fresh review import updates it.
   195→            score = assessment_score
   196→            pass_rate = score / 100.0
   197→        else:
   198→            # No assessment yet: explicit unassessed placeholder.
   199→            # Subjective dimensions start at 0 until an assessment is imported.
   200→            score = 0.0
   201→            pass_rate = 0.0
   202→        components: list[str] = []
   203→        component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   204→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
   205→            raw_components = assessment.get("components")
   206→            if isinstance(raw_components, list):
   207→                components = [
   208→                    str(item).strip()
   209→                    for item in raw_components
   210→                    if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
   211→                ]
   212→            raw_component_scores = assessment.get("component_scores")
   213→            if isinstance(raw_component_scores, dict):
   214→                for key, value in raw_component_scores.items():
   215→                    if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
   216→                        continue
   217→                    if not is_numeric(value):
   218→                        continue
   219→                    component_scores[key.strip()] = round(
   220→                        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))),
   221→                        1,
   222→                    )
   223→
   224→        results[display] = {
   225→            "score": round(float(score), 1),
   226→            "tier": 4,
   227→            "checks": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   228→            "issues": issue_count,
   229→            "detectors": {
   230→                "subjective_assessment": {
   231→                    "potential": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   232→                    "pass_rate": round(pass_rate, 4),
   233→                    "issues": issue_count,
   234→                    "weighted_failures": round(SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   235→                    "assessment_score": round(assessment_score, 1),
   236→                    "placeholder": reset_pending or not has_assessment,
   237→                    "dimension_key": dim_name,
   238→                    "configured_weight": round(
   239→                        _dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name), 6
   240→                    ),
   241→                    "components": components,
   242→                    **(
   243→                        {"component_scores": component_scores}
   244→                        if component_scores
   245→                        else {}
   246→                    ),
   247→                }
   248→            },
   249→        }
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   254→    "append_subjective_dimensions",
   255→]
```

```
    42→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""

    45→
    46→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    dim_scores: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    52→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    53→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):

    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))

    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low

    70→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    72→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    73→    state = runtime.state
    74→    config = runtime.config

    89→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    90→    plan_data: dict | None,
    91→    *,
    92→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    93→    scope: str | None,
    94→) -> str | None:

    96→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    97→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    98→        if active_cluster:

   107→    state: dict,

   111→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(

   113→    )
   114→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   115→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   116→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   117→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   118→        state,
   119→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   120→    )
   121→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [

   123→    ]

   133→    if output_file:

   145→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):

   172→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   173→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   174→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   175→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   176→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   177→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   178→
   179→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   180→
   181→    # Load the living plan
   182→    plan = load_plan()
   183→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   184→    if (
   185→        plan.get("queue_order")
   186→        or plan.get("overrides")
   187→        or plan.get("clusters")
   188→    ):
   189→        plan_data = plan
   190→
   191→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   192→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   193→        plan_data,
   194→        cluster_arg=cluster_arg,
   195→        scope=scope,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=count,
   202→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   203→            scope=scope,
   204→            status=status,
   205→            include_subjective=True,
   206→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   207→            explain=explain,
   208→            plan=plan_data,
   209→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   210→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   211→        ),
   212→    )
   213→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   214→
   215→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   216→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   217→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   218→        state,
   219→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   220→    )
   221→
   222→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   223→        queue=queue,
   224→        items=items,
   225→        state=state,
   226→        narrative=narrative,
   227→        plan_data=plan_data,
   228→    )
   229→    write_query(payload)
   230→
   231→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   232→        return
   233→
   234→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   235→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   236→        state.get("findings", {}),
   237→        state.get("scan_path"),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   241→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   242→        state=state,
   243→        plan_data=plan_data,
   244→    )
   245→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   246→
   247→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   248→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   249→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   250→        queue,
   251→        strict_score,
   252→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   253→        target_strict=target_strict,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   258→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   259→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   260→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   261→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   262→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   263→    )
   264→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   265→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   266→        state,
   267→        dim_scores,
   268→        findings_scoped,
   269→        strict_score=strict_score,
   270→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   271→        queue_total=queue_total,
   272→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   273→        breakdown=breakdown,
   274→    )
   275→    print()
   276→
   277→
   278→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   279→
```

```
    37→
    38→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    39→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
    40→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    41→    state = runtime.state
    42→    config = runtime.config
    43→
    44→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    45→        return
    46→
    47→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    48→    if skill_warning:
    49→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    50→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    51→    if config_warning:
    52→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    53→
    54→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(state=state)
    55→
    56→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
    57→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
    58→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
    59→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
    60→    if not ok or pattern is None:
    61→        return
    62→
    63→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    64→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    65→    narrative = compute_narrative(
    66→        state,
    67→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
    68→    )
    69→
    70→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
    71→
    72→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    73→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
    77→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
    81→        matches = _load_dimension_findings(state, entity, status_filter)
    82→        if not matches:
    83→            _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
    84→            return
    85→        pattern = entity.display_name
    86→    else:
    87→        matches = load_matches(
    88→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
    89→        )
    90→
    91→    if not matches:
    92→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
    93→        return
    94→
    95→    no_budget = getattr(args, "no_budget", False)
    96→    (
    97→        surfaced_matches,
    98→        hidden_by_detector,
    99→        noise_budget,
   100→        global_noise_budget,
   101→        budget_warning,
   102→    ) = resolve_noise(
   103→        config,
   104→        matches,
   105→        no_budget=no_budget,
   106→    )
   107→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   108→
   109→    payload = build_show_payload(
   110→        surfaced_matches,
   111→        pattern,
   112→        status_filter,
   113→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   114→            total_matches=len(matches),
   115→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   116→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   117→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   118→        ),
   119→    )
   120→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   121→
   122→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   123→    if output_file:
   124→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   125→            return
   126→        raise SystemExit(1)
   127→
   128→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   129→    render_findings(
   130→        surfaced_matches,
   131→        pattern=pattern,
   132→        status_filter=status_filter,
   133→        show_code=show_code,
   134→        top=top,
   135→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   136→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   137→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   138→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   139→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   140→    )
   141→    try:
   142→        plan = load_plan()
   143→        plan_active = plan if (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")) else None
   144→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   145→        plan_active = None
   146→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=plan_active)
   147→    show_subjective_followup(
   148→        state,
   149→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   150→    )
   151→
   152→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   153→
   154→
   155→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
```

```
1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
    10→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    11→    QueueBuildOptions,
    12→    build_work_queue,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class ResolvedEntity:
    19→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    20→
    21→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    22→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    23→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    24→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    25→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_special_view(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    29→    if lowered not in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    30→        return None
    31→    return ResolvedEntity(
    32→        kind="special_view",
    33→        pattern=pattern,
    34→        display_name=pattern,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→
    38→def _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    39→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    40→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    41→    if not detectors:
    42→        return None
    43→    dim_display = pattern
    44→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
    45→        dim_lowered = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    46→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim_lowered == lowered:
    47→            dim_display = dim.name
    48→            break
    49→    return ResolvedEntity(
    50→        kind="dimension",
    51→        pattern=pattern,
    52→        display_name=dim_display,
    53→        detectors=tuple(detectors),
    54→        is_subjective=False,
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→def _resolve_subjective_dimension(
    59→    pattern: str,
    60→    lowered: str,
    61→    state: dict,
    62→) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    63→    display_name = scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.get(lowered)
    64→    if not display_name:
    65→        for key in state.get("dimension_scores") or {}:
    66→            if key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    67→                display_name = key
    68→                break
    69→    if not display_name:
    70→        return None
    71→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, display_name)
    72→    is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (
    73→        dim_data.get("detectors", {}) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else {}
    74→    )
    75→    return ResolvedEntity(
    76→        kind="dimension",
    77→        pattern=pattern,
    78→        display_name=display_name,
    79→        detectors=(),
    80→        is_subjective=is_subj,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: dict) -> ResolvedEntity:
    85→    """Classify a user pattern as a dimension, special view, or passthrough.
    86→
    87→    Resolution priority:
    88→    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"
    89→    2. Mechanical dimension name (from DIMENSIONS)
    90→    3. Subjective dimension name (from DISPLAY_NAMES / dimension_scores)
    91→    4. Everything else: file_or_pattern passthrough
    92→    """
    93→    lowered = pattern.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    94→
    95→    # 1. Special views
    96→    special_view = _resolve_special_view(pattern, lowered)
    97→    if special_view is not None:
    98→        return special_view
    99→
   100→    # 2. Mechanical dimension (via DIMENSIONS list)
   101→    mechanical = _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern, lowered)
   102→    if mechanical is not None:
   103→        return mechanical
   104→
   105→    # 3. Subjective dimension (via DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
   106→    subjective = _resolve_subjective_dimension(pattern, lowered, state)
   107→    if subjective is not None:
   108→        return subjective
   109→
   110→    # 4. Everything else
   111→    return ResolvedEntity(
   112→        kind="file_or_pattern",
   113→        pattern=pattern,
   114→        display_name=pattern,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→
   118→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   119→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
   120→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   121→    for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   122→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   123→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
   124→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
   125→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   126→        if key not in lookup:
   127→            lookup[key] = detectors
   128→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
   129→    for key, display in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   130→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   131→        normalized_display = display.lower()
   132→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
   133→        for dim in scoring_mod.DIMENSIONS:
   134→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
   135→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
   136→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   137→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
   138→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
   139→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
   140→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
   141→    return lookup
   142→
   143→
   144→def _lookup_dimension_score(
   145→    state: dict, display_name: str,
   146→) -> tuple[dict, str]:
   147→    """Find dimension_scores entry with case-insensitive fallback.
   148→
   149→    Returns (dim_data_dict, resolved_display_name).
   150→    """
   151→    lowered = display_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   152→    dim_data = (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(display_name, {})
   153→    if not dim_data:
   154→        for ds_key, ds_val in (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).items():
   155→            if ds_key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
   156→                dim_data = ds_val
   157→                display_name = ds_key
   158→                break
   159→    return dim_data, display_name
   160→
   161→
   162→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
   163→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   164→    names = getattr(registry_mod, "DISPLAY_ORDER", [])
   165→    if names:
   166→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   167→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   168→
   169→
   170→def resolve_show_scope(args) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   171→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   172→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   173→    pattern = args.pattern
   174→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   175→    if chronic:
   176→        scope = pattern
   177→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   178→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   179→    if not pattern:
   180→        print(
   181→            colorize(
   182→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   183→                "yellow",
   184→            )
   185→        )
   186→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   187→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   188→
   189→
   190→def load_matches(
   191→    state: dict,
   192→    *,
   193→    scope: str | None,
   194→    status_filter: str,
   195→    chronic: bool,
   196→) -> list[dict]:
   197→    """Load matching findings from the ranked queue."""
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=None,
   202→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   203→            scope=scope,
   204→            status=status_filter,
   205→            include_subjective=False,
   206→            chronic=chronic,
   207→        ),
   208→    )
   209→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "finding"]
   210→
   211→
   212→def resolve_noise(config: dict, matches: list[dict], *, no_budget: bool = False):
   213→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches.
   214→
   215→    When *no_budget* is True, all matches are surfaced (nothing hidden).
   216→    """
   217→    if no_budget:
   218→        return (
   219→            matches,
   220→            {},
   221→            0,
   222→            0,
   223→            None,
   224→        )
   225→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   226→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   227→    )
   228→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   229→        matches,
   230→        budget=noise_budget,
   231→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   232→    )
   233→    return (
   234→        surfaced_matches,
   235→        hidden_by_detector,
   236→        noise_budget,
   237→        global_noise_budget,
   238→        budget_warning,
   239→    )
   240→
   241→
   242→__all__ = [
   243→    "ResolvedEntity",
   244→    "_detector_names_hint",
   245→    "_lookup_dimension_score",
   246→    "load_matches",
   247→    "resolve_entity",
   248→    "resolve_noise",
   249→    "resolve_show_scope",
   250→]
   251→
```

```
    51→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    61→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)
    62→
    63→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    64→        print(
    65→            json.dumps(
    66→                _status_json_payload(
    67→                    state,
    68→                    stats,
    69→                    dim_scores,
    70→                    scorecard_dims,
    71→                    subjective_measures,
    72→                    suppression,
    73→                ),
    74→                indent=2,
    75→            )
    76→        )
    77→        return
    78→
    79→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    83→    if skill_warning:
    84→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    85→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    86→    if config_warning:
    87→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    88→
    89→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    90→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    91→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    92→
    93→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    94→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    95→
    96→    # Load living plan for plan-aware rendering and narrative
    97→    _plan = load_plan()
    98→    _plan_active = _plan if (
    99→        _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   100→    ) else None
   101→
   102→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(plan=_plan, state=state)
   103→
   104→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   105→        state,
   106→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="status", plan=_plan_active),
   107→    )
   108→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
   109→
   110→    # Show frozen plan-start score when in an active queue cycle
   111→    _plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(_plan)
   112→    _breakdown = None
   113→    _status_queue_remaining = 0
   114→    if _plan_start_strict is not None:
   115→        try:
   116→            _breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, _plan)
   117→            _status_queue_remaining = _breakdown.queue_total
   118→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   119→            logging.debug("Plan-aware queue count failed: %s", exc)
   120→            _status_queue_remaining = 0
   121→    if _plan_start_strict is not None and _status_queue_remaining > 0:
   122→        print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   123→            _plan, _status_queue_remaining, breakdown=_breakdown,
   124→        )
   125→    else:
   126→        for line, style in score_summary_lines(
   127→            overall_score=scores.overall,
   128→            objective_score=scores.objective,
   129→            strict_score=scores.strict,
   130→            verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   131→            target_strict=target_strict_score,
   132→        ):
   133→            print(colorize(line, style))
   134→        # Show queue breakdown even without frozen score
   135→        if _breakdown is None:
   136→            try:
   137→                _breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, _plan)
   138→            except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   139→                _breakdown = None
   140→        if _breakdown is not None and _breakdown.queue_total > 0:
   141→            block = format_queue_block(_breakdown)
   142→            for text, style in block:
   143→                print(colorize(text, style))
   144→    print_scan_metrics(state)
   145→    print_open_scope_breakdown(state)
   146→    print_scan_completeness(state)
   147→
   148→    # Compute objective backlog once for consistent subjective actionability gating
   149→    _objective_backlog = 0
   150→    if _breakdown is not None:
   151→        _objective_backlog = max(0, _breakdown.queue_total - _breakdown.subjective)
   152→
   153→    if dim_scores:
   154→        show_dimension_table(state, dim_scores, objective_backlog=_objective_backlog)
   155→        reporting_dimensions_mod.show_score_model_breakdown(
   156→            state,
   157→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
   158→        )
   159→    else:
   160→        show_tier_progress_table(by_tier)
   161→
   162→    if dim_scores:
   163→        show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores, state, plan=_plan_active)
   164→        show_subjective_followup(
   165→            state,
   166→            dim_scores,
   167→            target_strict_score=target_strict_score,
   168→            objective_backlog=_objective_backlog,
   169→        )
   170→
   171→    show_review_summary(state)
   172→    show_structural_areas(state)
   173→    show_agent_plan(narrative, plan=_plan_active)
   174→
   175→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   176→        print(colorize(f"  -> {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   177→        print()
   178→
   179→    if ignores:
   180→        show_ignore_summary(ignores, suppression)
   181→
   182→    review_age = config.get("review_max_age_days", 30)
   183→    if review_age != 30:
   184→        label = "never" if review_age == 0 else f"{review_age} days"
   185→        print(colorize(f"  Review staleness: {label}", "dim"))
   186→    print()
   187→
   188→    write_status_query(
   189→        state=state,
   190→        stats=stats,
   191→        by_tier=by_tier,
   192→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   193→        scorecard_dims=scorecard_dims,
   194→        subjective_measures=subjective_measures,
   195→        suppression=suppression,
   196→        narrative=narrative,
   197→        ignores=ignores,
   198→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   199→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   200→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   201→        verified_strict_score=scores.verified,
   202→        plan=_plan_active,
   203→    )
   204→
   205→
   206→def _status_json_payload(
   207→    state: dict,
   208→    stats: dict,
   209→    dim_scores: dict,
   210→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
   211→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
   212→    suppression: dict,
   213→) -> dict:
   214→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   215→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   216→    open_scope = (
   217→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
   218→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
   219→        else None
   220→    )
   221→    return {
   222→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
   223→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
   224→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
   225→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   226→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   227→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
   228→        "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
   229→        "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
   230→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   231→        "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
   232→        "stats": stats,
   233→        "open_scope": open_scope,
   234→        "suppression": suppression,
   235→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
   236→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   237→    }
   238→
   239→__all__ = [
   240→    "cmd_status",
   241→    "show_dimension_table",
   242→    "show_focus_suggestion",
   243→    "show_ignore_summary",
   244→    "show_structural_areas",
   245→    "show_subjective_followup",
   246→]
   247→
```

```
1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    10→    cmd_plan_describe,
    11→    cmd_plan_done,
    12→    cmd_plan_focus,
    13→    cmd_plan_note,
    14→    cmd_plan_reopen,
    15→    cmd_plan_skip,
    16→    cmd_plan_unskip,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
    19→from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output as _plan_output_impl
    20→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    21→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    22→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    23→    append_log_entry,
    24→    load_plan,
    25→    purge_ids,
    26→    reset_plan,
    27→    save_plan,
    28→)
    29→
    30→
    31→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    32→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    33→    # Auto-resolve the create-plan workflow item when plan runs
    34→    plan = load_plan()
    35→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    36→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
    37→        save_plan(plan)
    38→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    45→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    46→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    47→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    48→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    49→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    50→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    51→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    52→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    53→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    54→
    55→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    56→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    57→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    58→
    59→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    60→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    61→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    62→    if total_skipped:
    63→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    64→    else:
    65→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    66→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    67→    if clusters:
    68→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    69→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    70→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    71→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    72→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    73→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    74→    if described or noted:
    75→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    76→    if active:
    77→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    78→    if superseded:
    79→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    80→
    81→
    82→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    83→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    84→    plan = load_plan()
    85→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    86→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
    87→    reset_plan(plan)
    88→    append_log_entry(
    89→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
    90→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    91→    )
    92→    save_plan(plan)
    93→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    94→
    95→
    96→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    97→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    98→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    99→
   100→    if plan_action is None:
   101→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "show":
   105→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if plan_action == "queue":
   109→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "reset":
   113→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   114→        return
   115→
   116→    if plan_action == "move":
   117→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    if plan_action in (
   121→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   122→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   123→    ):
   124→        dispatch = {
   125→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   126→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   127→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   128→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   129→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   130→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   131→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   132→        }
   133→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   137→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   138→        return
   139→
   140→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   141→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   142→
   143→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   147→
   148→
   149→# Backwards-compatible alias
   150→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   151→
   152→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   153→
```

```
1→"""Markdown plan rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from datetime import date
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import dimension_action_type
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanState
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→from desloppify.engine.planning.render_sections import (
    13→    addressed_section as _addressed_section,
    14→    plan_skipped_section as _plan_skipped_section,
    15→    plan_superseded_section as _plan_superseded_section,
    16→    plan_user_ordered_section as _plan_user_ordered_section,
    17→    summary_lines as _summary_lines,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS, DISPLAY_NAMES
    20→from desloppify.state import get_objective_score, get_overall_score, get_strict_score
    21→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    22→
    23→
    24→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    25→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    26→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    27→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    28→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    29→
    30→    if (
    31→        overall_score is not None
    32→        and objective_score is not None
    33→        and strict_score is not None
    34→    ):
    35→        header_score = (
    36→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    37→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    38→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    39→        )
    40→    elif overall_score is not None:
    41→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    42→    else:
    43→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    44→
    45→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    46→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    47→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    48→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    49→
    50→    lines = [
    51→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    52→        "",
    53→        f"{header_score} | "
    54→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    55→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    56→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    57→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    58→        "",
    59→    ]
    60→
    61→    if total_files:
    62→        loc_str = (
    63→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    64→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    65→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    66→        )
    67→        lines.append(
    68→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    69→        )
    70→
    71→    return lines
    72→
    73→
    74→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    75→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    76→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    77→    if not dim_scores:
    78→        return []
    79→
    80→    lines = [
    81→        "## Health by Dimension",
    82→        "",
    83→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    84→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    85→    ]
    86→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    87→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    88→    subjective_display_names = {
    89→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    90→    }
    91→
    92→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    93→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    94→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    95→            return True
    96→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    97→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    98→
    99→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   100→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   101→        if not ds:
   102→            continue
   103→        static_names.add(dim.name)
   104→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
   105→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   106→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   107→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   108→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   109→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   110→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   111→        lines.append(
   112→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   113→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   114→        )
   115→
   116→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   117→
   118→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   119→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   120→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   121→    ]
   122→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   123→
   124→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   125→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   126→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   127→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   128→            continue
   129→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   130→            continue
   131→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   132→        rendered_names.add(name)
   133→
   134→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   135→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   136→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   137→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   138→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   139→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   140→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   141→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   142→        lines.append(
   143→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   144→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   145→        )
   146→
   147→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   148→        (name, ds)
   149→        for name, ds in sorted(
   150→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   151→        )
   152→        if (
   153→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   154→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   155→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   156→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   157→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   158→        )
   159→    ]
   160→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   161→
   162→    if subjective_rows:
   163→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   164→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   165→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   166→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   167→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   168→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   169→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   170→            lines.append(
   171→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   172→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   173→            )
   174→
   175→    lines.append("")
   176→    return lines
   177→
   178→
   179→def _plan_item_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   180→    """Build per-file sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   181→
   182→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   183→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   184→    raw_target = (
   185→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   186→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   187→        else 95
   188→    )
   189→    try:
   190→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   191→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   192→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   193→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   194→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   195→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   196→
   197→    queue = build_work_queue(
   198→        queue_state,
   199→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   200→            count=None,
   201→            scan_path=scan_path,
   202→            status="open",
   203→            include_subjective=True,
   204→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   205→        ),
   206→    )
   207→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   208→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   209→    for item in open_items:
   210→        by_file[item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   211→
   212→    lines: list[str] = []
   213→    total_count = len(open_items)
   214→    if not open_items:
   215→        return lines
   216→
   217→    lines.extend([
   218→        "---",
   219→        f"## Open Items ({total_count})",
   220→        "",
   221→    ])
   222→
   223→    sorted_files = sorted(
   224→        by_file.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→    )
   226→    for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→        display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→        lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→        lines.append("")
   230→        for item in file_items:
   231→            if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                    lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                continue
   237→
   238→            conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→            lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→            lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→        lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   247→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   248→
   249→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   250→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   251→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   252→    """
   253→    findings = state["findings"]
   254→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   255→
   256→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   257→    if plan is None:
   258→        try:
   259→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   260→            plan = load_plan()
   261→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   262→            plan = {}
   263→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   264→        plan = {}
   265→    plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   266→    plan.setdefault("skipped", {})
   267→    plan.setdefault("clusters", {})
   268→
   269→    has_plan = bool(
   270→        plan
   271→        and (
   272→            plan.get("queue_order")
   273→            or plan.get("skipped")
   274→            or plan.get("clusters")
   275→        )
   276→    )
   277→
   278→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   279→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   280→    lines.extend(_summary_lines(stats))
   281→
   282→    if has_plan:
   283→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   284→        queue = build_work_queue(
   285→            state,
   286→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   287→                count=None,
   288→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   289→                status="open",
   290→                include_subjective=True,
   291→            ),
   292→        )
   293→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   294→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   295→
   296→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   297→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   298→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   299→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   300→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   301→        if remaining:
   302→            lines.append("---")
   303→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   304→            lines.append("")
   305→
   306→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(findings, state=state))
   307→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   308→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   309→    else:
   310→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(findings, state=state))
   311→
   312→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   313→
   314→    return "\n".join(lines)
   315→
```

```
100→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   101→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
   102→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
   106→    """Count unique open findings matching the provided patterns."""
   107→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
   108→    for pattern in patterns:
   109→        for finding in state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   110→            finding_id = finding.get("id")
   111→            if finding_id:
   112→                matched_ids.add(finding_id)
   113→    return len(matched_ids)
   114→
   115→
   116→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   117→    state: dict,
   118→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   119→    *,
   120→    attestation: str | None,
   121→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   122→) -> float:
   123→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   124→    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   125→    if before is None:
   126→        return 0.0
   127→
   128→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   129→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   130→    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)
   131→    if after is None:
   132→        return 0.0
   133→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   137→    state: dict,
   138→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   139→    *,
   140→    attestation: str | None,
   141→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   142→) -> None:
   143→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   147→    if preview_count <= 10:
   148→        return
   149→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   150→        return
   151→
   152→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   153→        state,
   154→        args,
   155→        attestation=attestation,
   156→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   157→    )
   158→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} findings).")
   159→    if strict_delta > 0:
   160→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   161→    _emit_warning(
   162→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   163→    )
   164→    sys.exit(1)
   165→
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_synthesis_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    13→
    14→
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    22→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    23→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    24→        plan_data = plan
    25→
    26→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    27→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    28→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    29→        if active_cluster:
    30→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    31→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    32→
    33→
    34→def _print_queue_header(
    35→    *,
    36→    items: list[dict],
    37→    include_skipped: bool,
    38→    plan: dict,
    39→    plan_data: dict | None,
    40→    new_count: int = 0,
    41→) -> None:
    42→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
    43→        QueueBreakdown,
    44→        format_queue_headline,
    45→    )
    46→
    47→    total = len(items)
    48→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    49→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    50→    plan_skipped_total = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    51→
    52→    # Count subjective items in the visible list
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for it in items if it.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    # Count plan-ordered items (minus skipped)
    58→    plan_ordered = 0
    59→    if plan_data:
    60→        queue_order = plan_data.get("queue_order", [])
    61→        skipped_ids = set(plan_data.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    62→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    63→
    64→    breakdown = QueueBreakdown(
    65→        queue_total=non_skipped,
    66→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    67→        skipped=plan_skipped_total,
    68→        subjective=subjective,
    69→    )
    70→    headline = format_queue_headline(breakdown)
    71→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    72→    print(colorize(f"\n  {headline}{new_suffix}", "bold"))
    73→
    74→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    75→    if focus:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    77→
    78→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    79→        return
    80→    if not plan_skipped_total:
    81→        return
    82→    print(
    83→        colorize(
    84→            f"  ({plan_skipped_total} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_total != 1 else ''}"
    85→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→    )
    89→
    90→
    91→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    92→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    93→        return items[:top]
    94→    return items
    95→
    96→
    97→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    98→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    99→    _new = new_ids or set()
   100→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   101→        pos = str(idx)
   102→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   103→
   104→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   105→            blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
   106→            blocked_tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
   107→            conf_str = "—"
   108→            detector = "planning"
   109→            summary = f"{item.get('summary', '')}{blocked_tag}"
   110→            cluster_name = ""
   111→        elif kind == "workflow_action":
   112→            conf_str = "—"
   113→            detector = "workflow"
   114→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   115→            cluster_name = ""
   116→        elif kind == "cluster":
   117→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "high")
   118→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   119→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   120→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   121→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   122→                if m.get("id") in _new
   123→            )
   124→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   125→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   126→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   127→        else:
   128→            conf_str = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   129→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   130→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   131→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   132→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   133→
   134→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   135→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   136→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   137→        rows.append([pos, conf_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   138→    return rows
   139→
   140→
   141→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   143→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   144→    state = runtime.state
   145→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   146→        return
   147→
   148→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   149→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   150→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   151→
   152→    plan = load_plan()
   153→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   154→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   155→
   156→    queue = build_work_queue(
   157→        state,
   158→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   159→            count=None,
   160→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   161→            status="open",
   162→            include_subjective=True,
   163→            plan=plan_data,
   164→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   165→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   166→            collapse_clusters=True,
   167→        ),
   168→    )
   169→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   170→
   171→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   172→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   173→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last synthesis)
   174→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   175→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   176→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   177→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   178→
   179→    if sort_by == "recent":
   180→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   181→
   182→    _print_queue_header(
   183→        items=items,
   184→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   185→        plan=plan,
   186→        plan_data=plan_data,
   187→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   188→    )
   189→
   190→    if not items:
   191→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Determine which items to show
   195→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   196→    headers = ["#", "Confidence", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   197→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   198→
   199→    print()
   200→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   201→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   202→
   203→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   204→        remaining = len(items) - top
   205→        print(colorize(
   206→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   207→        ))
   208→    print()
   209→
   210→
   211→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   212→
```
