```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueueBreakdown:
    17→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    18→
    19→    queue_total: int = 0
    20→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    21→    skipped: int = 0
    22→    subjective: int = 0
    23→    workflow: int = 0
    24→    suppressed: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→
    30→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    plan: dict | None = None,
    33→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    34→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    35→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    36→
    37→    result = build_work_queue(
    38→        state,
    39→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    40→            status="open",
    41→            count=None,
    42→            plan=plan,
    43→            collapse_clusters=True,
    44→            include_skipped=False,
    45→        ),
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    queue_total = result["total"]
    49→    suppressed = int(result.get("suppressed_count", 0))
    50→
    51→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue
    52→    items = result.get("items", [])
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for item in items
    55→        if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    56→    )
    57→    workflow = sum(
    58→        1 for item in items
    59→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    # Plan-derived counts
    63→    plan_ordered = 0
    64→    skipped = 0
    65→    if plan:
    66→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    67→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    68→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    71→
    72→    # Focus cluster info
    73→    focus_cluster = None
    74→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    75→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    76→    if plan:
    77→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→        if active:
    79→            focus_cluster = active
    80→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    81→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    82→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    83→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    84→            open_findings = {
    85→                fid
    86→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    87→                if f.get("status") == "open"
    88→            }
    89→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
    90→
    91→    return QueueBreakdown(
    92→        queue_total=queue_total,
    93→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    94→        skipped=skipped,
    95→        subjective=subjective,
    96→        workflow=workflow,
    97→        suppressed=suppressed,
    98→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
    99→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
   100→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   105→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   106→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   107→
   108→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   109→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   110→
   111→    Examples::
   112→
   113→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   114→        Queue: 1934 items
   115→    """
   116→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   117→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   118→
   119→    # Parenthesized segments
   120→    segments: list[str] = []
   121→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   122→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   123→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   124→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   125→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   126→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   127→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   128→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   129→    if breakdown.suppressed > 0:
   130→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.suppressed} suppressed")
   131→
   132→    if segments:
   133→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   134→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   135→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   136→    return label
   137→
   138→
   139→def format_queue_block(
   140→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   141→    *,
   142→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   143→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   144→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   145→
   146→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   147→    """
   148→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   149→
   150→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   151→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   152→        focus_line = (
   153→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   154→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   155→            f" items remaining"
   156→        )
   157→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   158→
   159→    # Frozen score line
   160→    if frozen_score is not None:
   161→        lines.append((
   162→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   163→            "cyan",
   164→        ))
   165→
   166→    # Queue headline — always the same
   167→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   168→
   169→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   170→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   171→        lines.append((
   172→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   173→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   174→            "dim",
   175→        ))
   176→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   177→        lines.append((
   178→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   179→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   180→            "dim",
   181→        ))
   182→    else:
   183→        lines.append((
   184→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   185→            "dim",
   186→        ))
   187→
   188→    return lines
   189→
   190→
   191→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   192→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   193→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   194→
   195→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   196→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
   197→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
   198→
   199→    result = build_work_queue(
   200→        state,
   201→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   202→            status="open",
   203→            count=None,
   204→            plan=plan,
   205→            collapse_clusters=True,
   206→            include_skipped=False,
   207→        ),
   208→    )
   209→    return result["total"]
   210→
   211→
   212→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   213→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   214→    if not plan:
   215→        return None
   216→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   217→
   218→
   219→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   220→    plan: dict,
   221→    queue_remaining: int,
   222→    *,
   223→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   224→) -> None:
   225→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   226→
   227→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   228→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   229→    """
   230→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   231→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   232→    if strict is None:
   233→        return
   234→
   235→    if breakdown is not None:
   236→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   237→        print()
   238→        for text, style in block:
   239→            print(colorize(text, style))
   240→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   241→            print(colorize(
   242→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   243→                "dim",
   244→            ))
   245→        return
   246→
   247→    # Legacy fallback
   248→    print(
   249→        colorize(
   250→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   251→            "cyan",
   252→        )
   253→    )
   254→    print(
   255→        colorize(
   256→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   257→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   258→            "dim",
   259→        )
   260→    )
   261→
   262→
   263→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   264→    state: dict,
   265→    prev,
   266→    plan: dict | None,
   267→) -> None:
   268→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   269→
   270→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
   271→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
   272→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
   273→    """
   274→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   275→        try:
   276→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   277→            remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   278→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   279→            breakdown = None
   280→            remaining = 0
   281→        if remaining > 0 and breakdown is not None:
   282→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   283→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   284→            )
   285→            return
   286→
   287→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
   288→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   289→
   290→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   291→
   292→
   293→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   294→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   295→
   296→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   297→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   298→    """
   299→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   300→
   301→    try:
   302→        plan = load_plan()
   303→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   304→        plan = None
   305→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   306→
   307→
   308→__all__ = [
   309→    "QueueBreakdown",
   310→    "format_queue_block",
   311→    "format_queue_headline",
   312→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   313→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   314→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   315→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   316→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   317→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   318→]
   319→
```

```
1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     8→
     9→
    10→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    patterns: list[str],
    13→    status: str,
    14→) -> bool:
    15→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    16→    if status != "fixed":
    17→        return False
    18→    try:
    19→        if not has_living_plan():
    20→            return False
    21→        plan = load_plan()
    22→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    23→        if not queue_order:
    24→            return False
    25→
    26→        result = build_work_queue(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
    29→                plan=plan,
    30→                count=1,
    31→                collapse_clusters=True,
    32→                include_subjective=True,
    33→            ),
    34→        )
    35→        if not result["items"]:
    36→            return False
    37→
    38→        front_item = result["items"][0]
    39→        front_id = front_item["id"]
    40→
    41→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
    42→        if front_item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    43→            for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    44→                front_ids.add(member["id"])
    45→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    46→        else:
    47→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    48→
    49→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    50→        resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    51→        for pattern in patterns:
    52→            if pattern in clusters:
    53→                resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("finding_ids", []))
    54→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    55→            else:
    56→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    57→
    58→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    59→        resolved_ids = {
    60→            finding_id
    61→            for finding_id in resolved_ids
    62→            if finding_id in clusters
    63→            or (finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open")
    64→        }
    65→        if not resolved_ids:
    66→            return False
    67→
    68→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
    69→        for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    70→            if cluster_id in clusters:
    71→                alive_members = {
    72→                    finding_id
    73→                    for finding_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("finding_ids", [])
    74→                    if finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open"
    75→                }
    76→                if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    77→                    out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    78→        if not out_of_order:
    79→            return False
    80→
    81→        print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    82→        for finding_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    83→            print(f"    {finding_id}")
    84→        print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    85→        print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    86→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan move <pattern> --position top    # move to front", "dim"))
    87→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    88→        print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    89→        return True
    90→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    91→        return False
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
    95→
```

```
   651→
   652→def test_workflow_items_preserved_at_front_during_interleave():
   653→    """Workflow items (workflow_stage, workflow_action) should not participate
   654→    in the subjective/objective interleave — they stay at the front."""
   655→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail
   656→
   657→    workflow = {"id": "synthesis::observe", "kind": "workflow_stage"}
   658→    obj1 = {"id": "smells::a", "kind": "finding"}
   659→    obj2 = {"id": "smells::b", "kind": "finding"}
   660→    obj3 = {"id": "smells::c", "kind": "finding"}
   661→    subj1 = {"id": "subjective::naming", "kind": "subjective_dimension"}
   662→    subj2 = {"id": "subjective::clarity", "kind": "subjective_dimension"}
   663→
   664→    items = [workflow, obj1, obj2, obj3, subj1, subj2]
   665→    result = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(items)
   666→
   667→    # Workflow item must be first
   668→    assert result[0]["id"] == "synthesis::observe"
   669→    # All items preserved
   670→    assert len(result) == 6
   671→    # No workflow items mixed into the interleaved portion
   672→    result_ids = [r["id"] for r in result]
   673→    assert result_ids.index("synthesis::observe") == 0
   674→
   675→
   676→def test_workflow_action_also_bypasses_interleave():
   677→    """workflow_action kind is also excluded from interleave."""
   678→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail

   681→    obj = {"id": "smells::a", "kind": "finding"}
   682→    subj = {"id": "subjective::naming", "kind": "subjective_dimension"}
   683→
   684→    result = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail([wf, obj, subj])
   685→    assert result[0]["id"] == "workflow::create-plan"
   686→    assert len(result) == 3
```

## Complete Analysis: Marginal Score Impact in Desloppify

### 1. Overall Health Score Formula

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/health.py`

The overall score is a two-pool weighted average:

```
overall_score = mechanical_avg * MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION + subjective_avg * SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
```

Where:
- `MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.40`
- `SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60`

If only one pool has data, the other pool's fraction is absorbed (the present pool gets 100%).

**Mechanical pool average** (weighted average of dimension scores):
```python
mech_avg = sum(score_i * effective_weight_i) / sum(effective_weight_i)
```

Where `effective_weight_i = configured_weight * sample_factor` and `sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE)` with `MIN_SAMPLE = 200`.

**Subjective pool average** (weighted average of dimension scores):
```python
subj_avg = sum(score_i * effective_weight_i) / sum(effective_weight_i)
```

For subjective dimensions, `effective_weight = configured_weight` (no sample dampening).

### 2. Weight Constants

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py`

**Mechanical pool weights** (total = 6.0 if all present):
```python
MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS = {
    "file health": 2.0,
    "code quality": 1.0,
    "duplication": 1.0,
    "test health": 1.0,
    "security": 1.0,
}
```

**Subjective pool weights** (total = 115.0):
```python
SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS = {
    "high elegance": 22.0,
    "mid elegance": 22.0,
    "low elegance": 12.0,
    "contracts": 12.0,
    "type safety": 12.0,
    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
    "logic clarity": 6.0,
    "structure nav": 5.0,
    "error consistency": 3.0,
    "naming quality": 2.0,
    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
    "design coherence": 10.0,
}
```

### 3. Dimension Score Formula

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/core.py`, lines 77-94

A dimension's score is computed from all its constituent detectors:

```python
dim_score = max(0.0, (total_checks - total_weighted_failures) / total_checks) * 100
```

Where `total_checks` and `total_weighted_failures` are summed across all detectors in the dimension.

### 4. Weighted Failures per Finding

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/detection.py`

For **non-file-based detectors** (e.g., `unused`, `logs`, `exports`, `deprecated`, etc.):
```python
weight = CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS[finding.confidence]
# CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {HIGH: 1.0, MEDIUM: 0.7, LOW: 0.3}
```

Each open finding adds its confidence weight to `weighted_failures`.

For **file-based detectors** (e.g., `smells`, `test_coverage`, `security`):
- With `use_loc_weight=True` (test_coverage): uses `finding.detail.loc_weight` and caps per-file weight
- Without `use_loc_weight`: uses confidence weights but applies a tiered per-file cap:
  - 1-2 findings in file: cap 1.0
  - 3-5 findings: cap 1.5
  - 6+: cap 2.0

The denominator (`potential`) is the file count for file-based detectors, or the total check count for others.

### 5. The Existing `compute_score_impact` Function

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/impact.py`

This is the exact function you need. Here it is in full:

```python
def compute_score_impact(
    dimension_scores: dict,
    potentials: dict[str, int],
    detector: str,
    issues_to_fix: int,
) -> float:
    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
    # 1. Find which dimension this detector belongs to
    target_dim = None
    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
        if detector in dim.detectors:
            target_dim = dim
            break
    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
        return 0.0

    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    if potential <= 0:
        return 0.0

    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)  # current overall score

    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
    if not det_data:
        return 0.0

    # 2. Simulate removing N issues (each weighted at 1.0)
    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)

    # 3. Recompute the dimension score with reduced failures
    total_potential = 0
    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
    for det in target_dim.detectors:
        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
        if not det_values:
            continue
        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
        if det == detector:
            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
        else:
            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
    if total_potential <= 0:
        return 0.0

    new_dim_score = max(0.0, (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential) * 100

    # 4. Recompute overall score with simulated dimension score
    simulated = {name: dict(data) for name, data in dimension_scores.items()}
    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
```

**Key limitation:** On line 41, each issue is assumed to reduce `weighted_failures` by exactly 1.0. This is a simplification -- in reality, issues have confidence-based weights (HIGH=1.0, MEDIUM=0.7, LOW=0.3). For file-based detectors with per-file caps, the real effect is even more complex.

### 6. How It's Currently Used

The function is called in three places:

1. **`next` command render** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`, line 169): Shows impact of fixing 1 item; falls back to bulk impact for all issues in the detector.

2. **`status` command render** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_parts/render.py`, line 167): Shows focus dimension with total impact.

3. **Narrative action engine** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/action_engine.py`, line 54): Builds an impact estimator closure for action prioritization.

4. **Narrative dimensions** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/narrative/dimensions.py`, line 145): Estimates max impact across detectors in a dimension.

### 7. The Derivable Formula for Marginal Impact

Given the full scoring pipeline, here is the closed-form marginal impact of fixing one finding:

**For a mechanical dimension:**

```
per_point_contribution = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION * (effective_weight / total_mech_weight)
```

Where `effective_weight = configured_weight * min(1.0, checks / 200)`.

The dimension score change from fixing one finding:
```
delta_dim_score = (finding_weighted_failure / total_checks_in_dimension) * 100
```

Where `finding_weighted_failure` is the confidence weight of the finding (1.0/0.7/0.3), subject to file-based caps.

The overall score change:
```
delta_overall = per_point_contribution * delta_dim_score
```

Expanded:
```
delta_overall = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION 
                * (effective_weight / total_mech_weight) 
                * (finding_weight / total_checks) 
                * 100
```

**For a subjective dimension:**

```
per_point_contribution = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION * (configured_weight / total_subj_weight)
```

But subjective dimension scores are set by assessment import, not by finding resolution. Resolving review/concerns findings does NOT change the subjective dimension score (this is explicitly stated in the code). Only a fresh review import updates subjective scores.

### 8. Important Caveats for Your Queue Prioritization Overhaul

1. **The existing `compute_score_impact` already does what you need** -- it simulates fixing N issues in a detector and recomputes the full overall score. The approach is a full simulation, not an approximation.

2. **The 1.0-weight-per-issue assumption** (line 41 in impact.py) means the function overestimates impact for LOW-confidence findings (which only contribute 0.3 weighted failures) and slightly overestimates for MEDIUM (0.7). This is the main inaccuracy you might want to fix.

3. **Review/concerns findings are excluded from mechanical scoring** (detection.py line 145-146: `if detector in ("review", "concerns"): return (1.0, 0, 0.0)`). They only affect subjective scores indirectly through assessment imports.

4. **The health breakdown** (`compute_health_breakdown` in health.py) already computes `overall_drag` per dimension (lines 146-147):
   ```python
   "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score)
   ```
   This tells you the total drag each dimension exerts on the overall score. If you fix ALL issues in a dimension, the score would increase by approximately `overall_drag` points (modulo rounding and reweighting effects).

5. **The `per_point` value** (line 132) tells you how much 1 point of improvement in a dimension is worth in overall score points. This is the most useful single number for marginal impact estimation:
   ```python
   per_point = pool_fraction * pool_share
   # where pool_share = effective_weight / sum(all_effective_weights_in_pool)
   ```

6. **Sample dampening** (MIN_SAMPLE=200) means dimensions with fewer than 200 checks have proportionally reduced weight. A dimension with only 50 checks has `sample_factor = 0.25`, so its weight is quartered.

7. **For a work queue item**, the cleanest marginal impact formula is:
   ```
   marginal_impact = per_point * (finding_weighted_failure / total_dimension_checks) * 100
   ```
   Where `per_point` comes from the health breakdown and `finding_weighted_failure` is the actual confidence-adjusted weight of the specific finding being considered.

## Complete Work Queue Ordering Consumer Analysis

### Architecture Overview

The ordering system is layered:

1. **`item_sort_key`** (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py:81`) -- the fundamental sort function. Produces a tuple that implements the priority cascade: initial-review subjective (-3) > workflow stages (-2) > workflow actions (-1) > clusters (0) > findings/subjective (1), with sub-sorting by confidence, review weight, count, and ID.

2. **`build_work_queue`** (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:110`) -- the central queue builder. Calls `item_sort_key` at line 167, then optionally applies plan ordering, cluster collapsing, and subjective interleaving.

3. **`_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail`** (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:66`) -- post-sort pass that interleaves subjective/objective items in a 3:1 burst pattern, preserving workflow items at the front.

4. **`apply_plan_order`** (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py:24`) -- reorders items per the living plan's `queue_order`, placing user-ordered items first, then remaining existing items, then new items.

5. **`collapse_clusters`** (in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py:118`) -- replaces cluster member items with meta-items. Uses `item_sort_key` to re-sort the result at line 188.

---

### 1. `build_work_queue` -- All Call Sites

**A. `next` command** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py:198`)
```python
queue = build_work_queue(
    state,
    options=QueueBuildOptions(
        count=count,              # user-specified, default 1
        scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
        scope=scope,
        status=status,            # default "open"
        include_subjective=True,
        subjective_threshold=target_strict,
        explain=explain,
        plan=plan_data,           # living plan if active
        include_skipped=include_skipped,
        cluster=effective_cluster,
    ),
)
```
**Ordering sensitivity: HIGH.** This is the primary consumer for "what to work on next." The first item in the returned list is what the user sees. Changing sort order directly changes the user's workflow recommendation. Cares about: plan ordering, cluster collapsing, subjective interleaving, and the full `item_sort_key` priority cascade.

**B. `show` command -- `load_matches`** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/scope.py:198`)

```python
        include_subjective=False,
        chronic=chronic,

)
```
**Ordering sensitivity: LOW.** Returns all findings for a pattern/scope, then renders them. The ordering within the result set affects display order but not functional behavior. Notably, no plan and no subjective items are included.

**C. `plan queue` command** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py:156`)

### 7. `next` Command Queue Usage

`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py`

The `next` command is the **primary ordering-sensitive consumer**. In `_get_items` (line 170):
- Loads the living plan
- Resolves cluster focus (auto-scopes to `active_cluster` if set)
- Calls `build_work_queue` with full options: plan, cluster, scope, count, subjective threshold, explain
- Uses the result's items list directly -- **position matters**
- The first item(s) are what gets rendered as "do this next"
- Renders queue header, items, resolution hints, and followup nudges
- Also writes the queue to `query.json` for downstream consumption

Any change to the ordering algorithm would directly change what the `next` command recommends.

- `include_subjective=False`

The resulting items are then filtered through a noise budget system and rendered. Ordering within the show output affects display order but is not functionally critical -- the command is about showing all matches for a pattern, not prioritizing.

6. **`tests/plan/test_auto_cluster.py`** -- cluster sort key ordering tests
7. **`tests/commands/test_queue_order_guard.py`** -- depends on "front" item identity

**LOW (display order only):**

**NONE (counting only):**

11. **`queue_progress` helpers** -- only counts
12. **`review/preflight`** -- only counts
13. **`build_open_plan_queue`** -- not called in production (dead code)

> DEVELOPER

Design a plan to overhaul the work queue prioritization system in desloppify. The goal is to **simplify** the system from 5 layers down to 3, and make it **impact-aware** so items that would improve the overall health score the most are surfaced first.

## Current Architecture (5 layers, overly complex)

The queue is built in `engine/_work_queue/core.py:build_work_queue()`:

**Layer 1: `item_sort_key` in `ranking.py`** — sorts items into priority tiers:
- Tier -3: Unassessed subjective dimensions (initial review)  
- Tier -2: Synthesis workflow stages
- Tier -1: Workflow actions
- Tier 0: Collapsed clusters  
- Tier 1, sub-tier 0: **Mechanical findings** (always before subjective)
- Tier 1, sub-tier 1: **Subjective findings/dimensions** (always after mechanical)

**Layer 2: `apply_plan_order` in `plan_order.py`** — places items in explicit plan `queue_order` positions, overriding algorithmic sort.

**Layer 3: `collapse_clusters` in `plan_order.py`** — replaces cluster members with meta-items, then **re-sorts everything with `item_sort_key`**, destroying plan order.

**Layer 4: `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail` in `core.py`** — patches the bad mechanical-first ordering by interleaving 3 objective : 1 subjective.

**Layer 5: `backlog_gated` suppression in `core.py`** — completely removes stale subjective items from queue when ANY mechanical findings exist.

## The Problem

Scoring weights: 60% subjective, 40% mechanical. But the queue puts mechanical first unconditionally. With mechanical at 96.5% and subjective at ~50%, mechanical items yield ~0.0 score impact while subjective items have massive headroom. The interleave guardrail was a patch for this but still gives only 25% of slots to subjective items.

## Key Existing Infrastructure

1. **`compute_health_breakdown`** in `engine/_scoring/results/health.py` — already computes `per_point` (how much 1% of dimension improvement is worth in overall score) and `headroom` (100 - score) for each dimension. This is exactly what we need.

2. **`compute_score_impact`** in `engine/_scoring/results/impact.py` — does full simulation of fixing N issues in a detector. Too slow for sort key computation but useful for reference.

3. **CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS** in `engine/_scoring/detection.py` — `{HIGH: 1.0, MEDIUM: 0.7, LOW: 0.3}`

4. **DIMENSIONS** registry in `engine/_scoring/policy/` — maps detectors to dimensions.

## Ordering-Sensitive Consumers

Only 3 consumers care about ordering:
- `next` command (`app/commands/next.py`) — recommends what to work on
- `resolve/queue_guard` (`app/commands/resolve/queue_guard.py`) — enforces front-of-queue resolution
- `plan queue` command (`app/commands/plan/queue_render.py`) — displays queue with positions

All others (status, preflight, counting) only use totals/counts.

## Proposed New Architecture (3 layers)

### Layer 1: Impact-aware `item_sort_key`

Before sorting, enrich each item with an `estimated_impact` float:
- For mechanical findings: `per_point * (confidence_weight / total_dimension_checks) * 100`
- For subjective dimensions: `per_point * headroom` (total improvement potential)
- For subjective findings (review): `per_point * headroom / open_findings_in_dim`

Then change `item_sort_key` tier 1 to:
```python
# OLD: (1, 0/1, ...) — mechanical before subjective
# NEW: (1, -estimated_impact, tiebreakers...)
```

Keep tiers -3, -2, -1, 0 unchanged — they handle workflow stages and clusters which have their own ordering logic.

### Layer 2: `apply_plan_order` (unchanged)

Explicit plan ordering still overrides the algorithmic sort. This is correct behavior — user intent trumps automation.

### Layer 3: `collapse_clusters` (fixed — no re-sort)

Instead of building a new list and re-sorting, walk items in order and replace the first member of each cluster with the cluster meta-item, skipping subsequent members. This preserves impact-aware ordering.

### Removed: Interleave guardrail

No longer needed. Impact-aware sort naturally interleaves based on actual score impact.

### Removed: `backlog_gated` suppression

No longer needed. If a stale subjective dimension has high impact, it should appear in the queue. The review preflight may still block the action, but at least the user sees the priority correctly.

## Files to Modify

1. **`engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`** — Add `_enrich_with_impact()` function. Change tier 1 in `item_sort_key` to use `-estimated_impact`. Update `item_explain` to show impact.

2. **`engine/_work_queue/core.py`** — Call `_enrich_with_impact()` before sorting. Remove `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail` call. Remove `backlog_gated` suppression logic. Remove `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail` function.

3. **`engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`** — Rewrite `collapse_clusters` to preserve order (walk-and-replace instead of append-and-resort).

4. **`engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`** — Remove `backlog_gated` field from `build_subjective_items`. Update `build_create_plan_item` and `build_synthesis_stage_items` if needed.

5. **Tests** — Update ordering assertions in `tests/review/work_queue_cases.py`, `tests/core/test_plan.py`, `tests/plan/test_auto_cluster.py`.

## Key Design Decisions to Present

1. **Fallback when no scoring data exists**: When `dimension_scores` is empty (first scan), `estimated_impact` should be 0 for all items, and the tiebreakers (confidence, count) provide reasonable default ordering.

2. **Cluster impact**: Cluster meta-items inherit the max `estimated_impact` of their members, so they appear at the right position.

3. **Performance**: `compute_health_breakdown` is called once at queue build time. The per-item impact computation is O(1) after that. No impact on performance.

Please design a concrete, detailed implementation plan with specific code changes for each file. Keep it as simple as possible — the user emphasized simplicity.

```
    66→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    67→    items: list[dict],
    68→    *,
    69→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    70→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    71→) -> list[dict]:
    72→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation.
    73→
    74→    Workflow items (synthesis stages, create-plan) are excluded from the
    75→    interleave and prepended — they maintain their natural high-priority sort.
    76→    """
    77→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    78→        return items
    79→
    80→    workflow_items: list[dict] = []
    81→    subjective_items: deque[dict] = deque()
    82→    objective_items: deque[dict] = deque()

    88→        else:

    96→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    97→            if not objective_items:
    98→                break
    99→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
   100→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
   101→            if not subjective_items:

   103→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())

   159→    # Inject synthesis stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   160→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   161→        synth_items = build_synthesis_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   162→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   163→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   164→        if plan_item is not None:
   165→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   166→
   167→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   168→
   169→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   170→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   171→    if resolved_options.plan:
   172→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   173→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   174→            all_items,
   175→            resolved_options.plan,
   176→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   177→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   178→            new_ids=new_ids,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   182→    should_collapse = (
   183→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   184→        and resolved_options.plan
   185→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   186→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   187→    )
   188→    if should_collapse:
   189→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   190→
   191→    should_interleave = (
   192→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   193→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   194→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   195→        and not resolved_options.scope
   196→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   197→    )
   198→    if should_interleave:
   199→        all_items = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(all_items)
   200→
   201→    total = len(all_items)
   202→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   203→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   204→
   205→    if resolved_options.explain:
   206→        for item in all_items:
   207→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   208→
   209→    return {
   210→        "items": all_items,
   211→        "total": total,
   212→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   213→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   214→        "suppressed_count": suppressed_count,
   215→    }
   216→
   217→
   218→__all__ = [
   219→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   220→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   221→    "WorkQueueResult",
   222→    "build_work_queue",
   223→    "group_queue_items",
   224→]
   225→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    subjective_strict_scores,
    16→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    19→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    20→
    21→
    22→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    23→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    24→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    25→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    26→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    27→
    28→
    29→def build_finding_items(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    *,
    32→    scan_path: str | None,
    33→    status_filter: str,
    34→    scope: str | None,
    35→    chronic: bool,
    36→) -> list[dict]:
    37→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    38→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    39→    out: list[dict] = []
    40→
    41→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    42→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    43→            continue
    44→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    45→            continue
    46→        if chronic and not (
    47→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    48→        ):
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        item = dict(finding)
    52→        item["id"] = finding_id
    53→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    54→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    55→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    56→        item["review_weight"] = (
    57→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    58→        )
    59→        subjective_score = None
    60→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    61→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    62→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    63→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    64→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    65→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    66→            )
    67→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    68→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    69→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    70→            item,
    71→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    72→        )
    73→
    74→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    75→            continue
    76→        out.append(item)
    77→
    78→    return out
    79→
    80→
    81→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    82→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    83→
    84→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
    86→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
    87→
    88→    # Synthesis stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
    89→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
    90→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
    91→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
    92→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
    93→
    94→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
    95→    if kind == "workflow_action":
    96→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    97→
    98→    if kind == "cluster":
    99→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   100→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   101→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   102→        )
   103→        return (
   104→            0,
   105→            action_pri,
   106→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   107→            item.get("id", ""),
   108→        )
   109→
   110→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   111→        return (
   112→            1,
   113→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
   114→            subjective_score_value(item),
   115→            item.get("id", ""),
   116→        )
   117→
   118→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   119→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   120→    return (
   121→        1,
   122→        0,
   123→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   124→        -review_weight,
   125→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   126→        item.get("id", ""),
   127→    )
   128→
   129→
   130→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   131→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   132→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   133→        return {
   134→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   135→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   136→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   137→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   138→            "policy": "Synthesis stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   139→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   140→        }
   141→
   142→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   143→        return {
   144→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   145→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after synthesis stages, before findings.",
   146→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   147→        }
   148→
   149→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   150→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   151→        return {
   152→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   153→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   154→            "initial_review": initial,
   155→            "policy": (
   156→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   157→                if initial else
   158→                "Subjective dimensions sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   159→            ),
   160→            "ranking_factors": ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   161→        }
   162→
   163→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   164→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   165→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   166→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   167→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   168→    if is_subjective:
   169→        ranking_factors = ["subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   170→    elif is_review:
   171→        ranking_factors = [
   172→            "confidence asc",
   173→            "review_weight desc",
   174→            "count desc",
   175→            "id asc",
   176→        ]
   177→    else:
   178→        ranking_factors = ["confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   179→    explain = {
   180→        "kind": "finding",
   181→        "confidence": confidence,
   182→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   183→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   184→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   185→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   186→    }
   187→    if is_review:
   188→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   189→    if is_subjective:
   190→        explain["policy"] = (
   191→            "Subjective findings sort after mechanical items at the same level."
   192→        )
   193→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   194→    return explain
   195→
   196→
   197→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   198→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   199→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   200→    for item in items:
   201→        if group == "file":
   202→            key = item.get("file", "")
   203→        elif group == "detector":
   204→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   205→        elif group == "cluster":
   206→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   207→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   208→        else:
   209→            key = "items"
   210→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   211→    return grouped
   212→
   213→
   214→__all__ = [
   215→    "build_finding_items",
   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",
   218→    "subjective_score_value",
   219→    "group_queue_items",
   220→]
   221→
```

```
1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import item_sort_key
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     7→
     8→
     9→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    10→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    11→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    12→    if not scan_history:
    13→        return set()
    14→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    15→    if not threshold:
    16→        return set()
    17→    return {
    18→        finding_id
    19→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    20→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def apply_plan_order(
    25→    items: list[dict],
    26→    plan: dict,
    27→    *,
    28→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    29→    cluster: str | None = None,
    30→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    31→) -> list[dict]:
    32→    """Reorder items according to the living plan."""
    33→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    34→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    35→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    36→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    37→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    38→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    39→
    40→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    41→    for item in items:
    42→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    43→
    44→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    45→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    46→        if override.get("description"):
    47→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    48→        if override.get("note"):
    49→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    50→        if override.get("cluster"):
    51→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    52→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    53→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    54→                "name": cluster_name,
    55→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    56→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    57→            }
    58→
    59→    ordered: list[dict] = []
    60→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
    61→    for finding_id in queue_order:
    62→        if finding_id in by_id and finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    63→            ordered.append(by_id[finding_id])
    64→            ordered_ids.add(finding_id)
    65→
    66→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
    67→    remaining_existing: list[dict] = []
    68→    remaining_new: list[dict] = []
    69→    _new = new_ids or set()
    70→    for item in items:
    71→        item_id = item["id"]
    72→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
    73→            continue
    74→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
    75→            skipped_items.append(item)
    76→        elif item_id in _new:
    77→            remaining_new.append(item)
    78→        else:
    79→            remaining_existing.append(item)
    80→
    81→    result = ordered + remaining_existing + remaining_new
    82→    if include_skipped:
    83→        result = result + skipped_items
    84→
    85→    for position, item in enumerate(result):
    86→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
    87→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    88→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
    89→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
    90→            if skip_entry:
    91→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
    92→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
    93→                if skip_reason:
    94→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
    95→
    96→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
    97→    if effective_cluster:
    98→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
    99→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   100→        if cluster_member_ids:
   101→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   102→    return result
   103→
   104→
   105→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   106→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   107→    try:
   108→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   109→
   110→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   111→        if meta:
   112→            return meta.action_type
   113→    except ImportError as exc:
   114→        _ = exc
   115→    return "manual_fix"
   116→
   117→
   118→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   119→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   120→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   121→    if not clusters:
   122→        return items
   123→
   124→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   125→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   126→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   127→            continue
   128→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   129→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   130→
   131→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   132→        return items
   133→
   134→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   135→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   136→    for item in items:
   137→        cluster_name = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   138→        if cluster_name:
   139→            cluster_members.setdefault(cluster_name, []).append(item)
   140→        else:
   141→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   142→
   143→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   144→    for cluster_name, members in cluster_members.items():
   145→        if len(members) < 2:
   146→            result.extend(members)
   147→            continue
   148→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   149→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   150→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   151→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   152→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   153→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   154→            action_type = "reorganize"
   155→        else:
   156→            action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   157→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   158→                action_type = "refactor"
   159→
   160→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   161→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   162→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   163→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   164→        else:
   165→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   166→
   167→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   168→        if not primary_command:
   169→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   170→
   171→        meta_item: dict = {
   172→            "id": cluster_name,
   173→            "kind": "cluster",
   174→            "action_type": action_type,
   175→            "summary": summary,
   176→            "members": members,
   177→            "member_count": len(members),
   178→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   179→            "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   180→            "cluster_auto": True,
   181→            "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   182→            "confidence": "high",
   183→            "detector": detector,
   184→            "file": "",
   185→        }
   186→        result.append(meta_item)
   187→
   188→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   189→    return result
   190→
   191→
   192→__all__ = ["apply_plan_order", "collapse_clusters", "new_item_ids"]
   193→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    21→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    22→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    23→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    51→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    52→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    53→    weight_by_confidence = {
    54→        "high": 1.0,
    55→        "medium": 0.7,
    56→        "low": 0.3,
    57→    }
    58→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    59→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    60→        weight *= 10.0
    61→    return float(weight)
    62→
    63→
    64→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    65→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    66→    if not scope:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    70→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    71→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    72→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    73→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    74→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    75→
    76→    if "*" in scope:
    77→        return any(
    78→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    79→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    80→        )
    81→
    82→    if "::" in scope:
    83→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    84→
    85→    lowered = scope.lower()
    86→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        return (
    88→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    89→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    90→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    91→        )
    92→
    93→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    94→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    95→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    96→
    97→    return (
    98→        detector == scope
    99→        or filepath == scope
   100→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   101→    )
   102→
   103→
   104→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   105→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   106→
   107→
   108→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   109→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   110→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   111→    target = cleaned.lower()
   112→
   113→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   114→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   115→            return str(dim_key)
   116→    return slugify(cleaned)
   117→
   118→
   119→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   120→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   121→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   122→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   123→    return {
   124→        cleaned.lower(),
   125→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   126→        slugify(cleaned),
   127→        canonical.lower(),
   128→        slugify(canonical),
   129→    }
   130→
   131→
   132→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   133→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   134→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   135→    if not lang:
   136→        return None
   137→
   138→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   139→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   140→        return None
   141→
   142→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   143→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   144→        return None
   145→
   146→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   147→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   148→        return None
   149→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   150→
   151→
   152→def primary_command_for_finding(
   153→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   154→) -> str:
   155→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   156→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   157→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   158→        available_fixers = [
   159→            fixer
   160→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   161→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   162→        ]
   163→        if available_fixers:
   164→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   165→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   166→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   167→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   168→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   169→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   170→
   171→
   172→def build_synthesis_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``synthesis::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   174→
   175→    Returns an empty list when no synthesis stages are pending.
   176→    """
   177→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesis_playbook import (
   178→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   179→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS,
   180→    )
   181→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   182→        SYNTHESIS_IDS,
   183→        SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS,
   184→    )
   185→
   186→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   187→    order_set = set(order)
   188→    present = order_set & SYNTHESIS_IDS
   189→    if not present:
   190→        return []
   191→
   192→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   193→    confirmed = set(meta.get("synthesis_stages", {}).keys())
   194→
   195→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   196→    open_review_count = sum(
   197→        1 for f in findings.values()
   198→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   199→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   200→    )
   201→
   202→    label_map = dict(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS)
   203→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   204→
   205→    items: list[dict] = []
   206→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(SYNTHESIS_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   207→        if sid not in present:
   208→            continue
   209→        if name in confirmed:
   210→            continue
   211→
   212→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   213→        deps = SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   214→        blocked_by = sorted(
   215→            f"synthesis::{dep}" for dep in deps
   216→            if f"synthesis::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   217→        )
   218→
   219→        cmd = f"desloppify plan synthesize --stage {name}"
   220→        if name == "commit":
   221→            cmd = 'desloppify plan synthesize --complete --strategy "..."'
   222→
   223→        items.append({
   224→            "id": sid,
   225→            "tier": 1,
   226→            "confidence": "high",
   227→            "detector": "synthesis",
   228→            "file": ".",
   229→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   230→            "stage_name": name,
   231→            "stage_index": idx,
   232→            "summary": f"Planning: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   233→            "detail": {
   234→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   235→                "stage": name,
   236→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   237→            },
   238→            "primary_command": cmd,
   239→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   240→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   241→        })
   242→    return items
   243→
   244→
   245→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> dict | None:
   246→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if it's in the queue.
   247→
   248→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   249→    """
   250→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID
   251→
   252→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   253→        return None
   254→
   255→    return {
   256→        "id": WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   257→        "tier": 1,
   258→        "confidence": "high",
   259→        "detector": "workflow",
   260→        "file": ".",
   261→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   262→        "summary": "Create prioritized plan from review results",
   263→        "detail": {},
   264→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan",
   265→        "blocked_by": [],
   266→        "is_blocked": False,
   267→    }
   268→
   269→
   270→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   271→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   272→    if not dim_scores:
   273→        return {}
   274→
   275→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   276→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   277→    for entry in entries:
   278→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   279→        if not name:
   280→            continue
   281→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   282→        [REDACTED](name)
   283→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   284→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   285→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   286→            if not key:
   287→                continue
   288→            aliases.add(key)
   289→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   290→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   291→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   292→        for alias in aliases:
   293→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   294→    return scores
   295→
   296→
   297→def _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   298→    """Build scorecard-like entries for unassessed placeholder dimensions.
   299→
   300→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` goes through the scorecard pipeline which
   301→    intentionally hides placeholders from display.  This function reads
   302→    ``dimension_scores`` directly so initial-review items are never lost.
   303→    """
   304→    entries: list[dict] = []
   305→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   306→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   307→            continue
   308→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   309→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   310→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   311→            continue
   312→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   313→            continue
   314→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   315→        entries.append(
   316→            {
   317→                "name": name,
   318→                "score": float(data.get("score", 0.0)),
   319→                "strict": float(data.get("strict", 0.0)),
   320→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   321→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   322→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   323→                "placeholder": True,
   324→                "stale": False,
   325→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   326→                "cli_keys": [dim_key] if dim_key else [],
   327→            }
   328→        )
   329→    return entries
   330→
   331→
   332→def build_subjective_items(
   333→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   334→) -> list[dict]:
   335→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   336→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   337→    if not dim_scores:
   338→        return []
   339→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   340→
   341→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   342→
   343→    # The scorecard pipeline hides unassessed placeholders from display.
   344→    # Merge them in so initial-review items always appear in the queue.
   345→    seen_dims: set[str] = {
   346→        str(e.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   347→        for e in subjective_entries
   348→        if e.get("dimension_key")
   349→    }
   350→    for entry in _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores):
   351→        dk = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   352→        if dk and dk not in seen_dims:
   353→            subjective_entries.append(entry)
   354→            seen_dims.add(dk)
   355→
   356→    if not subjective_entries:
   357→        return []
   358→    unassessed_dims = {
   359→        str(name).strip()
   360→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   361→            dim_scores
   362→        )
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   366→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   367→    for finding in findings.values():
   368→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   369→            continue
   370→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   371→        if not dim_key:
   372→            continue
   373→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   374→
   375→    items: list[dict] = []
   376→    def _prepare_command(
   377→        cli_keys: list[str],
   378→        *,
   379→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   380→    ) -> str:
   381→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   382→        if cli_keys:
   383→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   384→        if force_review_rerun:
   385→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   386→        return command
   387→
   388→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   389→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   390→        if not name:
   391→            continue
   392→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   394→            continue
   395→
   396→        [REDACTED](name)
   397→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   398→        cli_keys = [
   399→            str(key).strip().lower()
   400→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   401→            if str(key).strip()
   402→        ]
   403→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   404→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   405→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   406→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   407→            name in unassessed_dims
   408→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   409→        )
   410→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   411→        # Items whose only action is --force-review-rerun are gated by the
   412→        # review preflight while objective backlog exists.  Flag them so the
   413→        # queue builder can suppress them until the backlog drains.
   414→        is_backlog_gated = open_review == 0 and not is_unassessed
   415→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   416→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   417→        if open_review > 0:
   418→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   419→        elif is_unassessed:
   420→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   421→        elif is_stale:
   422→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   423→                cli_keys,
   424→                force_review_rerun=True,
   425→            )
   426→        elif cli_keys:
   427→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   428→                cli_keys,
   429→                force_review_rerun=True,
   430→            )
   431→        else:
   432→            primary_command = _prepare_command(
   433→                cli_keys,
   434→                force_review_rerun=True,
   435→            )
   436→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   437→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   438→        items.append(
   439→            {
   440→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   441→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   442→                "file": ".",
   443→                "confidence": "medium",
   444→                "summary": summary,
   445→                "detail": {
   446→                    "dimension_name": name,
   447→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   448→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   449→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   450→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   451→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   452→                },
   453→                "status": "open",
   454→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   455→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   456→                "backlog_gated": is_backlog_gated,
   457→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   458→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   459→            }
   460→        )
   461→    return items
   462→
   463→
   464→__all__ = [
   465→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   466→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   467→    "build_create_plan_item",
   468→    "build_subjective_items",
   469→    "build_synthesis_stage_items",
   470→    "detail_dict",
```

```
    15→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
    16→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
    17→
    18→
    19→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:

    28→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
    29→    subjective_meta = (

    31→        if isinstance(data, dict)
    32→        else {}
    33→    )

    36→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)

    38→    )

    42→    return float(
```
