    50→def compute_health_breakdown(
    51→    dimension_scores: dict,
    52→    *,
    53→    score_key: str = "score",
    54→) -> dict[str, object]:
    55→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
    56→    if not dimension_scores:
    57→        return {
    58→            "overall_score": 100.0,
    59→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
    60→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
    61→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
    62→            "subjective_avg": None,
    63→            "entries": [],
    64→        }
    65→
    66→    mech_sum = 0.0
    67→    mech_weight = 0.0
    68→    subj_sum = 0.0
    69→    subj_weight = 0.0
    70→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    71→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
    72→
    73→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
    74→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
    75→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
    76→        if is_subjective:
    77→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
    78→            effective = configured
    79→            subj_sum += score * effective
    80→            subj_weight += effective
    81→            subjective_rows.append(
    82→                {
    83→                    "name": str(name),
    84→                    "score": score,
    85→                    "configured_weight": configured,
    86→                    "effective_weight": effective,
    87→                }
    88→            )
    89→            continue
    90→
    91→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
    92→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
    93→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
    94→        effective = configured * sample_factor
    95→        mech_sum += score * effective
    96→        mech_weight += effective
    97→        mechanical_rows.append(
    98→            {
    99→                "name": str(name),
   100→                "score": score,
   101→                "checks": checks,
   102→                "sample_factor": sample_factor,
   103→                "configured_weight": configured,
   104→                "effective_weight": effective,
   105→            }
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   109→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   110→
   111→    if subj_avg is None:
   112→        mechanical_fraction = 1.0
   113→        subjective_fraction = 0.0
   114→        overall_score = round(mech_avg, 1)
   115→    elif mech_weight == 0:
   116→        mechanical_fraction = 0.0
   117→        subjective_fraction = 1.0
   118→        overall_score = round(subj_avg, 1)
   119→    else:
   120→        mechanical_fraction = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   121→        subjective_fraction = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   122→        overall_score = round(
   123→            mech_avg * mechanical_fraction + subj_avg * subjective_fraction,
   124→            1,
   125→        )
   126→
   127→    entries: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []

   130→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / mech_weight if mech_weight > 0 else 0.0
   131→        )
   132→        per_point = mechanical_fraction * pool_share
   133→        score = float(row["score"])

   136→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   137→                "pool": "mechanical",
   138→                "score": score,

   153→        )
   154→        per_point = subjective_fraction * pool_share

   159→                "pool": "subjective",

   162→                "sample_factor": 1.0,

   165→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   166→                "overall_per_point": per_point,

   168→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   169→            }
   170→        )
   171→
   172→    return {

   182→def compute_health_score(
   183→    dimension_scores: dict,
   184→    *,
   185→    score_key: str = "score",
   186→) -> float:
   187→    """Budget-weighted blend of mechanical and subjective dimension scores."""
   188→    return float(
   189→        compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key=score_key)[
   190→            "overall_score"
   191→        ]
   192→    )
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = ["compute_health_breakdown", "compute_health_score"]
   196→
   197→

1→"""Detector-to-score impact helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     6→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES,
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
     9→    Dimension,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_score
    12→
    13→
    14→def compute_score_impact(
    15→    dimension_scores: dict,
    16→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    17→    detector: str,
    18→    issues_to_fix: int,
    19→) -> float:
    20→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
    21→    target_dim = None
    22→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    23→        if detector in dim.detectors:
    24→            target_dim = dim
    25→            break
    26→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
    27→        return 0.0
    28→
    29→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    30→    if potential <= 0:
    31→        return 0.0
    32→
    33→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
    34→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
    35→
    36→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
    37→    if not det_data:
    38→        return 0.0
    39→
    40→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
    41→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
    42→
    43→    total_potential = 0
    44→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
    45→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
    46→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
    47→        if not det_values:
    48→            continue
    49→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
    50→        if det == detector:
    51→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
    52→        else:
    53→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
    54→    if total_potential <= 0:
    55→        return 0.0
    56→
    57→    new_dim_score = (
    58→        max(
    59→            0.0,
    60→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
    61→        )
    62→        * 100
    63→    )
    64→
    65→    simulated = {name: dict(data) for name, data in dimension_scores.items()}
    66→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
    67→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
    68→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
    69→
    70→
    71→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
    72→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
    73→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
    74→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
    75→        return None
    76→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
    77→
    78→
    79→__all__ = ["compute_score_impact", "get_dimension_for_detector"]
    80→
    81→

1→"""Dimension and overall scoring aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import detector_stats_by_mode
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     9→    DIMENSIONS,
    10→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    11→    SCORING_MODES,
    12→    ScoreMode,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import (
    15→    compute_health_breakdown,
    16→    compute_health_score,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import (
    19→    compute_score_impact,
    20→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import (
    23→    append_subjective_dimensions,
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class ScoreBundle:
    29→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    30→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    overall_score: float
    33→    objective_score: float
    34→    strict_score: float
    35→    verified_strict_score: float
    36→
    37→
    38→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    39→    findings: dict,
    40→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    41→    *,
    42→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    43→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    44→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    45→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    46→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    47→
    48→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    49→        totals = {
    50→            mode: {
    51→                "checks": 0,
    52→                "issues": 0,
    53→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    54→                "detectors": {},
    55→            }
    56→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    57→        }
    58→
    59→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    60→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    61→            if potential <= 0:
    62→                continue
    63→
    64→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    65→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    66→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    67→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    68→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    69→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    70→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    71→                    "potential": potential,
    72→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    73→                    "issues": issues,
    74→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    75→                }
    76→
    77→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    78→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    79→            if total_checks <= 0:
    80→                continue
    81→            dim_score = (
    82→                max(
    83→                    0.0,
    84→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    85→                )
    86→                * 100
    87→            )
    88→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    89→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    90→                "tier": dim.tier,
    91→                "checks": total_checks,
    92→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    93→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    94→            }
    95→
    96→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    97→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    98→            results[mode],
    99→            findings,
   100→            subjective_assessments,
   101→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   102→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   103→        )
   104→    return results
   105→
   106→
   107→def compute_dimension_scores(
   108→    findings: dict,
   109→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   110→    *,
   111→    strict: bool = False,
   112→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   113→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   114→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   115→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   116→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   117→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   118→        findings,
   119→        potentials,
   120→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   121→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   122→    )[mode]
   123→
   124→
   125→def compute_score_bundle(
   126→    findings: dict,
   127→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   128→    *,
   129→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   130→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   131→) -> ScoreBundle:
   132→    """Compute all score channels from one scoring engine pass."""
   133→    by_mode = compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   134→        findings,
   135→        potentials,
   136→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   137→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    lenient_scores = by_mode["lenient"]
   141→    strict_scores = by_mode["strict"]
   142→    verified_strict_scores = by_mode["verified_strict"]
   143→
   144→    mechanical_lenient_scores = {
   145→        name: data
   146→        for name, data in lenient_scores.items()
   147→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   148→    }
   149→
   150→    return ScoreBundle(
   151→        dimension_scores=lenient_scores,
   152→        strict_dimension_scores=strict_scores,
   153→        verified_strict_dimension_scores=verified_strict_scores,
   154→        overall_score=compute_health_score(lenient_scores),
   155→        objective_score=compute_health_score(mechanical_lenient_scores),
   156→        strict_score=compute_health_score(strict_scores),
   157→        verified_strict_score=compute_health_score(verified_strict_scores),
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→__all__ = [
   162→    "ScoreBundle",
   163→    "compute_dimension_scores_by_mode",
   164→    "compute_dimension_scores",
   165→    "compute_health_breakdown",
   166→    "compute_health_score",
   167→    "compute_score_bundle",
   168→    "compute_score_impact",
   169→    "get_dimension_for_detector",
   170→]
   171→

1→"""Scoring policies, detector mappings, and shared constants."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Literal
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import Confidence, Tier
     9→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES
    10→
    11→ScoreMode = Literal["lenient", "strict", "verified_strict"]
    12→SCORING_MODES: tuple[ScoreMode, ...] = ("lenient", "strict", "verified_strict")
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class Dimension:
    17→    name: str
    18→    tier: int
    19→    detectors: list[str]
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class DetectorScoringPolicy:
    24→    detector: str
    25→    dimension: str | None
    26→    tier: int | None
    27→    file_based: bool = False
    28→    use_loc_weight: bool = False
    29→    excluded_zones: frozenset[str] = frozenset(EXCLUDED_ZONE_VALUES)
    30→
    31→
    32→# Security findings are excluded in non-production zones.
    33→SECURITY_EXCLUDED_ZONES = frozenset({"test", "config", "generated", "vendor"})
    34→
    35→# Central scoring policy for each detector: dimension/tier assignment,
    36→# weighting mode, and zone exclusions.
    37→DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES: dict[str, DetectorScoringPolicy] = {
    38→    # File health
    39→    "structural": DetectorScoringPolicy("structural", "File health", 3),
    40→    # Code quality
    41→    "unused": DetectorScoringPolicy("unused", "Code quality", 3),
    42→    "logs": DetectorScoringPolicy("logs", "Code quality", 3),
    43→    "exports": DetectorScoringPolicy("exports", "Code quality", 3),
    44→    "deprecated": DetectorScoringPolicy("deprecated", "Code quality", 3),
    45→    "props": DetectorScoringPolicy("props", "Code quality", 3),
    46→    "smells": DetectorScoringPolicy("smells", "Code quality", 3, file_based=True),
    47→    "react": DetectorScoringPolicy("react", "Code quality", 3),
    48→    "dict_keys": DetectorScoringPolicy("dict_keys", "Code quality", 3, file_based=True),
    49→    "global_mutable_config": DetectorScoringPolicy(
    50→        "global_mutable_config", "Code quality", 3
    51→    ),
    52→    "orphaned": DetectorScoringPolicy("orphaned", "Code quality", 3),
    53→    "flat_dirs": DetectorScoringPolicy("flat_dirs", "Code quality", 3),
    54→    "naming": DetectorScoringPolicy("naming", "Code quality", 3),
    55→    "facade": DetectorScoringPolicy("facade", "Code quality", 3),
    56→    "stale_exclude": DetectorScoringPolicy("stale_exclude", "Code quality", 3),
    57→    "patterns": DetectorScoringPolicy("patterns", "Code quality", 3),
    58→    "single_use": DetectorScoringPolicy("single_use", "Code quality", 3),
    59→    "coupling": DetectorScoringPolicy("coupling", "Code quality", 3),
    60→    "responsibility_cohesion": DetectorScoringPolicy(
    61→        "responsibility_cohesion", "Code quality", 3
    62→    ),
    63→    "private_imports": DetectorScoringPolicy("private_imports", "Code quality", 3),
    64→    "layer_violation": DetectorScoringPolicy("layer_violation", "Code quality", 3),
    65→    # Duplication
    66→    "dupes": DetectorScoringPolicy("dupes", "Duplication", 3),
    67→    "boilerplate_duplication": DetectorScoringPolicy(
    68→        "boilerplate_duplication", "Duplication", 3
    69→    ),
    70→    # Test health
    71→    "test_coverage": DetectorScoringPolicy(
    72→        "test_coverage", "Test health", 4, file_based=True, use_loc_weight=True
    73→    ),
    74→    "subjective_review": DetectorScoringPolicy(
    75→        "subjective_review", "Test health", 4, file_based=True
    76→    ),
    77→    # Security
    78→    "security": DetectorScoringPolicy(
    79→        "security",
    80→        "Security",
    81→        4,
    82→        file_based=True,
    83→        excluded_zones=SECURITY_EXCLUDED_ZONES,
    84→    ),
    85→    "cycles": DetectorScoringPolicy("cycles", "Security", 4),
    86→    # Design coherence (concerns confirmed by subjective review)
    87→    "concerns": DetectorScoringPolicy("concerns", None, None, file_based=True),
    88→    # Review findings are scored via subjective dimensions, not mechanical dimensions.
    89→    "review": DetectorScoringPolicy("review", None, None, file_based=True),
    90→}
    91→_BASE_DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES: dict[str, DetectorScoringPolicy] = dict(
    92→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES
    93→)
    94→
    95→# Detectors where potential = file count but findings are per-(file, sub-type).
    96→# Per-file weighted failures are capped at 1.0 to match the file-based denominator.
    97→FILE_BASED_DETECTORS = {
    98→    detector
    99→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items()
   100→    if policy.file_based
   101→}
   102→
   103→
   104→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:
   105→    """Derive dimensions from DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.
   106→
   107→    Each unique (dimension, tier) pair becomes a Dimension, with its detectors
   108→    collected automatically. Order follows first-seen in the policies dict.
   109→    """
   110→    # Collect (dimension_name -> tier) preserving first-seen order,
   111→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   112→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}
   113→    grouped: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   114→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():
   115→        if policy.dimension is None or policy.tier is None:
   116→            continue
   117→        if policy.dimension not in dim_tiers:
   118→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   119→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   120→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   121→    return [
   122→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   123→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   124→    ]
   125→
   126→
   127→DIMENSIONS = _build_dimensions()
   128→DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME = {d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS}
   129→
   130→TIER_WEIGHTS = {
   131→    Tier.AUTO_FIX: 1,
   132→    Tier.QUICK_FIX: 2,
   133→    Tier.JUDGMENT: 3,
   134→    Tier.MAJOR_REFACTOR: 4,
   135→}
   136→CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS = {Confidence.HIGH: 1.0, Confidence.MEDIUM: 0.7, Confidence.LOW: 0.3}
   137→
   138→# Minimum checks for full dimension weight — below this, weight is dampened
   139→# proportionally. Prevents small-sample dimensions from swinging the overall score.
   140→MIN_SAMPLE = 200
   141→
   142→# Holistic review weight: findings with file="." and detail.holistic=True
   143→# get a 10x weight multiplier for display/priority purposes (issues list,
   144→# remediation engine).  NOT used in score computation — review findings are
   145→# excluded from the detection scoring pipeline (scored via assessments only).
   146→HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER = 10.0
   147→HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL = 10
   148→
   149→# Budget: subjective dimensions get this fraction of the overall score.
   150→# Mechanical dimensions get the remainder.
   151→SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 0.60
   152→MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION = 1.0 - SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   153→
   154→# Per-dimension weighting within the mechanical pool.
   155→# Keep this balanced: no special boost for security/test in the pool itself.
   156→MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   157→    "file health": 2.0,
   158→    "code quality": 1.0,
   159→    "duplication": 1.0,
   160→    "test health": 1.0,
   161→    "security": 1.0,
   162→}
   163→
   164→# Per-dimension weighting within the subjective pool.
   165→# Emphasize elegance and contract/type coherence for a stronger architecture north star.
   166→SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   167→    "high elegance": 22.0,
   168→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
   169→    "low elegance": 12.0,
   170→    "contracts": 12.0,
   171→    "type safety": 12.0,
   172→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
   173→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
   174→    # Low-but-meaningful structural signal (about half of the subjective
   175→    # average weight) so it matters without dominating craftsmanship axes.
   176→    "structure nav": 5.0,
   177→    "error consistency": 3.0,
   178→    "naming quality": 2.0,
   179→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
   180→    "design coherence": 10.0,
   181→}
   182→
   183→# Synthetic check count for subjective dimensions in dimension_scores.
   184→SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS = 10
   185→
   186→FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
   187→    "lenient": frozenset({"open"}),
   188→    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix"}),
   189→    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive"}),
   190→}
   191→
   192→# Tolerance for treating a subjective score as "on target" in integrity checks.
   193→# Scores within this band of the target are flagged as potential gaming.
   194→SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE = 0.05
   195→
   196→
   197→def matches_target_score(
   198→    score: object,
   199→    target: object,
   200→    *,
   201→    tolerance: float = SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
   202→) -> bool:
   203→    """Return True when score is within tolerance of target."""
   204→    try:
   205→        score_value = float(score)
   206→        target_value = float(target)
   207→        tolerance_value = max(0.0, float(tolerance))
   208→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   209→        return False
   210→    return abs(score_value - target_value) <= tolerance_value
   211→
   212→
   213→def register_scoring_policy(policy: DetectorScoringPolicy) -> None:
   214→    """Register a scoring policy at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   215→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES[policy.detector] = policy
   216→    _rebuild_derived()
   217→
   218→
   219→def reset_registered_scoring_policies() -> None:
   220→    """Reset runtime-added scoring policies to built-in defaults."""
   221→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.clear()
   222→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.update(_BASE_DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES)
   223→    _rebuild_derived()
   224→
   225→
   226→def _rebuild_derived() -> None:
   227→    """Rebuild DIMENSIONS, DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME, FILE_BASED_DETECTORS from current state.
   228→
   229→    Mutates existing objects in-place so that all references (including imports
   230→    that bound the original objects) see the updates.
   231→    """
   232→    new_dims = _build_dimensions()
   233→    DIMENSIONS.clear()
   234→    DIMENSIONS.extend(new_dims)
   235→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.clear()
   236→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.update({d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS})
   237→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.clear()
   238→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.update(
   239→        det for det, pol in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items() if pol.file_based
   240→    )
   241→
   242→
   243→def detector_policy(detector: str) -> DetectorScoringPolicy:
   244→    """Get scoring policy for a detector, with a safe default fallback."""
   245→    return DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(
   246→        detector,
   247→        DetectorScoringPolicy(detector=detector, dimension=None, tier=None),
   248→    )
   249→
   250→
   251→__all__ = [
   252→    "CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS",
   253→    "DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES",
   254→    "DIMENSIONS",
   255→    "DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME",
   256→    "FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE",
   257→    "FILE_BASED_DETECTORS",
   258→    "HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER",
   259→    "HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL",
   260→    "MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS",
   261→    "MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION",
   262→    "MIN_SAMPLE",
   263→    "SCORING_MODES",
   264→    "SECURITY_EXCLUDED_ZONES",
   265→    "SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS",
   266→    "SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS",
   267→    "SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE",
   268→    "SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION",
   269→    "TIER_WEIGHTS",
   270→    "DetectorScoringPolicy",

   274→    "matches_target_score",
   275→    "register_scoring_policy",
   276→    "reset_registered_scoring_policies",
   277→]

1→"""Per-detector scoring calculations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     8→    CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS,
     9→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    10→    HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER,
    11→    SCORING_MODES,
    12→    ScoreMode,
    13→    detector_policy,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import Finding
    16→
    17→# Tiered file-count cap thresholds for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    18→# Controls how many findings per file contribute to the weighted failure sum.
    19→_FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD = 6     # findings in file for high cap
    20→_FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD = 3      # findings in file for mid cap
    21→_FILE_CAP_HIGH = 2.0             # cap value at high concentration
    22→_FILE_CAP_MID = 1.5              # cap value at mid concentration
    23→_FILE_CAP_LOW = 1.0              # cap value at low concentration (1-2 findings)
    24→
    25→
    26→def merge_potentials(potentials_by_lang: dict[str, dict[str, int]]) -> dict[str, int]:
    27→    """Sum potentials across languages per detector."""
    28→    merged: dict[str, int] = {}
    29→    for lang_potentials in potentials_by_lang.values():
    30→        for detector, count in lang_potentials.items():
    31→            merged[detector] = merged.get(detector, 0) + count
    32→    return merged
    33→
    34→
    35→def _iter_scoring_candidates(
    36→    detector: str,
    37→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    38→    excluded_zones: frozenset[str],
    39→):
    40→    """Yield in-scope findings for a detector (zone-filtered)."""
    41→    for finding in findings.values():
    42→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    43→            continue
    44→        if finding.get("detector") != detector:
    45→            continue
    46→        if finding.get("zone", "production") in excluded_zones:
    47→            continue
    48→        yield finding
    49→
    50→
    51→def _finding_weight(finding: Finding, *, use_loc_weight: bool) -> float:
    52→    """Compute the scoring weight for a single finding."""
    53→    if use_loc_weight:
    54→        return finding.get("detail", {}).get("loc_weight", 1.0)
    55→    return CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS.get(finding.get("confidence", "medium"), 0.7)
    56→
    57→
    58→def _file_count_cap(findings_in_file: int) -> float:
    59→    """Tiered cap for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    60→
    61→    Keeps file-count denominator semantics while preserving concentration signal:
    62→    1-2 findings => _FILE_CAP_LOW, 3-5 => _FILE_CAP_MID, 6+ => _FILE_CAP_HIGH.
    63→    """
    64→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD:
    65→        return _FILE_CAP_HIGH
    66→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD:
    67→        return _FILE_CAP_MID
    68→    return _FILE_CAP_LOW
    69→
    70→
    71→@dataclass
    72→class _ModeAccum:
    73→    """Per-mode accumulator for file-based detector scoring."""
    74→
    75→    by_file: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    76→    by_file_count: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    77→    file_cap: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    78→    holistic_sum: float = 0.0
    79→    issue_count: int = 0
    80→
    81→
    82→def _file_based_failures_by_mode(
    83→    detector: str,
    84→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    85→    policy,
    86→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]]:
    87→    """Accumulate weighted failures by score mode for file-based detectors."""
    88→    accum: dict[ScoreMode, _ModeAccum] = {mode: _ModeAccum() for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    89→
    90→    for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones):
    91→        status = finding.get("status", "open")
    92→        holistic = finding.get("file") == "." and finding.get("detail", {}).get(
    93→            "holistic"
    94→        )
    95→
    96→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    97→            if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
    98→                continue
    99→
   100→            if holistic:
   101→                accum[mode].holistic_sum += (
   102→                    _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False) * HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
   103→                )
   104→                accum[mode].issue_count += 1
   105→                continue
   106→
   107→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=policy.use_loc_weight)
   108→            file_key = finding.get("file", "")
   109→            a = accum[mode]
   110→            a.by_file[file_key] = a.by_file.get(file_key, 0.0) + weight
   111→            a.by_file_count[file_key] = a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0) + 1
   112→            if policy.use_loc_weight and file_key not in a.file_cap:
   113→                a.file_cap[file_key] = weight
   114→            a.issue_count += 1
   115→
   116→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]] = {}
   117→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   118→        a = accum[mode]
   119→        if policy.use_loc_weight:
   120→            weighted = sum(
   121→                min(weighted_sum, a.file_cap.get(file_key, weighted_sum))
   122→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   123→            )
   124→        else:
   125→            weighted = sum(
   126→                min(weighted_sum, _file_count_cap(a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0)))
   127→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   128→            )
   129→        out[mode] = (a.issue_count, weighted + a.holistic_sum)
   130→    return out
   131→
   132→
   133→def detector_stats_by_mode(
   134→    detector: str,
   135→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   136→    potential: int,
   137→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]]:
   138→    """Compute (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures) for each score mode."""
   139→    if potential <= 0:
   140→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   141→
   142→    # Review and concern findings are scored via subjective assessments only —
   143→    # exclude them from the detection-side scoring pipeline so resolving these
   144→    # findings never changes the score directly.
   145→    if detector in ("review", "concerns"):
   146→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   147→
   148→    policy = detector_policy(detector)
   149→
   150→    if policy.file_based:
   151→        mode_failures = _file_based_failures_by_mode(detector, findings, policy)
   152→    else:
   153→        issue_count: dict[ScoreMode, int] = {mode: 0 for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   154→        weighted_failures: dict[ScoreMode, float] = {
   155→            mode: 0.0 for mode in SCORING_MODES
   156→        }
   157→
   158→        for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(
   159→            detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones
   160→        ):
   161→            status = finding.get("status", "open")
   162→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False)
   163→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   164→                if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
   165→                    continue
   166→                issue_count[mode] += 1
   167→                weighted_failures[mode] += weight
   168→
   169→        mode_failures = {
   170→            mode: (issue_count[mode], weighted_failures[mode]) for mode in SCORING_MODES
   171→        }
   172→
   173→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]] = {}
   174→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   175→        issues, weighted = mode_failures[mode]
   176→        pass_rate = max(0.0, (potential - weighted) / potential)
   177→        out[mode] = (pass_rate, issues, weighted)
   178→    return out
   179→
   180→
   181→def detector_pass_rate(
   182→    detector: str,
   183→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   184→    potential: int,
   185→    *,
   186→    strict: bool = False,
   187→) -> tuple[float, int, float]:
   188→    """Pass rate for one detector.
   189→
   190→    Returns (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures).
   191→    Zero potential -> (1.0, 0, 0.0).
   192→    """
   193→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   194→    return detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)[mode]
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = [
   198→    "detector_pass_rate",
   199→    "detector_stats_by_mode",
   200→    "merge_potentials",
   201→]
   202→

1→"""Tests for shared queue selection in desloppify.work_queue."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     6→    QueueBuildOptions,
     7→)
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     9→    build_work_queue as _build_work_queue,
    10→)
    11→
    12→
    13→def build_work_queue(state, **kwargs):
    14→    return _build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(**kwargs))
    15→
    16→
    17→def _finding(
    18→    fid: str,
    19→    *,
    20→    detector: str = "smells",
    21→    file: str = "src/a.py",
    22→    tier: int = 3,
    23→    confidence: str = "medium",
    24→    status: str = "open",
    25→    detail: dict | None = None,
    26→) -> dict:
    27→    return {
    28→        "id": fid,
    29→        "detector": detector,
    30→        "file": file,
    31→        "tier": tier,
    32→        "confidence": confidence,
    33→        "summary": fid,
    34→        "status": status,
    35→        "detail": detail or {},
    36→    }
    37→
    38→
    39→def _state(findings: list[dict], *, dimension_scores: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    40→    return {
    41→        "findings": {f["id"]: f for f in findings},
    42→        "dimension_scores": dimension_scores or {},
    43→    }
    44→
    45→
    46→def test_review_finding_uses_natural_tier():
    47→    review = _finding(
    48→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    49→        detector="review",
    50→        tier=2,
    51→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    52→    )
    53→    mechanical = _finding("smells::src/a.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
    54→    state = _state(
    55→        [review, mechanical],
    56→        dimension_scores={
    57→            "Naming quality": {"score": 94.0, "strict": 94.0, "issues": 1}
    58→        },
    59→    )
    60→
    61→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
    62→    by_id = {item["id"]: item for item in queue["items"] if item["kind"] == "finding"}
    63→    assert "review::src/a.py::naming" in by_id
    64→    assert "smells::src/a.py::x" in by_id
    65→
    66→
    67→def test_review_items_ranked_alongside_mechanical():
    68→    urgent = _finding(
    69→        "security::src/a.py::x", detector="security", tier=1, confidence="high"
    70→    )
    71→    review = _finding(
    72→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    73→        detector="review",
    74→        tier=2,
    75→        confidence="high",
    76→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    77→    )
    78→    state = _state(
    79→        [urgent, review],
    80→        dimension_scores={
    81→            "Naming quality": {"score": 92.0, "strict": 92.0, "issues": 2}
    82→        },
    83→    )
    84→
    85→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)
    86→    ids = {item["id"] for item in queue["items"]}
    87→    # Both items appear in the queue
    88→    assert "security::src/a.py::x" in ids
    89→    assert "review::src/a.py::naming" in ids
    90→
    91→
    92→def test_review_items_sort_by_issue_weight_within_tier():
    93→    standard = _finding(
    94→        "review::src/a.py::naming",
    95→        detector="review",
    96→        tier=2,
    97→        confidence="high",
    98→        detail={"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    99→    )
   100→    holistic = _finding(

   114→
   115→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=False)

   396→# ── Subjective threshold clamping ─────────────────────────

   398→
   399→def test_subjective_threshold_clamped_to_valid_range():
   400→    """Threshold values outside [0, 100] are clamped, not rejected."""
   401→    state = _state(
   402→        [],
   403→        dimension_scores={
   404→            "Naming quality": {"score": 50.0, "strict": 50.0, "issues": 1},
   405→        },
   406→    )
   407→    # threshold=-10 clamps to 0.0 -> score 50 >= 0 -> item excluded
   408→    queue = build_work_queue(
   409→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=-10
   410→    )
   411→    subj_items = [item for item in queue["items"] if item["kind"] == "subjective_dimension"]
   412→    assert subj_items == []
   413→
   414→    # threshold=200 clamps to 100.0 -> score 50 < 100 -> item included
   415→    queue2 = build_work_queue(
   416→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=200
   417→    )
   418→    subj_items2 = [item for item in queue2["items"] if item["kind"] == "subjective_dimension"]
   419→    assert len(subj_items2) >= 1

   548→# ── Backlog-gated subjective suppression ──────────────────
   549→
   550→
   551→def test_backlog_gated_subjective_items_absent_when_objective_backlog():
   552→    """Stale subjective items (no open review findings) are fully suppressed
   553→    while objective findings exist — their only action (--force-review-rerun)
   554→    is blocked by the review preflight."""
   555→    objective_findings = [
   556→        _finding(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   557→        for c in "abcd"
   558→    ]
   559→    state = _state(
   560→        objective_findings,
   561→        dimension_scores={
   562→            "Naming quality": {
   563→                "score": 70.0,
   564→                "strict": 70.0,
   565→                "issues": 1,
   566→                "detectors": {
   567→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   568→                },
   569→            },
   570→        },
   571→    )
   572→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   573→        "naming_quality": {
   574→            "score": 70.0,
   575→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   576→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   577→        }
   578→    }
   579→
   580→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   581→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   582→    subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
   583→
   584→    # Backlog-gated subjective items do not appear at all
   585→    assert subj_ids == []
   586→    assert len(ids) == 4  # only objective items
   587→
   588→
   589→def test_backlog_gated_items_surface_when_queue_empty():
   590→    """When no objective findings remain, backlog-gated subjective items
   591→    surface so the user is prompted to refresh stale dimensions."""
   592→    state = _state(
   593→        [],
   594→        dimension_scores={
   595→            "Naming quality": {
   596→                "score": 70.0,
   597→                "strict": 70.0,
   598→                "issues": 1,
   599→                "detectors": {
   600→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   601→                },
   602→            },
   603→        },
   604→    )
   605→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   606→        "naming_quality": {
   607→            "score": 70.0,
   608→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   609→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   610→        }
   611→    }
   612→

   615→    assert "subjective::naming_quality" in ids
   616→
   617→
   618→def test_actionable_subjective_items_still_appear_during_backlog():
   619→    """Subjective items with open review findings (actionable work) still
   620→    appear in the queue alongside objective items."""
   621→    review = _finding(
   622→        "review::.::holistic::naming_quality::abc12345",
   623→        detector="review",
   624→        tier=3,
   625→        detail={"holistic": True, "dimension": "naming_quality"},
   626→    )
   627→    objective_findings = [
   628→        _finding(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   629→        for c in "abcdef"
   630→    ]
   631→    state = _state(
   632→        objective_findings + [review],
   633→        dimension_scores={
   634→            "Naming quality": {"score": 70.0, "strict": 70.0, "issues": 1},
   635→        },
   636→    )
   637→
   638→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   639→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   640→    subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
   641→
   642→    # Subjective item with open review findings is NOT gated
   643→    assert len(subj_ids) >= 1
   644→    # And it's interleaved, not at the very end
   645→    first_subj_pos = ids.index(subj_ids[0])
   646→    assert first_subj_pos < len(ids) - 1
   647→
   648→
   649→# ── Workflow items bypass interleave guardrail ─────────────
   650→
   651→
   652→def test_workflow_items_preserved_at_front_during_interleave():
   653→    """Workflow items (workflow_stage, workflow_action) should not participate
   654→    in the subjective/objective interleave — they stay at the front."""
   655→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail
   656→
   657→    workflow = {"id": "synthesis::observe", "kind": "workflow_stage"}
   658→    obj1 = {"id": "smells::a", "kind": "finding"}
   659→    obj2 = {"id": "smells::b", "kind": "finding"}
   660→    obj3 = {"id": "smells::c", "kind": "finding"}
   661→    subj1 = {"id": "subjective::naming", "kind": "subjective_dimension"}
   662→    subj2 = {"id": "subjective::clarity", "kind": "subjective_dimension"}
   663→
   664→    items = [workflow, obj1, obj2, obj3, subj1, subj2]
   665→    result = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(items)
   666→
   667→    # Workflow item must be first
   668→    assert result[0]["id"] == "synthesis::observe"
   669→    # All items preserved
   670→    assert len(result) == 6
   671→    # No workflow items mixed into the interleaved portion
   672→    result_ids = [r["id"] for r in result]
   673→    assert result_ids.index("synthesis::observe") == 0
   674→
   675→
   676→def test_workflow_action_also_bypasses_interleave():
   677→    """workflow_action kind is also excluded from interleave."""
   678→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail
   679→
   680→    wf = {"id": "workflow::create-plan", "kind": "workflow_action"}
   681→    obj = {"id": "smells::a", "kind": "finding"}
   682→    subj = {"id": "subjective::naming", "kind": "subjective_dimension"}
   683→
   684→    result = _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail([wf, obj, subj])
   685→    assert result[0]["id"] == "workflow::create-plan"
   686→    assert len(result) == 3
   687→

    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    28→
    29→def _finding(fid: str, detector: str = "unused", tier: int = 1,
    30→             file: str = "test.py", detail: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    31→    return {
    32→        "id": fid,
    33→        "detector": detector,
    34→        "file": file,
    35→        "tier": tier,
    36→        "confidence": "high",
    37→        "summary": f"Finding {fid}",
    38→        "status": "open",
    39→        "detail": detail or {},
    40→    }
    41→
    42→
    43→def _state_with(*findings: dict) -> dict:
    44→    fmap = {}
    45→    for f in findings:
    46→        fmap[f["id"]] = f
    47→    return {"findings": fmap, "scan_count": 5}

   260→def test_collapse_clusters_replaces_members():

   272→    items = [

   416→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   417→# _generate_action always returns something

   419→
   420→def test_generate_action_always_returns_something():
   421→    """Every detector/subtype combination must produce a non-None action."""
   422→    from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   423→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import _generate_action
   424→
   425→    # No metadata → fallback
   426→    assert _generate_action(None, None) == "review and fix each finding"
   427→
   428→    # Every registered detector, with and without subtype
   429→    for name, meta in DETECTORS.items():
   430→        result = _generate_action(meta, None)
   431→        assert result, f"_generate_action({name}, None) returned empty"
   432→
   433→        result_sub = _generate_action(meta, "some_subtype")
   434→        assert result_sub, f"_generate_action({name}, 'some_subtype') returned empty"
   435→
   436→
   437→def test_generate_action_strips_subtype_examples():
   438→    """Guidance with ' — ' should be stripped to the core verb for subtypes."""
   439→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import _strip_guidance_examples
   440→
   441→    assert _strip_guidance_examples("fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains") == "fix code smells"
   442→    assert _strip_guidance_examples("fix dict key mismatches — dead writes are likely dead code") == "fix dict key mismatches"
   443→    # No dash → keep as-is
   444→    assert _strip_guidance_examples("review and fix each finding") == "review and fix each finding"
   445→

   531→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   532→# Initial review (unscored) cluster
   533→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   534→
   535→def _unscored_state(*dim_keys: str) -> dict:
   536→    """Build a state with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions."""
   537→    dim_scores: dict = {}
   538→    assessments: dict = {}
   539→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
   540→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
   541→            "score": 0,
   542→            "strict": 0,
   543→            "checks": 1,
   544→            "issues": 0,
   545→            "detectors": {
   546→                "subjective_assessment": {
   547→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
   548→                    "placeholder": True,
   549→                }
   550→            },
   551→        }
   552→        assessments[dim_key] = {
   553→            "score": 0.0,
   554→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   555→            "placeholder": True,
   556→        }
   557→    return {
   558→        "findings": {},
   559→        "scan_count": 1,
   560→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   561→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
   562→    }
   563→
   564→
   565→def _stale_state(*dim_keys: str, score: float = 50.0) -> dict:
   566→    """Build a state with stale (previously scored) subjective dimensions."""
   567→    dim_scores: dict = {}
   568→    assessments: dict = {}
   569→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
   570→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
   571→            "score": score,
   572→            "strict": score,
   573→            "checks": 1,
   574→            "issues": 0,
   575→            "detectors": {
   576→                "subjective_assessment": {
   577→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
   578→                    "placeholder": False,
   579→                }
   580→            },
   581→        }
   582→        assessments[dim_key] = {
   583→            "score": score,
   584→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   585→            "refresh_reason": "mechanical_findings_changed",
   586→            "stale_since": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   587→        }
   588→    return {
   589→        "findings": {},
   590→        "scan_count": 5,
   591→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
   592→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
   593→    }
   594→
   595→
   596→def test_initial_review_cluster_created():
   597→    """Unscored dims are grouped into auto/initial-review."""
   598→    plan = empty_plan()
   599→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   600→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   601→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   602→    ]
   603→    state = _unscored_state("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   604→
   605→    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
   606→    assert changes >= 1
   607→    assert "auto/initial-review" in plan["clusters"]
   608→
   609→    cluster = plan["clusters"]["auto/initial-review"]
   610→    assert cluster["auto"] is True
   611→    assert cluster["cluster_key"] == "subjective::unscored"
   612→    assert set(cluster["finding_ids"]) == {
   613→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   614→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   615→    }
   616→    assert "Initial review" in cluster["description"]
   617→    assert "2 unscored" in cluster["description"]
   618→    assert "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions" in cluster["action"]
   619→
   620→
   621→def test_single_unscored_dim_creates_cluster():
   622→    """Even 1 unscored dim creates an initial-review cluster (min size 1)."""
   623→    plan = empty_plan()
   624→    plan["queue_order"] = ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   625→    state = _unscored_state("design_coherence")
   626→
   627→    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
   628→    assert changes >= 1
   629→    assert "auto/initial-review" in plan["clusters"]
   630→    assert len(plan["clusters"]["auto/initial-review"]["finding_ids"]) == 1
   631→
   632→
   633→def test_stale_and_unscored_separate_clusters():
   634→    """Unscored and stale dims create two disjoint clusters."""
   635→    plan = empty_plan()
   636→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   637→        "subjective::design_coherence",   # unscored
   638→        "subjective::error_consistency",   # stale
   639→        "subjective::convention_drift",    # stale
   640→    ]
   641→    # Mixed state: design_coherence is unscored, the other two are stale
   642→    state = _unscored_state("design_coherence")
   643→    stale = _stale_state("error_consistency", "convention_drift")
   644→    state["dimension_scores"].update(stale["dimension_scores"])
   645→    state["subjective_assessments"].update(stale["subjective_assessments"])
   646→
   647→    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
   648→    assert changes >= 2
   649→
   650→    # Initial review cluster
   651→    assert "auto/initial-review" in plan["clusters"]
   652→    initial = plan["clusters"]["auto/initial-review"]
   653→    assert initial["finding_ids"] == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   654→
   655→    # Stale review cluster
   656→    assert "auto/stale-review" in plan["clusters"]
   657→    stale_cluster = plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]
   658→    assert set(stale_cluster["finding_ids"]) == {
   659→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   660→        "subjective::convention_drift",
   661→    }
   662→
   663→    # Disjoint
   664→    initial_set = set(initial["finding_ids"])
   665→    stale_set = set(stale_cluster["finding_ids"])
   666→    assert initial_set.isdisjoint(stale_set)
   667→

   669→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   670→# _repair_ghost_cluster_refs
   671→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   672→
   673→def test_repair_ghost_cluster_refs():
   674→    """Overrides pointing to non-existent clusters should be cleared."""
   675→    plan = empty_plan()
   676→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   677→
   678→    # Create an override pointing to a cluster that doesn't exist
   679→    plan["overrides"]["a"] = {
   680→        "finding_id": "a",
   681→        "cluster": "deleted-cluster",
   682→        "created_at": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   683→    }
   684→    # Create an override pointing to an existing cluster
   685→    plan["clusters"]["real-cluster"] = {
   686→        "name": "real-cluster",
   687→        "finding_ids": ["b"],
   688→        "auto": False,
   689→        "cluster_key": "",
   690→        "action": None,
   691→        "user_modified": False,
   692→    }
   693→    plan["overrides"]["b"] = {
   694→        "finding_id": "b",
   695→        "cluster": "real-cluster",
   696→        "created_at": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   697→    }
   698→
   699→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
   700→    repaired = _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, utc_now())
   701→
   702→    assert repaired == 1
   703→    assert plan["overrides"]["a"]["cluster"] is None
   704→    assert plan["overrides"]["b"]["cluster"] == "real-cluster"
   705→

1→"""Shared plan constants and helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→CONFIDENCE_ORDER = {"high": 0, "medium": 1, "low": 2}
     6→
     7→
     8→def is_subjective_phase(phase) -> bool:
     9→    """Return True when a phase is subjective/review oriented."""
    10→    label = (phase.label or "").lower()
    11→    run_name = getattr(phase.run, "__name__", "").lower()
    12→    return (
    13→        "subjective" in label
    14→        or "review" in label
    15→        or run_name == "phase_subjective_review"
    16→    )
    17→

1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     8→
     9→
    10→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    patterns: list[str],
    13→    status: str,
    14→) -> bool:
    15→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    16→    if status != "fixed":
    17→        return False
    18→    try:
    19→        if not has_living_plan():
    20→            return False
    21→        plan = load_plan()
    22→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    23→        if not queue_order:
    24→            return False
    25→
    26→        result = build_work_queue(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
    29→                plan=plan,
    30→                count=1,
    31→                collapse_clusters=True,
    32→                include_subjective=True,
    33→            ),
    34→        )
    35→        if not result["items"]:
    36→            return False
    37→
    38→        front_item = result["items"][0]
    39→        front_id = front_item["id"]
    40→
    41→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
    42→        if front_item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    43→            for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    44→                front_ids.add(member["id"])
    45→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    46→        else:
    47→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    48→
    49→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    50→        resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    51→        for pattern in patterns:
    52→            if pattern in clusters:
    53→                resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("finding_ids", []))
    54→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    55→            else:
    56→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    57→
    58→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    59→        resolved_ids = {
    60→            finding_id
    61→            for finding_id in resolved_ids
    62→            if finding_id in clusters
    63→            or (finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open")
    64→        }
    65→        if not resolved_ids:
    66→            return False
    67→
    68→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
    69→        for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    70→            if cluster_id in clusters:
    71→                alive_members = {
    72→                    finding_id
    73→                    for finding_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("finding_ids", [])
    74→                    if finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open"
    75→                }
    76→                if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    77→                    out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    78→        if not out_of_order:
    79→            return False
    80→
    81→        print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    82→        for finding_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    83→            print(f"    {finding_id}")
    84→        print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    85→        print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    86→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan move <pattern> --position top    # move to front", "dim"))
    87→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    88→        print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    89→        return True
    90→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    91→        return False
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
    95→
```

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueueBreakdown:
    17→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    18→
    19→    queue_total: int = 0
    20→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    21→    skipped: int = 0
    22→    subjective: int = 0
    23→    workflow: int = 0
    24→    suppressed: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→
    30→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    plan: dict | None = None,
    33→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    34→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    35→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    36→
    37→    result = build_work_queue(
    38→        state,
    39→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    40→            status="open",
    41→            count=None,
    42→            plan=plan,
    43→            collapse_clusters=True,
    44→            include_skipped=False,
    45→        ),
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    queue_total = result["total"]
    49→    suppressed = int(result.get("suppressed_count", 0))
    50→
    51→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue
    52→    items = result.get("items", [])
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for item in items
    55→        if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    56→    )
    57→    workflow = sum(
    58→        1 for item in items
    59→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    # Plan-derived counts
    63→    plan_ordered = 0
    64→    skipped = 0
    65→    if plan:
    66→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    67→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    68→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    71→
    72→    # Focus cluster info
    73→    focus_cluster = None
    74→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    75→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    76→    if plan:
    77→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→        if active:
    79→            focus_cluster = active
    80→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
```

## Implementation Plan: Work Queue Prioritization Overhaul

### 1. Overview of Changes

The goal is to reduce 5 layers of queue prioritization to 3, and make the sort impact-aware using existing `compute_health_breakdown` data. Here is the exact change plan for each file.

---

### 2. File-by-File Implementation

---

#### File 1: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`

**Purpose**: Add impact enrichment, change tier-1 sort key to use `-estimated_impact`, update `item_explain`.

**Step 1a**: Add a new function `enrich_with_impact` that takes the list of items and `dimension_scores` from state, calls `compute_health_breakdown` once, then stamps each item with an `estimated_impact` float.

The function signature:

```python
def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], state: dict) -> None:
    """Stamp each item with estimated_impact based on health breakdown."""
```

The logic inside:

```python
from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector

def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], state: dict) -> None:
    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    if not dim_scores:
        for item in items:
            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
        return

    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])

    # Build lookup: dimension_name (lowered) -> {per_point, score, headroom}
    dim_lookup: dict[str, dict] = {}
    for entry in entries:
        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip().lower()
        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
        score = float(entry.get("score", 100.0))
        dim_lookup[name] = {
            "per_point": per_point,
            "score": score,
            "headroom": 100.0 - score,
        }

    for item in items:
        item["estimated_impact"] = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_lookup)


def _compute_item_impact(item: dict, dim_lookup: dict) -> float:
    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")

    # Workflow items keep impact 0 — they're sorted by tiers -3/-2/-1
    if kind in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action"):
        return 0.0

    # Subjective dimension items: full headroom * per_point
    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
        detail = detail_dict(item)
        dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
        dim_info = dim_lookup.get(dim_name.lower())
        if dim_info:
            return dim_info["per_point"] * dim_info["headroom"]
        return 0.0

    # Regular findings: look up dimension via detector
    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    if not dimension:
        return 0.0

    dim_info = dim_lookup.get(dimension.name.lower())
    if not dim_info:
        return 0.0

    # For subjective findings (review), share the headroom across open findings
    if item.get("is_subjective"):
        return dim_info["per_point"] * dim_info["headroom"]

    # For mechanical findings: per_point * confidence_weight
    # This gives a relative signal — higher confidence = more impact certainty
    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    conf_weight = {"high": 1.0, "medium": 0.7, "low": 0.3}.get(confidence, 0.3)
    return dim_info["per_point"] * conf_weight * dim_info["headroom"]
```

Key point: all mechanical findings in the same dimension with the same confidence get the same impact estimate. The differentiation within that is by `detail.count` and `review_weight`, which remain as tiebreakers.

**Step 1b**: Change `item_sort_key` tier 1 to sort by `-estimated_impact` instead of sub-tier 0/1.

Current tier-1 code (lines 110-127):

```python
    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
        return (
            1,
            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items at the same level.
            subjective_score_value(item),
            item.get("id", ""),
        )

    detail = detail_dict(item)
    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    return (
        1,
        0,
        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
        -review_weight,
        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
        item.get("id", ""),
    )
```

New tier-1 code:

```python
    # All tier-1 items (findings + subjective dimensions) sort by impact
    impact = -float(item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0))
    
    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
        return (
            1,
            impact,
            subjective_score_value(item),
            item.get("id", ""),
        )

    detail = detail_dict(item)
    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
    return (
        1,
        impact,
        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
        -review_weight,
        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
        item.get("id", ""),
    )
```

The key change: `impact` replaces the hard-coded sub-tier `0` or `1`. Higher estimated_impact items sort first (because it's negated). Within equal impact, tiebreakers still work: confidence order, review weight, count, id.

#### File 3: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`

**Purpose**: Rewrite `collapse_clusters` to preserve input order instead of appending and re-sorting.

Current approach (lines 118-189): Separates items into `non_cluster_items` and `cluster_members`, creates meta-items, appends them all to a list, then calls `result.sort(key=item_sort_key)` -- destroying plan order.

New approach -- walk-and-replace:

```python
def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
    
    Walks items in order and replaces the first occurrence of each cluster's
    member with the cluster meta-item, skipping subsequent members.
    This preserves the input ordering (impact-aware or plan-ordered).
    """
    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    if not clusters:
        return items

    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
        if not cluster.get("auto"):
            continue
        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name

    if not fid_to_cluster:
        return items

    # Collect members per cluster (preserving order within each)
    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    for item in items:
        cluster_name = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
        if cluster_name:
            cluster_members.setdefault(cluster_name, []).append(item)

    # Build meta-items for clusters with 2+ members
    meta_items: dict[str, dict] = {}
    singleton_clusters: set[str] = set()
    for cluster_name, members in cluster_members.items():
        if len(members) < 2:
            singleton_clusters.add(cluster_name)
            continue
        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
        meta_items[cluster_name] = _build_cluster_meta_item(
            cluster_name, cluster_data, members
        )

    # Walk in order: first member of a real cluster -> replace with meta-item
    # Subsequent members -> skip
    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
    result: list[dict] = []
    for item in items:
        cluster_name = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
        if cluster_name is None or cluster_name in singleton_clusters:
            result.append(item)
            continue
        if cluster_name in seen_clusters:
            continue  # skip subsequent members
        seen_clusters.add(cluster_name)
        meta = meta_items.get(cluster_name)
        if meta:
            # Inherit the highest estimated_impact from members
            members = cluster_members.get(cluster_name, [])
            max_impact = max(
                (float(m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)) for m in members),
                default=0.0,
            )
            meta["estimated_impact"] = max_impact
            result.append(meta)

    return result
```

Extract the meta-item building into a helper `_build_cluster_meta_item` (same logic as the current inner part of `collapse_clusters`, lines 148-185):

```python
def _build_cluster_meta_item(
    cluster_name: str, cluster_data: dict, members: list[dict]
) -> dict:
    """Build a cluster meta-item from its members."""
    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
    if "desloppify fix" in action:
        action_type = "auto_fix"
    elif "desloppify move" in action:
        action_type = "reorganize"
    else:
        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
            action_type = "refactor"

    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
    else:
        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"

    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
    if not primary_command:
        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"

    return {
        "id": cluster_name,
        "kind": "cluster",
        "action_type": action_type,
        "summary": summary,
        "members": members,
        "member_count": len(members),
        "primary_command": primary_command,
        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
        "cluster_auto": True,
        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
        "confidence": "high",
        "detector": detector,
        "file": "",
    }
```

**Critical change**: Remove the `from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import item_sort_key` import at line 5. After this change, `plan_order.py` no longer needs `item_sort_key` at all.

---

## Complete Implementation Plan

### Summary of Architecture Change

**Before (5 layers):**
1. `item_sort_key` -- mechanical before subjective (hardcoded sub-tier 0 vs 1)
2. `apply_plan_order` -- user explicit ordering
3. `collapse_clusters` -- replaces then RE-SORTS, destroying plan order
4. `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail` -- patches bad mechanical-first ordering (3:1 interleave)
5. `backlog_gated` suppression -- hides stale subjective when any mechanical exists

**After (3 layers):**
1. `enrich_with_impact` + `item_sort_key` -- impact-aware sort (high score headroom first)
2. `apply_plan_order` -- user explicit ordering (unchanged)
3. `collapse_clusters` -- walk-and-replace, preserving order

### Execution Order

Implement in this order to keep tests passing at each step:

**Phase 1: Add impact enrichment (additive, no behavior change yet)**
- Add `enrich_with_impact` and `_compute_item_impact` to `ranking.py`
- Call `enrich_with_impact` in `core.py` before sort (stamps items but sort key not yet changed)

**Phase 2: Change sort key to use impact**
- Modify `item_sort_key` tier-1 to use `-estimated_impact` instead of hard sub-tier
- Update `item_explain` ranking_factors
- Update test assertions for `ranking_factors` in `work_queue_cases.py` (line 210) and `integration/work_queue_cases.py` (line 192)

**Phase 3: Fix collapse_clusters**
- Rewrite `collapse_clusters` in `plan_order.py` to walk-and-replace
- Remove `import item_sort_key` from `plan_order.py`
- Add `test_collapse_preserves_plan_order`

**Phase 4: Remove interleave guardrail**
- Delete `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail`, `_is_subjective_queue_item`, `_WORKFLOW_KINDS` from `core.py`
- Remove the `should_interleave` block from `build_work_queue`
- Delete tests: `test_workflow_items_preserved_at_front_during_interleave`, `test_workflow_action_also_bypasses_interleave`

**Phase 5: Remove backlog_gated suppression**
- Remove backlog_gated suppression logic from `build_work_queue` in `core.py`
- Remove `backlog_gated` field from `build_subjective_items` in `helpers.py`
- Set `suppressed_count` to always 0 in return dict
- Delete tests: `test_backlog_gated_subjective_items_suppressed_when_objective_exists`, `test_backlog_gated_subjective_items_absent_when_objective_backlog`, `test_backlog_gated_items_surface_when_queue_empty`
- Update `test_actionable_subjective_items_still_appear_during_backlog` to assert presence without interleave position check

**Phase 6: Add new tests**
- `test_high_impact_subjective_sorts_before_low_impact_mechanical`
- `test_impact_fallback_when_no_scores`
- `test_cluster_meta_item_inherits_max_impact`

### Key Design Decisions

1. **Impact formula for mechanical findings**: `per_point * confidence_weight * headroom`. The `per_point` value from `compute_health_breakdown` already accounts for dimension weight, pool share, and sample factor. Multiplying by `confidence_weight` distinguishes high/medium/low confidence within the same dimension. Multiplying by `headroom` (100 - score) ensures dimensions near 100% have near-zero impact.

2. **Impact formula for subjective dimension items**: `per_point * headroom`. The entire dimension headroom is the impact because fixing the subjective dimension would close the full gap. No confidence factor needed since these are aggregate dimension items.

3. **Review findings (detector="review")**: `get_dimension_for_detector("review")` returns `None` because review findings have `dimension=None` in `DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES`. So they get `estimated_impact = 0.0`. This is correct -- review findings are scored through subjective assessments, not mechanical dimensions. They sort after high-impact items but their tiebreakers (review_weight, count) still work.

4. **Fallback when no dimension_scores**: All items get `estimated_impact = 0.0`. The tiebreakers (confidence, count, id) provide reasonable default ordering identical to the old mechanical-first ordering minus the subjective penalty.

5. **Cluster meta-item impact**: Inherit `max(estimated_impact)` from members, set during `collapse_clusters`. This ensures the cluster appears at the position of its highest-impact member.

6. **Performance**: `compute_health_breakdown` is called once per queue build. The per-item `_compute_item_impact` is O(1) -- just a dict lookup and multiplication. `get_dimension_for_detector` is also O(1). Total overhead: negligible.

7. **Backwards compatibility**: `suppressed_count` key stays in `WorkQueueResult` as always-0 to avoid breaking downstream consumers. Can be removed in a future cleanup.

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` - Add `enrich_with_impact` and `_compute_item_impact` functions; modify `item_sort_key` tier-1 to use `-estimated_impact`; update `item_explain`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` - Call `enrich_with_impact`; delete `_apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail` and its call site; remove backlog_gated suppression
